

################################################################################
# FICHIER: app.dart
################################################################################

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_localizations/flutter_localizations.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

import 'core/theme.dart';
import 'state/session_controller.dart';
import 'ui/auth_screen.dart';
import 'ui/consent_screen.dart';
import 'ui/home_shell.dart';
import 'ui/splash_screen.dart';

class SunuApp extends StatefulWidget {
  const SunuApp({super.key});
  @override
  State<SunuApp> createState() => _SunuAppState();
}

class _SunuAppState extends State<SunuApp> {
  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) => context.read<SessionController>().boot());
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final session = context.watch<SessionController>();
    return MaterialApp(
      title: 'SunuSound',
      debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
      theme: AppTheme.light(),
      darkTheme: AppTheme.dark(),
      themeMode: session.themeMode,
      locale: const Locale('fr'),
      supportedLocales: const [Locale('fr')],
      localizationsDelegates: const [
        GlobalMaterialLocalizations.delegate,
        GlobalWidgetsLocalizations.delegate,
        GlobalCupertinoLocalizations.delegate,
      ],
      home: AnimatedSwitcher(
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 400),
        child: _gate(session),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _gate(SessionController s) {
    switch (s.status) {
      case AuthStatus.booting:
        return const SplashScreen(key: ValueKey('splash'));
      case AuthStatus.signedOut:
        return const AuthScreen(key: ValueKey('auth'));
      case AuthStatus.signedIn:
        if (s.needsConsent) return const ConsentScreen(key: ValueKey('consent'));
        return const HomeShell(key: ValueKey('home'));
    }
  }
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: core/api.dart

import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:flutter_secure_storage/flutter_secure_storage.dart';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import 'package:latlong2/latlong.dart'; // <-- IMPORT AJOUTÉ

import 'config.dart';
import 'models.dart';

class ApiException implements Exception {
  ApiException(this.status, this.code);
  final int? status;
  final String code;
  @override
  String toString() => 'ApiException($status, $code)';
}

class TokenStore {
  final _s = const FlutterSecureStorage(
    aOptions: AndroidOptions(resetOnError: true),
  );
  String? access, refresh;

  Future<void> load() async {
    access = await _s.read(key: 'access_token');
    refresh = await _s.read(key: 'refresh_token');
  }

  Future<void> save(String a, String r) async {
    access = a;
    refresh = r;
    await _s.write(key: 'access_token', value: a);
    await _s.write(key: 'refresh_token', value: r);
  }

  Future<void> clear() async {
    access = null;
    refresh = null;
    await _s.delete(key: 'access_token');
    await _s.delete(key: 'refresh_token');
  }
}

class ApiClient {
  ApiClient(String serverUrl)
      : _dio = Dio(BaseOptions(
          baseUrl: _base(serverUrl),
          connectTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 15),
          receiveTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 30),
          headers: {'Accept': 'application/json'},
        )) {
    _dio.interceptors.add(InterceptorsWrapper(
      onRequest: (o, h) {
        if (!_isAuthPath(o.path) && tokens.access != null) {
          o.headers['Authorization'] = 'Bearer ${tokens.access}';
        }
        h.next(o);
      },
      onError: (e, h) async {
        final opts = e.requestOptions;
        if (e.response?.statusCode == 401 &&
            opts.extra['retried'] != true &&
            !_isAuthPath(opts.path) &&
            tokens.refresh != null) {
          final ok = await _refreshOnce();
          if (ok) {
            opts.extra['retried'] = true;
            opts.headers['Authorization'] = 'Bearer ${tokens.access}';
            try {
              return h.resolve(await _dio.fetch(opts));
            } on DioException catch (e2) {
              return h.next(e2);
            }
          } else {
            onSessionExpired?.call();
          }
        }
        h.next(e);
      },
    ));
  }

  final Dio _dio;
  final TokenStore tokens = TokenStore();
  void Function()? onSessionExpired;
  Future<bool>? _refreshing;

  static String _base(String u) => '${u.trim().replaceAll(RegExp(r'/+$'), '')}${AppConfig.apiPrefix}';
  void setBase(String serverUrl) => _dio.options.baseUrl = _base(serverUrl);

  static bool _isAuthPath(String p) =>
      p.startsWith('/auth/login') || p.startsWith('/auth/register') || p.startsWith('/auth/refresh');

  Future<bool> _refreshOnce() {
    return _refreshing ??= _doRefresh().whenComplete(() => _refreshing = null);
  }

  Future<bool> _doRefresh() async {
    final rt = tokens.refresh;
    if (rt == null) return false;
    try {
      final plain = Dio(BaseOptions(
          baseUrl: _dio.options.baseUrl, connectTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 15)));
      final r = await plain.post('/auth/refresh', data: {'refresh_token': rt});
      await tokens.save(r.data['access_token'] as String, r.data['refresh_token'] as String);
      return true;
    } catch (_) {
      await tokens.clear();
      return false;
    }
  }

  ApiException _wrap(DioException e) {
    final res = e.response;
    if (res == null) return ApiException(null, 'network');
    final data = res.data;
    String code;
    if (data is Map && data['detail'] is String) {
      code = data['detail'] as String;
    } else if (data is Map && data['detail'] is List) {
      code = 'validation_error';
    } else {
      code = 'http_${res.statusCode}';
    }
    return ApiException(res.statusCode, code);
  }

  Future<dynamic> _req(String method, String path,
      {Object? data, Map<String, dynamic>? query}) async {
    try {
      final r = await _dio.request(path,
          data: data, queryParameters: query, options: Options(method: method));
      return r.data;
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw _wrap(e);
    }
  }

  /// Appel générique (utilisé par api_ext.dart).
  Future<dynamic> call(String method, String path, {Object? data, Map<String, dynamic>? query}) =>
      _req(method, path, data: data, query: query);

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ auth
  Future<void> login(String email, String password) async {
    final d = await _req('POST', '/auth/login', data: {'email': email, 'password': password});
    await tokens.save(d['access_token'] as String, d['refresh_token'] as String);
  }

  Future<void> register(String email, String password) =>
      _req('POST', '/auth/register', data: {'email': email, 'password': password});
      
  Future<void> logout() => _req('POST', '/auth/logout', data: {'refresh_token': tokens.refresh});

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ utilisateur
  Future<UserMe> me() async => UserMe.fromJson(await _req('GET', '/users/me') as Map<String, dynamic>);

  Future<UserMe> patchMe({bool? hybrid, bool? paused}) async => UserMe.fromJson(await _req(
      'PATCH', '/users/me',
      data: {
        if (hybrid != null) 'hybrid_collection_enabled': hybrid,
        if (paused != null) 'participation_paused': paused,
      }) as Map<String, dynamic>);

  Future<List<ConsentItem>> consents() async => [
        for (final c in await _req('GET', '/users/me/consents') as List)
          ConsentItem.fromJson(c as Map<String, dynamic>)
      ];

  Future<List<ConsentItem>> setConsent(String type, bool granted) async => [
        for (final c in await _req('PUT', '/users/me/consents',
            data: {'consent_type': type, 'granted': granted}) as List)
          ConsentItem.fromJson(c as Map<String, dynamic>)
      ];

  Future<Contributions> contributions() async => Contributions.fromJson(
      await _req('GET', '/users/me/contributions') as Map<String, dynamic>);

  Future<void> deleteMe(String password) => _req('DELETE', '/users/me', data: {'password': password});

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ appareils
  Future<DeviceOut> registerDevice({
    required String installId,
    required String manufacturer,
    required String model,
    required String androidVersion,
    required int sampleRate,
  }) async =>
      DeviceOut.fromJson(await _req('POST', '/devices/register', data: {
        'install_id': installId,
        'manufacturer': manufacturer,
        'model': model,
        'android_version': androidVersion,
        'microphone': 'internal',
        'sample_rate_hz': sampleRate,
      }) as Map<String, dynamic>);

  Future<List<DeviceOut>> myDevices() async => [
        for (final d in await _req('GET', '/devices/me') as List)
          DeviceOut.fromJson(d as Map<String, dynamic>)
      ];

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ mesures
  Future<List<Map<String, dynamic>>> sendBatch(List<Map<String, dynamic>> items) async {
    final d = await _req('POST', '/measurements/batch', data: {'items': items});
    return (d['results'] as List).cast<Map<String, dynamic>>();
  }

  Future<List<HistoryItem>> history({int limit = 30, int offset = 0}) async => [
        for (final h in await _req('GET', '/measurements/history',
            query: {'limit': limit, 'offset': offset}) as List)
          HistoryItem.fromJson(h as Map<String, dynamic>)
      ];

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ carte
  Future<List<NoiseCell>> mapNoise(MapFilters f, {String? bbox}) async {
    final d = await _req('GET', '/map/noise', query: {
      ...f.timeQuery(),
      'include_indicative': f.includeIndicative,
      'min_confidence': f.minConfidence,
      if (bbox != null) 'bbox': bbox,
    });
    return [
      for (final feat in d['features'] as List) NoiseCell.fromFeature(feat as Map<String, dynamic>)
    ];
  }

  Future<CellDetail> cellDetail(String h3, MapFilters f) async => CellDetail.fromJson(await _req(
      'GET', '/map/cells/$h3',
      query: {...f.timeQuery(), 'include_indicative': f.includeIndicative}) as Map<String, dynamic>);

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ collecte hybride
  Future<ScheduleModel?> getSchedule() async {
    try {
      return ScheduleModel.fromJson(await _req('GET', '/collection/schedule') as Map<String, dynamic>);
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      if (e.code == 'schedule_not_set') return null;
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  Future<ScheduleModel> putSchedule(Map<String, dynamic> body) async => ScheduleModel.fromJson(
      await _req('PUT', '/collection/schedule', data: body) as Map<String, dynamic>);

  Future<ProposalDecision> requestProposal(
          String trigger, double lat, double lon, int battery) async =>
      ProposalDecision.fromJson(await _req('POST', '/collection/proposals/request', data: {
        'trigger': trigger,
        'latitude': lat,
        'longitude': lon,
        'battery_level': battery,
      }) as Map<String, dynamic>);

  Future<List<ProposalOut>> proposals() async => [
        for (final p in await _req('GET', '/collection/proposals') as List)
          ProposalOut.fromJson(p as Map<String, dynamic>)
      ];

  Future<ProposalOut> respondProposal(String id, bool accept) async => ProposalOut.fromJson(
      await _req('POST', '/collection/proposals/$id/${accept ? 'accept' : 'decline'}')
          as Map<String, dynamic>);

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ TomTom (via le backend)
  Future<TileSession> tileSession() async =>
      TileSession.fromJson(await _req('GET', '/tomtom/tile-session') as Map<String, dynamic>);

  Future<List<TrafficIncident>> trafficIncidents(String bbox) async => [
        for (final i in await _req('GET', '/tomtom/traffic/incidents', query: {'bbox': bbox}) as List)
          TrafficIncident.fromJson(i as Map<String, dynamic>)
      ];

  Future<TrafficFlow?> cellTraffic(String h3) async {
    try {
      return TrafficFlow.fromJson(await _req('GET', '/tomtom/cells/$h3/traffic') as Map<String, dynamic>);
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      if (e.code == 'no_traffic_data') return null;
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  Future<List<PlaceResult>> searchPlaces(String q, {double? lat, double? lon}) async => [
        for (final p in await _req('GET', '/tomtom/search', query: {
          'q': q,
          if (lat != null) 'lat': lat,
          if (lon != null) 'lon': lon,
        }) as List)
          PlaceResult.fromJson(p as Map<String, dynamic>)
      ];

  Future<RouteResult> route(double oLat, double oLon, double dLat, double dLon, {String mode = 'car'}) async =>
      RouteResult.fromJson(await _req('GET', '/tomtom/route', query: {
        'o_lat': oLat,
        'o_lon': oLon,
        'd_lat': dLat,
        'd_lon': dLon,
        'mode': mode,
      }) as Map<String, dynamic>);

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ OSRM (gratuit, sans clé)
  Future<OsrmRoute> getOsrmRoute(LatLng from, LatLng to, {String profile = 'driving'}) async {
    final baseUrl = 'https://router.project-osrm.org/route/v1';
    final coords = '${from.longitude},${from.latitude};${to.longitude},${to.latitude}';
    final uri = Uri.parse('$baseUrl/$profile/$coords?geometries=geojson&overview=full');

    final response = await http.get(uri).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 15));
    if (response.statusCode != 200) throw ApiException(response.statusCode, 'osrm_error');

    final data = jsonDecode(response.body) as Map<String, dynamic>;
    if (data['code'] != 'Ok' || (data['routes'] as List).isEmpty) {
      throw ApiException(500, 'osrm_no_route');
    }

    final route = (data['routes'] as List).first as Map<String, dynamic>;
    final geometry = route['geometry'] as Map<String, dynamic>;
    final coordinates = (geometry['coordinates'] as List).cast<List<dynamic>>();

    final points = [
      for (final c in coordinates) LatLng((c[1] as num).toDouble(), (c[0] as num).toDouble())
    ];

    return OsrmRoute(
      points: points,
      distanceM: (route['distance'] as num).toDouble(),
      durationS: (route['duration'] as num).toDouble(),
    );
  }

  Future<({int status, dynamic data, String? etag})> getConditional(String path,
      {Map<String, dynamic>? query, String? etag}) async {
    try {
      final r = await _dio.get(path,
          queryParameters: query,
          options: Options(
            headers: {if (etag != null) 'If-None-Match': etag},
            validateStatus: (s) => s != null && (s < 400 || s == 304),
          ));
      return (status: r.statusCode ?? 200, data: r.data, etag: r.headers.value('etag'));
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw _wrap(e);
    }
  }
}

class OsrmRoute {
  OsrmRoute({required this.points, required this.distanceM, required this.durationS});
  final List<LatLng> points;
  final double distanceM;
  final double durationS;
}

import 'api.dart';
import 'models.dart';
import 'models_ext.dart';
import 'models_field.dart';
import 'models_social.dart';
import 'models_roads.dart';
extension ApiExt on ApiClient {
  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ quota / missions / carte
  Future<QuotaState> quotaMe() async =>
      QuotaState.fromJson(await call('GET', '/quota/me') as Map<String, dynamic>);

  Future<QuotaCheck> quotaCheck(double lat, double lon, {String? proposalId}) async =>
      QuotaCheck.fromJson(await call('POST', '/quota/check', data: {
        'latitude': lat,
        'longitude': lon,
        if (proposalId != null) 'proposal_id': proposalId,
      }) as Map<String, dynamic>);

  Future<List<Mission>> missions() async => [
        for (final m in await call('GET', '/missions') as List) Mission.fromJson(m as Map<String, dynamic>)
      ];

  Future<List<Mission>> missionsSync(double lat, double lon) async => [
        for (final m in await call('POST', '/missions/sync', data: {'latitude': lat, 'longitude': lon}) as List)
          Mission.fromJson(m as Map<String, dynamic>)
      ];

  Future<Mission> missionRespond(String id, bool accept) async => Mission.fromJson(
      await call('POST', '/missions/$id/${accept ? 'accept' : 'decline'}') as Map<String, dynamic>);

  Future<Timelapse> timelapse(MapFilters f, {required String step, String? bbox}) async =>
      Timelapse.fromJson(await call('GET', '/map/timelapse', query: {
        ...f.timeQuery(),
        'step': step,
        'include_indicative': f.includeIndicative,
        if (bbox != null) 'bbox': bbox,
      }) as Map<String, dynamic>);

  Future<FieldGrid> fieldGrid(MapFilters f, String bbox) async =>
      FieldGrid.fromJson(await call('GET', '/map/field', query: {
        ...f.timeQuery(),
        'include_indicative': f.includeIndicative,
        'bbox': bbox,
      }) as Map<String, dynamic>);

  Future<Impact> myImpact({String period = '30d'}) async =>
      Impact.fromJson(await call('GET', '/users/me/impact', query: {'period': period}) as Map<String, dynamic>);

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ comptes
  /// Inscription complète : renvoie la réponse (dont `recovery_codes`, affichés une seule fois).
  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> registerAccount(Map<String, dynamic> payload) async =>
      await call('POST', '/auth/register', data: payload) as Map<String, dynamic>;

  Future<void> resetPassword(String email, String code, String newPassword) => call(
      'POST', '/auth/password/reset',
      data: {'email': email, 'recovery_code': code, 'new_password': newPassword});

  Future<void> changePassword(String current, String next) =>
      call('POST', '/auth/password/change', data: {'current_password': current, 'new_password': next});

  Future<List<String>> regenerateRecoveryCodes(String password) async =>
      (await call('POST', '/auth/recovery-codes', data: {'password': password}) as List).cast<String>();

  Future<void> logoutAll() => call('POST', '/auth/logout-all');

  Future<AccountProfile> accountMe() async =>
      AccountProfile.fromJson(await call('GET', '/account/me') as Map<String, dynamic>);

  Future<AccountProfile> accountUpdate(Map<String, dynamic> body) async =>
      AccountProfile.fromJson(await call('PATCH', '/account/me', data: body) as Map<String, dynamic>);

  Future<Connections> connections() async =>
      Connections.fromJson(await call('GET', '/account/connections') as Map<String, dynamic>);

  Future<List<Invitation>> invitations() async => [
        for (final i in await call('GET', '/account/invitations') as List)
          Invitation.fromJson((i as Map).cast<String, dynamic>())
      ];

  Future<Invitation> createInvitation() async =>
      Invitation.fromJson(await call('POST', '/account/invitations') as Map<String, dynamic>);

  Future<void> revokeInvitation(String id) => call('DELETE', '/account/invitations/$id');

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ classement
  Future<LeaderBoard> leaderboard(String period, {int limit = 30}) async => LeaderBoard.fromJson(
      await call('GET', '/leaderboard', query: {'period': period, 'limit': limit}) as Map<String, dynamic>);

  Future<MyBoard> myBoard() async => MyBoard.fromJson(await call('GET', '/leaderboard/me') as Map<String, dynamic>);

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ analyseur / avis
  Future<AnalystState> analystMe() async =>
      AnalystState.fromJson(await call('GET', '/analyst/me') as Map<String, dynamic>);

  Future<void> analystSet(bool enable) => call('POST', enable ? '/analyst/enable' : '/analyst/disable');

  Future<int> presence(double lat, double lon) async =>
      ((await call('POST', '/presence', data: {'latitude': lat, 'longitude': lon}) as Map)['pending'] as num).toInt();

  Future<List<ReviewRequest>> reviewsPending() async => [
        for (final r in await call('GET', '/reviews/pending') as List)
          ReviewRequest.fromJson((r as Map).cast<String, dynamic>())
      ];

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ routes
  Future<Map<String, RoadState>> roadStates(MapFilters f, String bbox, double zoom, int rank) async {
    final d = await call('GET', '/roads/state', query: {
      ...f.timeQuery(),
      'include_indicative': f.includeIndicative,
      'bbox': bbox,
      'zoom': zoom,
      'max_rank': rank,
    }) as Map<String, dynamic>;
    final segs = (d['segments'] as Map).cast<String, dynamic>();
    return {for (final e in segs.entries) e.key: RoadState.fromJson((e.value as Map).cast<String, dynamic>())};
  }

  Future<RoadDetail> roadSegment(String id, MapFilters f) async => RoadDetail.fromJson(await call(
      'GET', '/roads/segments/$id',
      query: {...f.timeQuery(), 'include_indicative': f.includeIndicative}) as Map<String, dynamic>);

  /// Itinéraire réel (par les routes) vers le point cible d'une mission.
  Future<MissionRoute> missionRoute(String id, double lat, double lon, {String mode = 'car'}) async =>
      MissionRoute.fromJson(await call('GET', '/missions/$id/route',
          query: {'o_lat': lat, 'o_lon': lon, 'mode': mode}) as Map<String, dynamic>);

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ guide
  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> guide() async => await call('GET', '/guide') as Map<String, dynamic>;

  Future<void> reviewVote(String id, String vote, bool strong) =>
      call('POST', '/reviews/$id/vote', data: {'vote': vote, 'strong': strong});

  Future<List<ReviewHistoryItem>> reviewsHistory() async => [
        for (final r in await call('GET', '/reviews/history') as List)
          ReviewHistoryItem.fromJson((r as Map).cast<String, dynamic>())
      ];
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: core/audio_meter.dart
################################################################################

import 'dart:math';
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'config.dart';

class _Biquad {
  _Biquad(this.b0, this.b1, this.b2, this.a1, this.a2);
  final double b0, b1, b2, a1, a2;
  double _z1 = 0, _z2 = 0;

  /// Transformée bilinéaire d'une section analogique (nb2 s² + nb1 s + nb0) / (da2 s² + da1 s + da0).
  factory _Biquad.fromAnalog(
      double nb2, double nb1, double nb0, double da2, double da1, double da0, double fs) {
    final c = 2 * fs, c2 = c * c;
    final n0 = nb2 * c2 + nb1 * c + nb0;
    final n1 = -2 * nb2 * c2 + 2 * nb0;
    final n2 = nb2 * c2 - nb1 * c + nb0;
    final d0 = da2 * c2 + da1 * c + da0;
    final d1 = -2 * da2 * c2 + 2 * da0;
    final d2 = da2 * c2 - da1 * c + da0;
    return _Biquad(n0 / d0, n1 / d0, n2 / d0, d1 / d0, d2 / d0);
  }

  double process(double x) {
    final y = b0 * x + _z1;
    _z1 = b1 * x - a1 * y + _z2;
    _z2 = b2 * x - a2 * y;
    return y;
  }

  double magnitude(double f, double fs) {
    final w = 2 * pi * f / fs;
    final nr = b0 + b1 * cos(w) + b2 * cos(2 * w);
    final ni = -b1 * sin(w) - b2 * sin(2 * w);
    final dr = 1 + a1 * cos(w) + a2 * cos(2 * w);
    final di = -a1 * sin(w) - a2 * sin(2 * w);
    return sqrt(nr * nr + ni * ni) / sqrt(dr * dr + di * di);
  }
}

/// Pondération A (pôles normalisés IEC 61672), gain normalisé à 0 dB à 1 kHz.
class AWeighting {
  AWeighting(double fs) {
    final w1 = 2 * pi * 20.598997;
    final w2 = 2 * pi * 107.65265;
    final w3 = 2 * pi * 737.86223;
    final w4 = 2 * pi * 12194.217;
    _sections = [
      _Biquad.fromAnalog(1, 0, 0, 1, 2 * w1, w1 * w1, fs),
      _Biquad.fromAnalog(1, 0, 0, 1, w2 + w3, w2 * w3, fs),
      _Biquad.fromAnalog(0, 0, 1, 1, 2 * w4, w4 * w4, fs),
    ];
    var m = 1.0;
    for (final s in _sections) {
      m *= s.magnitude(1000, fs);
    }
    _gain = 1 / m;
  }
  late final List<_Biquad> _sections;
  late final double _gain;

  double process(double x) {
    var y = x;
    for (final s in _sections) {
      y = s.process(y);
    }
    return y * _gain;
  }
}

class NoiseStats {
  NoiseStats(this.laeq, this.lmax, this.lmin, this.saturation, this.seconds);
  final double laeq, lmax, lmin, saturation, seconds;
}

/// Accumule du PCM16 mono little-endian ; calcule LAeq (moyenne énergétique), Lmax / Lmin sur blocs de 125 ms.
class NoiseAccumulator {
  NoiseAccumulator(this.sampleRate)
      : _w = AWeighting(sampleRate.toDouble()),
        _blockSize = (sampleRate * 0.125).round();

  final int sampleRate;
  final AWeighting _w;
  final int _blockSize;

  double _sumSq = 0, _blockSum = 0;
  int _n = 0, _blockN = 0, _clipped = 0;
  double _lmax = double.negativeInfinity, _lmin = double.infinity;
  int? _carry;
  double lastBlockDb = 0;
  final List<double> blocks = [];

  double get seconds => _n / sampleRate;
  double get leqDb => _n == 0 ? 0 : toDb(_sumSq / _n);
  double get maxDb => _lmax.isFinite ? _lmax : leqDb;

  static double toDb(double meanSquare) =>
      10 * (log(max(meanSquare, 1e-12)) / ln10) + AppConfig.micReferenceOffsetDb;

  void add(Uint8List data) {
    var start = 0;
    if (_carry != null && data.isNotEmpty) {
      _push(_toSample(_carry!, data[0]));
      _carry = null;
      start = 1;
    }
    final remaining = data.length - start;
    final usable = (remaining ~/ 2) * 2;
    if (usable > 0) {
      final bd = ByteData.sublistView(data, start, start + usable);
      for (var i = 0; i < usable; i += 2) {
        _push(bd.getInt16(i, Endian.little));
      }
    }
    if (remaining % 2 == 1) _carry = data[data.length - 1];
  }

  int _toSample(int lo, int hi) {
    final v = (hi << 8) | lo;
    return v >= 0x8000 ? v - 0x10000 : v;
  }

  void _push(int s) {
    if (s.abs() >= 32700) _clipped++;
    final y = _w.process(s / 32768.0);
    final p = y * y;
    _sumSq += p;
    _n++;
    _blockSum += p;
    _blockN++;
    if (_blockN >= _blockSize) _closeBlock();
  }

  void _closeBlock() {
    final db = toDb(_blockSum / _blockN);
    lastBlockDb = db;
    if (db > _lmax) _lmax = db;
    if (db < _lmin) _lmin = db;
    blocks.add(db);
    _blockSum = 0;
    _blockN = 0;
  }

  NoiseStats finish() {
    if (_blockN > 0) _closeBlock();
    final laeq = leqDb;
    final mx = max(_lmax.isFinite ? _lmax : laeq, laeq);
    final mn = min(_lmin.isFinite ? _lmin : laeq, laeq);
    return NoiseStats(laeq, mx, mn, _n == 0 ? 0 : _clipped / _n, seconds);
  }
}

class AppConfig {
  /// Surchargeable : --dart-define=API_BASE=https://ton-api
  static const String defaultApiBase =
      String.fromEnvironment('API_BASE', defaultValue: 'https://soundmaps.rhmanager.site');
  static const String apiPrefix = '/api/v1';

  /// À remplacer par ton applicationId (User-Agent des tuiles).
  static const String packageName =
      String.fromEnvironment('PACKAGE_NAME', defaultValue: 'com.nagora.sunusound');

  /// Niveau (dB) correspondant à une puissance quadratique moyenne de 1.0 (0 dBFS) après pondération A.
  static const double micReferenceOffsetDb = 90.0;

  // --- CORRECTION ICI ---
  // On met ta clé API directement comme valeur par défaut.
  // Comme ça, même si tu oublies --dart-define, la carte fonctionne.
  static const String cartoApiKey = String.fromEnvironment(
    'CARTO_API_KEY',
    defaultValue: 'cb1_50kr_1_9cd5f225ac0a2c59e3c90a47', 
  );

  static const int sampleRate = 44100;
  static const int defaultSessionSeconds = 15; // SESSION_DURATION_SECONDS côté backend
  static const int mapRefreshSeconds = 30;
  static const int proposalPollMinutes = 5;
  static const int batchMaxItems = 200; // BATCH_MAX_ITEMS côté backend
  static const int maxAttempts = 5;

  // --- UTILISATION DE LA CLE DANS LES URLS ---
  static const String tileLight = 'https://{s}.basemaps.cartocdn.com/light_all/{z}/{x}/{y}.png?key=$cartoApiKey';
  static const String tileDark = 'https://{s}.basemaps.cartocdn.com/dark_all/{z}/{x}/{y}.png?key=$cartoApiKey';

  /// Centre de repli tant que le GPS n'a pas répondu (Dakar).
  static const LatLng fallbackCenter = LatLng(14.6937, -17.4441);
}

import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_map/flutter_map.dart';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import 'package:latlong2/latlong.dart';

/// Trafic : seule couche qui demande une clé (lancer avec --dart-define=TOMTOM_KEY=...).
class LiveConfig {
  static const String trafficKey = String.fromEnvironment('TOMTOM_KEY');
  static bool get trafficEnabled => trafficKey.isNotEmpty;
  static String get trafficTiles =>
      'https://api.tomtom.com/traffic/map/4/tile/flow/relative/{z}/{x}/{y}.png?key=$trafficKey';
}

class WeatherPoint {
  const WeatherPoint({
    required this.pos,
    required this.temp,
    required this.feels,
    required this.humidity,
    required this.precip,
    required this.cloud,
    required this.wind,
    required this.windDir,
    required this.code,
    required this.isDay,
    this.soil,
  });
  final LatLng pos;
  final double temp, feels, precip, wind, windDir;
  final int humidity, cloud, code;
  final bool isDay;
  final double? soil; // température du sol (°C), si disponible

  WeatherPoint withSoil(double? s) => WeatherPoint(
        pos: pos,
        temp: temp,
        feels: feels,
        humidity: humidity,
        precip: precip,
        cloud: cloud,
        wind: wind,
        windDir: windDir,
        code: code,
        isDay: isDay,
        soil: s,
      );
}

class _WeatherCacheEntry {
  _WeatherCacheEntry(this.points, this.at);
  final List<WeatherPoint> points;
  final DateTime at;
}

/// Météo en direct Open-Meteo (sans clé) sur une grille régulière couvrant la zone demandée.
/// Les points sont renvoyés ligne par ligne (sud -> nord), colonne par colonne (ouest -> est).
class LiveWeatherService {
  static const _base = 'https://api.open-meteo.com/v1/forecast';
  static const _ttl = Duration(minutes: 15);
  static final Map<String, _WeatherCacheEntry> _cache = {};

  static double _n(dynamic v) => (v as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0;

  static String _key(LatLngBounds b, int c, int r, bool soil) =>
      '${b.south.toStringAsFixed(2)}_${b.north.toStringAsFixed(2)}_'
      '${b.west.toStringAsFixed(2)}_${b.east.toStringAsFixed(2)}_${c}x${r}_$soil';

  static Future<List<WeatherPoint>> fetchGrid(LatLngBounds b,
      {int cols = 5, int rows = 5, bool soil = false}) async {
    final key = _key(b, cols, rows, soil);
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final cached = _cache[key];
    if (cached != null && now.difference(cached.at) < _ttl) return cached.points;
    try {
      var pts = await _fetch(b, cols, rows);
      if (soil && pts.isNotEmpty) pts = await _attachSoil(pts);
      _cache[key] = _WeatherCacheEntry(pts, now);
      if (_cache.length > 10) _cache.remove(_cache.keys.first);
      return pts;
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Weather API error: $e');
      if (cached != null) return cached.points;
      return [];
    }
  }

  static List<LatLng> _gridPoints(LatLngBounds b, int cols, int rows) {
    final pts = <LatLng>[];
    for (var r = 0; r < rows; r++) {
      for (var c = 0; c < cols; c++) {
        final lat = b.south + (b.north - b.south) * (r + 0.5) / rows;
        final lon = b.west + (b.east - b.west) * (c + 0.5) / cols;
        pts.add(LatLng(lat.clamp(-89.9, 89.9).toDouble(), lon.clamp(-179.9, 179.9).toDouble()));
      }
    }
    return pts;
  }

  static List<List<LatLng>> _chunks(List<LatLng> pts) => [
        for (var i = 0; i < pts.length; i += 30) pts.sublist(i, i + 30 > pts.length ? pts.length : i + 30)
      ];

  static Uri _uri(List<LatLng> chunk, String current) => Uri.parse(_base).replace(queryParameters: {
        'latitude': chunk.map((p) => p.latitude.toStringAsFixed(3)).join(','),
        'longitude': chunk.map((p) => p.longitude.toStringAsFixed(3)).join(','),
        'current': current,
        'wind_speed_unit': 'kmh',
        'timezone': 'auto',
      });

  static Future<List<WeatherPoint>> _fetch(LatLngBounds b, int cols, int rows) async {
    final out = <WeatherPoint>[];
    for (final chunk in _chunks(_gridPoints(b, cols, rows))) {
      final res = await http
          .get(_uri(
              chunk,
              'temperature_2m,apparent_temperature,relative_humidity_2m,precipitation,'
              'weather_code,cloud_cover,wind_speed_10m,wind_direction_10m,is_day'))
          .timeout(const Duration(seconds: 10));
      if (res.statusCode != 200) throw Exception('open-meteo ${res.statusCode}');
      final body = jsonDecode(res.body);
      final list = body is List ? body : [body];
      for (var i = 0; i < list.length && i < chunk.length; i++) {
        final cur = (list[i] as Map)['current'] as Map?;
        if (cur == null) continue;
        out.add(WeatherPoint(
          pos: chunk[i],
          temp: _n(cur['temperature_2m']),
          feels: _n(cur['apparent_temperature']),
          humidity: _n(cur['relative_humidity_2m']).round(),
          precip: _n(cur['precipitation']),
          cloud: _n(cur['cloud_cover']).round(),
          wind: _n(cur['wind_speed_10m']),
          windDir: _n(cur['wind_direction_10m']),
          code: _n(cur['weather_code']).round(),
          isDay: _n(cur['is_day']) == 1,
        ));
      }
    }
    return out;
  }

  /// Requête séparée et facultative : si elle échoue, le reste de la météo n'est pas affecté.
  static Future<List<WeatherPoint>> _attachSoil(List<WeatherPoint> pts) async {
    try {
      final soil = <double?>[];
      for (var i = 0; i < pts.length; i += 30) {
        final chunk = pts.sublist(i, i + 30 > pts.length ? pts.length : i + 30);
        final res = await http
            .get(_uri([for (final p in chunk) p.pos], 'soil_temperature_0cm'))
            .timeout(const Duration(seconds: 10));
        if (res.statusCode != 200) return pts;
        final body = jsonDecode(res.body);
        final list = body is List ? body : [body];
        for (var k = 0; k < chunk.length; k++) {
          final cur = k < list.length ? (list[k] as Map)['current'] as Map? : null;
          soil.add((cur?['soil_temperature_0cm'] as num?)?.toDouble());
        }
      }
      if (soil.length != pts.length) return pts;
      return [for (var i = 0; i < pts.length; i++) pts[i].withSoil(soil[i])];
    } catch (_) {
      return pts;
    }
  }
}

Color tempColor(double t) {
  const stops = [
    (10.0, Color(0xFF3B82F6)),
    (20.0, Color(0xFF22C55E)),
    (28.0, Color(0xFFF59E0B)),
    (34.0, Color(0xFFEF4444)),
    (42.0, Color(0xFF9333EA)),
  ];
  if (t <= stops.first.$1) return stops.first.$2;
  for (var i = 1; i < stops.length; i++) {
    if (t <= stops[i].$1) {
      final a = stops[i - 1], b = stops[i];
      return Color.lerp(a.$2, b.$2, (t - a.$1) / (b.$1 - a.$1))!;
    }
  }
  return stops.last.$2;
}

Color windColor(double kmh) =>
    Color.lerp(const Color(0xFF38BDF8), const Color(0xFF9333EA), (kmh / 60).clamp(0.0, 1.0))!;

IconData weatherIcon(int code, bool day) {
  if (code == 0) return day ? Icons.wb_sunny_rounded : Icons.nightlight_round;
  if (code <= 3) return day ? Icons.wb_cloudy_rounded : Icons.cloud_rounded;
  if (code == 45 || code == 48) return Icons.blur_on_rounded;
  if (code >= 51 && code <= 57) return Icons.grain_rounded;
  if ((code >= 61 && code <= 67) || (code >= 80 && code <= 82)) return Icons.water_drop_rounded;
  if ((code >= 71 && code <= 77) || code == 85 || code == 86) return Icons.ac_unit_rounded;
  if (code >= 95) return Icons.thunderstorm_rounded;
  return Icons.cloud_rounded;
}

String weatherLabel(int code) {
  if (code == 0) return 'Dégagé';
  if (code <= 3) return 'Nuageux';
  if (code == 45 || code == 48) return 'Brouillard';
  if (code >= 51 && code <= 57) return 'Bruine';
  if ((code >= 61 && code <= 67) || (code >= 80 && code <= 82)) return 'Pluie';
  if ((code >= 71 && code <= 77) || code == 85 || code == 86) return 'Neige';
  if (code >= 95) return 'Orage';
  return 'Couvert';
}

String compass(double deg) {
  const d = ['N', 'NE', 'E', 'SE', 'S', 'SO', 'O', 'NO'];
  return d[((deg % 360) / 45).round() % 8];
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: core/local_store.dart
################################################################################

import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

import 'api.dart';
import 'config.dart';

class PendingMeasurement {
  PendingMeasurement(this.payload, [this.attempts = 0]);
  final Map<String, dynamic> payload;
  int attempts;
  String get clientId => payload['client_measurement_id'] as String;
  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {'payload': payload, 'attempts': attempts};
  factory PendingMeasurement.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> j) =>
      PendingMeasurement((j['payload'] as Map).cast<String, dynamic>(), j['attempts'] as int);
}

class FlushResult {
  final Map<String, String> accepted = {}; // client_measurement_id -> id serveur
  int dropped = 0;
  bool networkError = false;
  String? lastError;
}

class MyPoint {
  MyPoint(this.lat, this.lon, this.laeq, this.at);
  final double lat, lon, laeq;
  final DateTime at;
  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() =>
      {'lat': lat, 'lon': lon, 'laeq': laeq, 'at': at.toUtc().toIso8601String()};
  factory MyPoint.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> j) => MyPoint(
      (j['lat'] as num).toDouble(),
      (j['lon'] as num).toDouble(),
      (j['laeq'] as num).toDouble(),
      DateTime.parse(j['at'] as String).toLocal());
}

/// File d'envoi hors-ligne + dernières mesures personnelles (affichées sur la carte).
class LocalStore {
  LocalStore(this.prefs, this.userId);
  final SharedPreferences prefs;
  final String userId;

  String get _pendingKey => 'pending_$userId';
  String get _pointsKey => 'points_$userId';

  // Erreurs que l'utilisateur peut corriger : on ne compte pas de tentative.
  static const _userFixable = {'consent_required', 'participation_paused'};

  List<PendingMeasurement> loadPending() {
    final raw = prefs.getString(_pendingKey);
    if (raw == null) return [];
    return [
      for (final e in jsonDecode(raw) as List)
        PendingMeasurement.fromJson((e as Map).cast<String, dynamic>())
    ];
  }

  int get pendingCount => loadPending().length;

  Future<void> _savePending(List<PendingMeasurement> l) =>
      prefs.setString(_pendingKey, jsonEncode([for (final p in l) p.toJson()]));

  Future<void> addPending(Map<String, dynamic> payload) async {
    final l = loadPending()..add(PendingMeasurement(payload));
    await _savePending(l);
  }

  Future<FlushResult> flush(ApiClient api) async {
    final r = FlushResult();
    final all = loadPending();
    if (all.isEmpty) return r;
    final failed = <PendingMeasurement>[];
    for (var i = 0; i < all.length; i += AppConfig.batchMaxItems) {
      final chunk = all.skip(i).take(AppConfig.batchMaxItems).toList();
      List<Map<String, dynamic>> results;
      try {
        results = await api.sendBatch([for (final p in chunk) p.payload]);
      } on ApiException catch (e) {
        r.lastError = e.code;
        if (e.code == 'network') {
          r.networkError = true;
          failed.addAll(all.skip(i));
          break;
        }
        for (final p in chunk) {
          p.attempts++;
          if (p.attempts >= AppConfig.maxAttempts) {
            r.dropped++;
          } else {
            failed.add(p);
          }
        }
        continue;
      }
      final byId = {for (final x in results) x['client_measurement_id'] as String: x};
      for (final p in chunk) {
        final res = byId[p.clientId];
        if (res == null) {
          failed.add(p);
          continue;
        }
        final status = res['result'] as String;
        if (status == 'accepted' || status == 'duplicate') {
          r.accepted[p.clientId] = res['id'] as String;
        } else {
          final err = (res['error'] as String?) ?? 'error';
          r.lastError = err;
          if (!_userFixable.contains(err)) p.attempts++;
          if (p.attempts >= AppConfig.maxAttempts) {
            r.dropped++;
          } else {
            failed.add(p);
          }
        }
      }
    }
    await _savePending(failed);
    return r;
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ mes points
  List<MyPoint> loadPoints() {
    final raw = prefs.getString(_pointsKey);
    if (raw == null) return [];
    return [
      for (final e in jsonDecode(raw) as List) MyPoint.fromJson((e as Map).cast<String, dynamic>())
    ];
  }

  Future<void> addPoint(MyPoint p) async {
    final l = loadPoints()..add(p);
    final trimmed = l.length > 100 ? l.sublist(l.length - 100) : l;
    await prefs.setString(_pointsKey, jsonEncode([for (final x in trimmed) x.toJson()]));
  }
}

import 'package:intl/intl.dart';

double _d(dynamic v) => (v as num).toDouble();
DateTime? _dt(dynamic v) => v == null ? null : DateTime.parse(v as String).toLocal();

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ utilisateur
class UserMe {
  UserMe({
    required this.id,
    required this.pseudonym,
    required this.role,
    required this.hybridCollectionEnabled,
    required this.participationPaused,
    required this.createdAt,
  });
  final String id, pseudonym, role;
  final bool hybridCollectionEnabled, participationPaused;
  final DateTime createdAt;

  factory UserMe.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> j) => UserMe(
        id: j['id'] as String,
        pseudonym: j['pseudonym'] as String,
        role: j['role'] as String,
        hybridCollectionEnabled: j['hybrid_collection_enabled'] as bool,
        participationPaused: j['participation_paused'] as bool,
        createdAt: _dt(j['created_at'])!,
      );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
        'id': id,
        'pseudonym': pseudonym,
        'role': role,
        'hybrid_collection_enabled': hybridCollectionEnabled,
        'participation_paused': participationPaused,
        'created_at': createdAt.toUtc().toIso8601String(),
      };

  String get roleLabel => switch (role) {
        'tenant_admin' => 'Administrateur',
        'analyst' => 'Analyste',
        _ => 'Contributeur',
      };
}

class Contributions {
  Contributions({
    required this.total,
    required this.byStatus,
    required this.cellsCovered,
    required this.lastMeasurementAt,
  });
  final int total, cellsCovered;
  final Map<String, int> byStatus;
  final DateTime? lastMeasurementAt;
  factory Contributions.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> j) => Contributions(
        total: j['total_measurements'] as int,
        byStatus: (j['by_status'] as Map).map((k, v) => MapEntry(k as String, v as int)),
        cellsCovered: j['cells_covered'] as int,
        lastMeasurementAt: _dt(j['last_measurement_at']),
      );

  double get validationRate => total == 0 ? 0 : count('validated') / total;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ carte
class NoiseCell {
  NoiseCell({
    required this.h3,
    required this.polygon,
    required this.laeq,
    required this.lmax,
    required this.count,
    required this.devices,
    required this.confidence,
    required this.calibratedShare,
    required this.last,
  });
  final String h3;
  final List<LatLng> polygon;
  final double laeq, lmax, confidence, calibratedShare;
  final int count, devices;
  final DateTime? last;

  factory NoiseCell.fromFeature(Map<String, dynamic> f) {
    final p = f['properties'] as Map<String, dynamic>;
    final ring = ((f['geometry'] as Map)['coordinates'] as List).first as List;
    return NoiseCell(
      h3: p['h3_index'] as String,
      polygon: [for (final c in ring) LatLng(_d((c as List)[1]), _d(c[0]))],
      laeq: _d(p['laeq_db']),
      lmax: _d(p['lmax_db']),
      count: p['measurement_count'] as int,
      devices: p['device_count'] as int,
      confidence: _d(p['confidence']),
      calibratedShare: _d(p['calibrated_share']),
      last: _dt(p['last_measurement_at']),
    );
  }

  bool get isHot => last != null && DateTime.now().difference(last!).inMinutes < 60;
}

class MapFilters {
  const MapFilters({
    this.period = '7d',
    this.from,
    this.to,
    this.dayPart = 'all',
    this.includeIndicative = false,
    this.minConfidence = 0,
  });
  final String period; // today | 7d | 30d | custom
  final DateTime? from, to;
  final String dayPart; // all | morning | afternoon | evening | night
  final bool includeIndicative;
  final double minConfidence;

  MapFilters copyWith({
    String? period,
    DateTime? from,
    DateTime? to,
    String? dayPart,
    bool? includeIndicative,
    double? minConfidence,
  }) =>
      MapFilters(
        period: period ?? this.period,
        from: from ?? this.from,
        to: to ?? this.to,
        dayPart: dayPart ?? this.dayPart,
        includeIndicative: includeIndicative ?? this.includeIndicative,
        minConfidence: minConfidence ?? this.minConfidence,
      );

  Map<String, dynamic> timeQuery() {
    final f = DateFormat('yyyy-MM-dd');
    return {
      'period': period,
      'day_part': dayPart,
      if (period == 'custom' && from != null && to != null) ...{
        'date_from': f.format(from!),
        'date_to': f.format(to!),
      },
    };
  }

  String get periodLabel => switch (period) {
        'today' => "Aujourd'hui",
        '30d' => '30 jours',
        'custom' => (from != null && to != null)
            ? '${DateFormat('d MMM', 'fr_FR').format(from!)} → ${DateFormat('d MMM', 'fr_FR').format(to!)}'
            : 'Période',
        _ => '7 jours',
      };
}

class CellDetail {
  CellDetail({
    required this.h3,
    required this.center,
    required this.laeq,
    required this.lmax,
    required this.count,
    required this.devices,
    required this.calibratedShare,
    required this.confidence,
    required this.last,
    required this.byDayPart,
    required this.daily,
    required this.context,
  });
  final String h3;
  final LatLng center;
  final double laeq, lmax, calibratedShare, confidence;
  final int count, devices;
  final DateTime? last;
  final List<({String part, double laeq, int count})> byDayPart;
  final List<({DateTime date, double laeq, double lmax, int count})> daily;
  final Map<String, int> context;

  factory CellDetail.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> j) {
    final s = j['summary'] as Map<String, dynamic>;
    final c = j['center'] as Map<String, dynamic>;
    return CellDetail(
      h3: j['h3_index'] as String,
      center: LatLng(_d(c['lat']), _d(c['lon'])),
      laeq: _d(s['laeq_db']),
      lmax: _d(s['lmax_db']),
      count: s['measurement_count'] as int,
      devices: s['device_count'] as int,
      calibratedShare: _d(s['calibrated_share']),
      confidence: _d(s['confidence']),
      last: _dt(s['last_measurement_at']),
      byDayPart: [
        for (final p in j['by_day_part'] as List)
          (part: p['day_part'] as String, laeq: _d(p['laeq_db']), count: p['measurement_count'] as int)
      ],
      daily: [
        for (final d in j['daily'] as List)
          (
            date: DateTime.parse(d['date'] as String),
            laeq: _d(d['laeq_db']),
            lmax: _d(d['lmax_db']),
            count: d['measurement_count'] as int
          )
      ],
      context: (j['context_breakdown'] as Map).map((k, v) => MapEntry(k as String, v as int)),
    );
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ historique animé
class Timelapse {
  Timelapse._(this.h3s, this.polygons, this.labels, this.levels, this.alphas, this.minDb, this.maxDb);

  final List<String> h3s;
  final List<List<LatLng>> polygons;
  final List<String> labels;
  final List<Float32List> levels; // NaN = inconnu
  final List<Float32List> alphas; // 1 = mesuré ; < 1 = valeur persistante qui s'estompe
  final double? minDb, maxDb;

  int get frameCount => labels.length;

  factory Timelapse.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> j) {
    final cells = j['cells'] as List;
    final n = cells.length;
    final h3s = <String>[];
    final polys = <List<LatLng>>[];
    for (final c in cells) {
      h3s.add(c['h3'] as String);
      polys.add([
        for (final p in c['polygon'] as List) LatLng(_n((p as List)[1]), _n(p[0])),
      ]);
    }
    final frames = j['frames'] as List;
    final labels = <String>[];
    final levels = <Float32List>[];
    final alphas = <Float32List>[];
    for (var f = 0; f < frames.length; f++) {
      final fr = frames[f] as Map<String, dynamic>;
      final part = fr['part'] as String?;
      final d = DateTime.parse(fr['t'] as String);
      final day = '${d.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}/${d.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
      labels.add(part == null ? day : '$day · ${_part(part)}');
      final lv = Float32List(n)..fillRange(0, n, double.nan);
      final al = Float32List(n);
      for (final c in fr['cells'] as List) {
        final i = (c[0] as num).toInt();
        lv[i] = _n(c[1]);
        al[i] = 1;
      }
      if (f > 0) {
        for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
          if (al[i] == 0 && alphas[f - 1][i] > 0) {
            final a = alphas[f - 1][i] * 0.7;
            if (a >= 0.25) {
              lv[i] = levels[f - 1][i];
              al[i] = a;
            }
          }
        }
      }
      levels.add(lv);
      alphas.add(al);
    }
    return Timelapse._(h3s, polys, labels, levels, alphas, (j['min_db'] as num?)?.toDouble(),
        (j['max_db'] as num?)?.toDouble());
  }

  static String _part(String p) => switch (p) {
        'morning' => 'Matin',
        'afternoon' => 'Après-midi',
        'evening' => 'Soirée',
        _ => 'Nuit',
      };

  /// Cellules à l'instant `pos` (indice d'image fractionnaire), interpolées, triées du plus calme au plus bruyant.
  List<NoiseCell> cellsAt(double pos) {
    if (frameCount == 0) return const [];
    final f0 = pos.floor().clamp(0, frameCount - 1);
    final f1 = min(f0 + 1, frameCount - 1);
    final t = (pos - f0).clamp(0.0, 1.0).toDouble();
    final out = <NoiseCell>[];
    for (var i = 0; i < h3s.length; i++) {
      final a0 = alphas[f0][i].toDouble(), a1 = alphas[f1][i].toDouble();
      if (a0 == 0 && a1 == 0) continue;
      final v0 = levels[f0][i].toDouble(), v1 = levels[f1][i].toDouble();
      final level = (a0 > 0 && a1 > 0) ? v0 + (v1 - v0) * t : (a0 > 0 ? v0 : v1);
      final alpha = a0 + (a1 - a0) * t;
      out.add(NoiseCell(
        h3: h3s[i],
        polygon: polygons[i],
        laeq: level,
        lmax: level,
        count: 0,
        devices: 0,
        confidence: alpha,
        calibratedShare: 0,
        last: null,
      ));
    }
    out.sort((a, b) => a.laeq.compareTo(b.laeq));
    return out;
  }
}

# FICHIER: core/models_field.dart
################################################################################

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:math';
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'package:latlong2/latlong.dart';

double _n(dynamic v) => (v as num).toDouble();
DateTime _t(dynamic v) => DateTime.parse(v as String).toLocal();

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ champ de bruit (nuage) du serveur
class FieldWind {
  FieldWind({required this.u, required this.v, required this.speedKmh, required this.dirFrom});
  final double u, v, speedKmh, dirFrom; // u = vers l'est, v = vers le nord (m/s)
  factory FieldWind.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> j) => FieldWind(
      u: _n(j['u']), v: _n(j['v']), speedKmh: _n(j['speed_kmh']), dirFrom: _n(j['dir_from']));
}

class FieldGrid {
  FieldGrid({
    required this.west,
    required this.south,
    required this.east,
    required this.north,
    required this.cols,
    required this.rows,
    required this.level,
    required this.support,
    required this.dbOffset,
    required this.dbStep,
    required this.sources,
    this.wind,
    this.minDb,
    this.maxDb,
  });
  final double west, south, east, north, dbOffset, dbStep;
  final int cols, rows, sources;
  final Uint8List level, support; // lignes du sud au nord, colonnes de l'ouest à l'est
  final FieldWind? wind;
  final double? minDb, maxDb;

  factory FieldGrid.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> j) {
    final b = j['bounds'] as List;
    return FieldGrid(
      west: _n(b[0]),
      south: _n(b[1]),
      east: _n(b[2]),
      north: _n(b[3]),
      cols: (j['cols'] as num).toInt(),
      rows: (j['rows'] as num).toInt(),
      level: base64Decode(j['level_b64'] as String),
      support: base64Decode(j['support_b64'] as String),
      dbOffset: _n(j['db_offset']),
      dbStep: _n(j['db_step']),
      sources: (j['sources'] as num).toInt(),
      wind: j['wind'] == null ? null : FieldWind.fromJson(j['wind'] as Map<String, dynamic>),
      minDb: (j['min_db'] as num?)?.toDouble(),
      maxDb: (j['max_db'] as num?)?.toDouble(),
    );
  }

  /// (niveau dB(A), support 0..1). Niveau NaN et support 0 s'il n'y a aucune donnée.
  (double, double) sample(double lat, double lon) {
    if (lat < south || lat > north || lon < west || lon > east) return (double.nan, 0.0);
    final fx = ((lon - west) / (east - west) * cols - 0.5).clamp(0.0, cols - 1.0).toDouble();
    final fy = ((lat - south) / (north - south) * rows - 0.5).clamp(0.0, rows - 1.0).toDouble();
    final x0 = fx.floor(), y0 = fy.floor();
    final x1 = min(x0 + 1, cols - 1), y1 = min(y0 + 1, rows - 1);
    final tx = fx - x0, ty = fy - y0;
    final i00 = y0 * cols + x0, i10 = y0 * cols + x1, i01 = y1 * cols + x0, i11 = y1 * cols + x1;
    final w00 = (1 - tx) * (1 - ty), w10 = tx * (1 - ty), w01 = (1 - tx) * ty, w11 = tx * ty;
    final s00 = level[i00] == 0 ? 0.0 : support[i00] / 255.0;
    final s10 = level[i10] == 0 ? 0.0 : support[i10] / 255.0;
    final s01 = level[i01] == 0 ? 0.0 : support[i01] / 255.0;
    final s11 = level[i11] == 0 ? 0.0 : support[i11] / 255.0;
    final ws = w00 * s00 + w10 * s10 + w01 * s01 + w11 * s11;
    if (ws <= 0) return (double.nan, 0.0);
    final wl = w00 * s00 * (dbOffset + level[i00] * dbStep) +
        w10 * s10 * (dbOffset + level[i10] * dbStep) +
        w01 * s01 * (dbOffset + level[i01] * dbStep) +
        w11 * s11 * (dbOffset + level[i11] * dbStep);
    return (wl / ws, ws.clamp(0.0, 1.0).toDouble());
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ impact de mes mesures sur la carte générale
class ImpactPoint {
  ImpactPoint({
    required this.id,
    required this.measuredAt,
    required this.lat,
    required this.lon,
    required this.h3,
    required this.laeq,
    required this.status,
    this.score,
  });
  final String id, h3, status;
  final DateTime measuredAt;
  final double lat, lon, laeq;
  final double? score;
  factory ImpactPoint.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> j) => ImpactPoint(
        id: j['id'] as String,
        measuredAt: _t(j['measured_at']),
        lat: _n(j['lat']),
        lon: _n(j['lon']),
        h3: j['h3_index'] as String,
        laeq: _n(j['laeq_db']),
        status: j['quality_status'] as String,
        score: (j['quality_score'] as num?)?.toDouble(),
      );
}

class ImpactCell {
  ImpactCell({
    required this.h3,
    required this.polygon,
    required this.center,
    required this.myCount,
    required this.myValidated,
    required this.myLaeq,
    required this.visible,
    this.mapLaeq,
    this.mapCount,
    this.mapDevices,
    this.share,
    this.delta,
  });
  final String h3;
  final List<LatLng> polygon;
  final LatLng center;
  final int myCount, myValidated;
  final double myLaeq;
  final bool visible;
  final double? mapLaeq, share, delta;
  final int? mapCount, mapDevices;
  factory ImpactCell.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> j) {
    final c = j['center'] as Map<String, dynamic>;
    return ImpactCell(
      h3: j['h3_index'] as String,
      polygon: [for (final p in j['polygon'] as List) LatLng(_n((p as List)[1]), _n(p[0]))],
      center: LatLng(_n(c['lat']), _n(c['lon'])),
      myCount: (j['my_count'] as num).toInt(),
      myValidated: (j['my_validated'] as num).toInt(),
      myLaeq: _n(j['my_laeq_db']),
      visible: j['visible'] as bool,
      mapLaeq: (j['map_laeq_db'] as num?)?.toDouble(),
      mapCount: (j['map_count'] as num?)?.toInt(),
      mapDevices: (j['map_devices'] as num?)?.toInt(),
      share: (j['my_share'] as num?)?.toDouble(),
      delta: (j['delta_db'] as num?)?.toDouble(),
    );
  }
}

class Impact {
  Impact({
    required this.total,
    required this.byStatus,
    required this.visibleCells,
    required this.onMap,
    required this.minDevices,
    required this.points,
    required this.cells,
  });
  final int total, visibleCells, onMap, minDevices;
  final Map<String, int> byStatus;
  final List<ImpactPoint> points;
  final List<ImpactCell> cells;

  int count(String s) => byStatus[s] ?? 0;

  factory Impact.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> j) {
    final s = j['summary'] as Map<String, dynamic>;
    return Impact(
      total: (s['total'] as num).toInt(),
      byStatus: (s['by_status'] as Map).map((k, v) => MapEntry(k as String, (v as num).toInt())),
      visibleCells: (s['visible_cells'] as num).toInt(),
      onMap: (s['on_map'] as num).toInt(),
      minDevices: (s['min_devices'] as num).toInt(),
      points: [for (final p in j['points'] as List) ImpactPoint.fromJson(p as Map<String, dynamic>)],
      cells: [for (final c in j['cells'] as List) ImpactCell.fromJson(c as Map<String, dynamic>)],
    );
  }
}

# FICHIER: core/models_roads.dart

import 'dart:math';

import 'package:latlong2/latlong.dart';

import 'models.dart';
import 'models_ext.dart';

double _n(dynamic v) => (v as num).toDouble();
DateTime? _t(dynamic v) => v == null ? null : DateTime.parse(v as String).toLocal();

String roadClassLabel(String? h) => switch (h) {
      'motorway' || 'motorway_link' => 'Autoroute',
      'trunk' || 'trunk_link' => 'Route nationale',
      'primary' || 'primary_link' => 'Route principale',
      'secondary' || 'secondary_link' => 'Route secondaire',
      'tertiary' || 'tertiary_link' => 'Route de quartier',
      'unclassified' => 'Route locale',
      'residential' => 'Rue résidentielle',
      'living_street' => 'Rue apaisée',
      _ => 'Route',
    };

class RoadSeg {
  RoadSeg({
    required this.id,
    required this.highway,
    required this.rank,
    required this.points,
    this.name,
    this.ref,
    this.lengthM = 0,
    this.oneway = false,
    this.maxspeed,
  }) {
    var a = 90.0, b = -90.0, c = 180.0, d = -180.0;
    for (final p in points) {
      a = min(a, p.latitude);
      b = max(b, p.latitude);
      c = min(c, p.longitude);
      d = max(d, p.longitude);
    }
    minLat = a;
    maxLat = b;
    minLon = c;
    maxLon = d;
  }
  final String id, highway;
  final int rank;
  final List<LatLng> points;
  final String? name, ref, maxspeed;
  final double lengthM;
  final bool oneway;
  late final double minLat, maxLat, minLon, maxLon;

  String get title => name ?? ref ?? roadClassLabel(highway);

  factory RoadSeg.fromFeature(Map<String, dynamic> f) {
    final p = f['properties'] as Map<String, dynamic>;
    final coords = (f['geometry'] as Map)['coordinates'] as List;
    return RoadSeg(
      id: p['id'] as String,
      highway: (p['highway'] as String?) ?? 'residential',
      rank: (p['rank'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 6,
      name: p['name'] as String?,
      ref: p['ref'] as String?,
      lengthM: (p['length_m'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0,
      oneway: (p['oneway'] as bool?) ?? false,
      maxspeed: p['maxspeed'] as String?,
      points: [for (final c in coords) LatLng(_n((c as List)[1]), _n(c[0]))],
    );
  }
}

class RoadState {
  RoadState({
    required this.hasData,
    required this.laeq,
    required this.count,
    required this.devices,
    required this.confidence,
    required this.intensity,
    this.lmax,
    this.last,
  });
  final bool hasData;
  final double laeq, confidence, intensity;
  final double? lmax;
  final int count, devices;
  final DateTime? last;
  factory RoadState.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> j) => RoadState(
        hasData: (j['has_data'] as bool?) ?? false,
        laeq: _n(j['laeq_db']),
        lmax: (j['lmax_db'] as num?)?.toDouble(),
        count: (j['measurement_count'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 0,
        devices: (j['device_count'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 0,
        confidence: _n(j['confidence'] ?? 0),
        intensity: _n(j['intensity'] ?? 0),
        last: _t(j['last_measurement_at']),
      );
}

class RoadDetail {
  RoadDetail({
    required this.id,
    required this.title,
    required this.highway,
    required this.state,
    required this.byPart,
    required this.connected,
    required this.lengthM,
    this.maxspeed,
    this.lanes,
    this.oneway = false,
  });
  final String id, title, highway;
  final RoadState state;
  final List<({String part, double laeq, int count})> byPart;
  final List<({String id, String name, String highway})> connected;
  final double lengthM;
  final String? maxspeed;
  final int? lanes;
  final bool oneway;
  factory RoadDetail.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> j) => RoadDetail(
        id: j['id'] as String,
        title: (j['name'] ?? j['ref'] ?? roadClassLabel(j['highway'] as String?)) as String,
        highway: j['highway'] as String,
        lengthM: _n(j['length_m']),
        maxspeed: j['maxspeed'] as String?,
        lanes: (j['lanes'] as num?)?.toInt(),
        oneway: (j['oneway'] as bool?) ?? false,
        state: RoadState.fromJson((j['state'] as Map).cast<String, dynamic>()),
        byPart: [
          for (final p in j['by_day_part'] as List)
            (part: p['day_part'] as String, laeq: _n(p['laeq_db']), count: (p['measurement_count'] as num).toInt())
        ],
        connected: [
          for (final c in j['connected'] as List)
            (id: c['id'] as String, name: (c['name'] as String?) ?? roadClassLabel(c['highway'] as String?), highway: c['highway'] as String)
        ],
      );
}

class MissionTarget {
  MissionTarget({required this.lat, required this.lon, required this.radiusM, this.dayPart, this.windowEnd, this.roadName, this.roadClass});
  final double lat, lon, radiusM;
  final String? dayPart, roadName, roadClass;
  final DateTime? windowEnd;
  LatLng get point => LatLng(lat, lon);
  factory MissionTarget.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> j) => MissionTarget(
        lat: _n(j['lat']),
        lon: _n(j['lon']),
        radiusM: _n(j['radius_m']),
        dayPart: j['day_part'] as String?,
        windowEnd: _t(j['window_end_at']),
        roadName: j['road_name'] as String?,
        roadClass: j['road_class'] as String?,
      );
}

/// Itinéraire vers le point cible d'une mission (route réelle, calculée côté serveur).
class MissionRoute {
  MissionRoute({required this.missionId, required this.target, required this.points, required this.lengthM, required this.timeS});
  final String missionId;
  final MissionTarget target;
  final List<LatLng> points;
  final int lengthM, timeS;
  factory MissionRoute.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> j) {
    final r = RouteResult.fromJson((j['route'] as Map).cast<String, dynamic>());
    return MissionRoute(
        missionId: j['mission_id'] as String,
        target: MissionTarget.fromJson((j['target'] as Map).cast<String, dynamic>()),
        points: r.points,
        lengthM: r.lengthM,
        timeS: r.timeS);
  }
}

/// Résultat renvoyé par l'écran des missions à la carte.
class MissionFocus {
  MissionFocus(this.mission, {this.route = false});
  final Mission mission;
  final bool route;
}

import 'package:flutter_local_notifications/flutter_local_notifications.dart';

class Notifier {
  final FlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin _p = FlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin();
  static const _idRecording = 1;
  int _seq = 100;

  Future<void> init() async {
    const android = AndroidInitializationSettings('ic_stat_sunusound');

    // ⚠️ v20+ : le paramètre est maintenant nommé `settings:`
    await _p.initialize(settings: const InitializationSettings(android: android));

    final impl =
        _p.resolvePlatformSpecificImplementation<AndroidFlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin>();

    await impl?.createNotificationChannel(const AndroidNotificationChannel(
        'recording', 'Mesure en cours',
        description: "Progression d'une mesure sonore", importance: Importance.low));

    await impl?.createNotificationChannel(const AndroidNotificationChannel(
        'results', 'Résultats des mesures',
        description: "Envoi et validation de vos mesures", importance: Importance.defaultImportance));

    await impl?.createNotificationChannel(const AndroidNotificationChannel(
        'proposals', 'Propositions de mesure',
        description: 'Zones où une mesure vous est proposée', importance: Importance.high));
  }

  Future<void> requestPermission() async {
    final impl =
        _p.resolvePlatformSpecificImplementation<AndroidFlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin>();
    await impl?.requestNotificationsPermission();
  }

  // ⚠️ v20+ : show(id:, title:, body:, notificationDetails:)
  Future<void> recordingProgress(int elapsed, int total, double db) => _p.show(
        id: _idRecording,
        title: 'Mesure en cours',
        body: '${db.toStringAsFixed(0)} dB(A) · $elapsed/$total s',
        notificationDetails: NotificationDetails(
          android: AndroidNotificationDetails(
            'recording',
            'Mesure en cours',
            channelDescription: "Progression d'une mesure sonore",
            importance: Importance.low,
            priority: Priority.low,
            ongoing: true,
            onlyAlertOnce: true,
            showProgress: true,
            maxProgress: total,
            progress: elapsed.clamp(0, total),
            autoCancel: false,
            icon: 'ic_stat_sunusound',
          ),
        ),
      );

  // ⚠️ v20+ : cancel(id: ...)
  Future<void> cancelProgress() => _p.cancel(id: _idRecording);

  Future<void> result(String title, String body) => _p.show(
        id: _seq++,
        title: title,
        body: body,
        notificationDetails: const NotificationDetails(
          android: AndroidNotificationDetails(
            'results',
            'Résultats des mesures',
            channelDescription: 'Envoi et validation de vos mesures',
            importance: Importance.defaultImportance,
            priority: Priority.defaultPriority,
            icon: 'ic_stat_sunusound',
          ),
        ),
      );

  Future<void> proposal(String title, String body) => _p.show(
        id: _seq++,
        title: title,
        body: body,
        notificationDetails: const NotificationDetails(
          android: AndroidNotificationDetails(
            'proposals',
            'Propositions de mesure',
            channelDescription: 'Zones où une mesure vous est proposée',
            importance: Importance.high,
            priority: Priority.high,
            icon: 'ic_stat_sunusound',
          ),
        ),
      );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: core/theme.dart
################################################################################

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class AppTheme {
  static const Color seed = Color(0xFF0E9F9A);

  static ThemeData light() => _build(Brightness.light);
  static ThemeData dark() => _build(Brightness.dark);

  static ThemeData _build(Brightness b) {
    final dark = b == Brightness.dark;
    var scheme = ColorScheme.fromSeed(seedColor: seed, brightness: b);
    if (dark) {
      scheme = scheme.copyWith(
        surface: const Color(0xFF0B1116),
        surfaceContainerHigh: const Color(0xFF151D24),
        surfaceContainerHighest: const Color(0xFF1C262E),
      );
    } else {
      scheme = scheme.copyWith(
        surface: const Color(0xFFF6F9FA),
        surfaceContainerHigh: Colors.white,
      );
    }
    return ThemeData(
      useMaterial3: true,
      colorScheme: scheme,
      scaffoldBackgroundColor: scheme.surface,
      splashFactory: InkSparkle.splashFactory,
      appBarTheme: AppBarTheme(
        backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
        elevation: 0,
        scrolledUnderElevation: 0,
        centerTitle: false,
        titleTextStyle: TextStyle(
            color: scheme.onSurface, fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, letterSpacing: -0.2),
      ),
      navigationBarTheme: NavigationBarThemeData(
        backgroundColor: scheme.surfaceContainerHigh,
        indicatorColor: scheme.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.16),
        height: 66,
        labelBehavior: NavigationDestinationLabelBehavior.alwaysShow,
      ),
      floatingActionButtonTheme: FloatingActionButtonThemeData(
        backgroundColor: scheme.primary,
        foregroundColor: scheme.onPrimary,
        extendedTextStyle: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, letterSpacing: 0.2),
        shape: const StadiumBorder(),
      ),
      filledButtonTheme: FilledButtonThemeData(
        style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
          minimumSize: const Size.fromHeight(52),
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
          textStyle: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 16),
        ),
      ),
      outlinedButtonTheme: OutlinedButtonThemeData(
        style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
          minimumSize: const Size.fromHeight(48),
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
        ),
      ),
      chipTheme: ChipThemeData(
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)),
        side: BorderSide(color: scheme.outlineVariant),
      ),
      snackBarTheme: SnackBarThemeData(
        behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
      ),
      bottomSheetTheme: const BottomSheetThemeData(
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(28))),
        showDragHandle: true,
      ),
    );
  }
}

InputDecoration fieldDecoration(BuildContext context, String label, IconData icon, {Widget? suffix}) {
  final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
  OutlineInputBorder border(Color c, [double w = 1]) => OutlineInputBorder(
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16), borderSide: BorderSide(color: c, width: w));
  return InputDecoration(
    labelText: label,
    prefixIcon: Icon(icon),
    suffixIcon: suffix,
    filled: true,
    fillColor: cs.surfaceContainerHigh,
    enabledBorder: border(cs.outlineVariant),
    focusedBorder: border(cs.primary, 1.6),
    errorBorder: border(cs.error),
    focusedErrorBorder: border(cs.error, 1.6),
  );
}

# FICHIER: core/util.dart

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:geolocator/geolocator.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';

import 'api.dart';

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ tags de contexte sonore
enum NoiseTag { traffic, commerce, construction, music, gathering, other }

extension NoiseTagX on NoiseTag {
  String get label => switch (this) {
        NoiseTag.traffic => 'Circulation',
        NoiseTag.commerce => 'Commerce',
        NoiseTag.construction => 'Chantier',
        NoiseTag.music => 'Musique',
        NoiseTag.gathering => 'Rassemblement',
        NoiseTag.other => 'Autre',
      };
  IconData get icon => switch (this) {
        NoiseTag.traffic => Icons.directions_car_rounded,
        NoiseTag.commerce => Icons.storefront_rounded,
        NoiseTag.construction => Icons.construction_rounded,
        NoiseTag.music => Icons.music_note_rounded,
        NoiseTag.gathering => Icons.groups_rounded,
        NoiseTag.other => Icons.more_horiz_rounded,
      };
  static NoiseTag? parse(String? v) {
    for (final t in NoiseTag.values) {
      if (t.name == v) return t;
    }
    return null;
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ niveaux sonores
Color noiseColor(double db) {
  const stops = <double>[35, 50, 60, 70, 80, 95];
  const cols = <Color>[
    Color(0xFF22C55E),
    Color(0xFF84CC16),
    Color(0xFFFACC15),
    Color(0xFFF97316),
    Color(0xFFEF4444),
    Color(0xFF9333EA),
  ];
  if (db <= stops.first) return cols.first;
  if (db >= stops.last) return cols.last;
  for (var i = 0; i < stops.length - 1; i++) {
    if (db <= stops[i + 1]) {
      final t = (db - stops[i]) / (stops[i + 1] - stops[i]);
      return Color.lerp(cols[i], cols[i + 1], t)!;
    }
  }
  return cols.last;
}

String noiseLabel(double db) => db < 45
    ? 'Calme'
    : db < 60
        ? 'Modéré'
        : db < 70
            ? 'Bruyant'
            : db < 80
                ? 'Très bruyant'
                : 'Intense';

      'morning' => 'Matin',
      'afternoon' => 'Après-midi',
      'evening' => 'Soirée',
      'night' => 'Nuit',
      _ => 'Tout',
    };

  if (e is ApiException) return apiErrorLabel(e.code);
  return 'Une erreur est survenue.';
