// ------------------------------------------------------------------ formats
String fmtDateTime(DateTime d) => DateFormat('d MMM · HH:mm', 'fr_FR').format(d);
String fmtTime(DateTime d) => DateFormat('HH:mm', 'fr_FR').format(d);
String fmtDay(DateTime d) => DateFormat('d MMM', 'fr_FR').format(d);

String fmtAgo(DateTime d) {
  final diff = DateTime.now().difference(d);
  if (diff.inMinutes < 1) return "à l'instant";
  if (diff.inMinutes < 60) return 'il y a ${diff.inMinutes} min';
  if (diff.inHours < 24) return 'il y a ${diff.inHours} h';
  return 'il y a ${diff.inDays} j';
}

void showToast(BuildContext context, String msg) {
  ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
    ..hideCurrentSnackBar()
    ..showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(msg)));
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ permissions
Future<bool> ensureLocationPermission() async {
  if (!await Geolocator.isLocationServiceEnabled()) return false;
  var p = await Geolocator.checkPermission();
  if (p == LocationPermission.denied) p = await Geolocator.requestPermission();
  return p == LocationPermission.whileInUse || p == LocationPermission.always;
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: main.dart
################################################################################

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:intl/date_symbol_data_local.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';
import 'state/roads_view_model.dart';
import 'app.dart';
import 'core/api.dart';
import 'core/config.dart';
import 'core/notifier.dart';
import 'state/account_controller.dart';
import 'state/missions_controller.dart';
import 'state/proposals_controller.dart';
import 'state/quota_controller.dart';
import 'state/recorder_controller.dart';
import 'state/reviews_controller.dart';
import 'state/session_controller.dart';
import 'state/tomtom_view_model.dart';
import 'state/view_models.dart';

Future<void> main() async {
  WidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();
  await initializeDateFormatting('fr_FR');
  final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
  final notifier = Notifier();
  await notifier.init();

  final api = ApiClient(prefs.getString('server_url') ?? AppConfig.defaultApiBase);
  await api.tokens.load();
  final session = SessionController(api, prefs);

  final recorder = RecorderController(api, session, notifier);
  final proposals = ProposalsController(api, session, notifier);
  final mapVm = MapViewModel(api, session);
  final history = HistoryViewModel(api, session);
  final tomtom = TomTomViewModel(api, session);
  final quota = QuotaController(api, session);
  final roads = RoadsViewModel(api, session);
  final missions = MissionsController(api, session, notifier);
  final account = AccountController(api, session);
  final reviews = ReviewsController(api, session, notifier, account);

  runApp(MultiProvider(
    providers: [
      Provider<ApiClient>.value(value: api),
      Provider<Notifier>.value(value: notifier),
      ChangeNotifierProvider<SessionController>.value(value: session),
      ChangeNotifierProvider<RecorderController>.value(value: recorder),
      ChangeNotifierProvider<ProposalsController>.value(value: proposals),
      ChangeNotifierProvider<MapViewModel>.value(value: mapVm),
      ChangeNotifierProvider<HistoryViewModel>.value(value: history),
      ChangeNotifierProvider<TomTomViewModel>.value(value: tomtom),
      ChangeNotifierProvider<QuotaController>.value(value: quota),
      ChangeNotifierProvider<MissionsController>.value(value: missions),
      ChangeNotifierProvider<RoadsViewModel>.value(value: roads),
      ChangeNotifierProvider<AccountController>.value(value: account),
      ChangeNotifierProvider<ReviewsController>.value(value: reviews),
    ],
    child: const SunuApp(),
  ));
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: state/account_controller.dart
################################################################################

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';

import '../core/api.dart';
import '../core/api_ext.dart';
import '../core/models_social.dart';
import 'session_controller.dart';

class AccountController extends ChangeNotifier {
  AccountController(this.api, this.session) {
    session.addListener(_onSession);
  }

  final ApiClient api;
  final SessionController session;

  AccountProfile? profile;
  MyBoard? board;
  AnalystState? analyst;
  Connections? connections;
  List<Invitation> invitations = [];
  bool loading = false;
  bool _on = false;

  void _onSession() {
    final signed = session.status == AuthStatus.signedIn;
    if (signed && !_on) {
      _on = true;
      loadAll();
    } else if (!signed && _on) {
      _on = false;
      profile = null;
      board = null;
      analyst = null;
      connections = null;
      invitations = [];
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  Future<void> _safe(Future<void> Function() f) async {
    try {
      await f();
    } on ApiException catch (_) {}
  }

  Future<void> loadAll() async {
    if (session.user == null) return;
    loading = true;
    notifyListeners();
    await Future.wait([
      _safe(() async => profile = await api.accountMe()),
      _safe(() async => board = await api.myBoard()),
      _safe(() async => analyst = await api.analystMe()),
      _safe(() async => connections = await api.connections()),
      _safe(() async => invitations = await api.invitations()),
    ]);
    loading = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> saveProfile(Map<String, dynamic> body) async {
    profile = await api.accountUpdate(body);
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> setAnalyst(bool enable) async {
    await api.analystSet(enable);
    await _safe(() async => analyst = await api.analystMe());
    await _safe(() async => profile = await api.accountMe());
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> newInvitation() async {
    await api.createInvitation();
    invitations = await api.invitations();
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> revoke(String id) async {
    await api.revokeInvitation(id);
    invitations = await api.invitations();
    notifyListeners();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    session.removeListener(_onSession);
    super.dispose();
  }
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: state/missions_controller.dart
################################################################################

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:geolocator/geolocator.dart';
import '../core/models_roads.dart';
import '../core/api.dart';
import '../core/api_ext.dart';
import '../core/models_ext.dart';
import '../core/notifier.dart';
import '../core/util.dart';
import 'session_controller.dart';

class MissionsController extends ChangeNotifier {
  MissionsController(this.api, this.session, this.notifier) {
    session.addListener(_onSession);
  }

  final ApiClient api;
  final SessionController session;
  final Notifier notifier;

  List<Mission> list = [];
  bool loading = false;
  String? error;
  Timer? _timer;
  bool _on = false;
  final Map<String, String> _prev = {};

  int get offeredCount => list.where((m) => m.status == 'offered').length;
  List<Mission> get active => list.where((m) => m.isActive).toList();

  String get _notifKey => 'missions_notified_${session.user?.id}';

  void _onSession() {
    final signed = session.status == AuthStatus.signedIn;
    if (signed && !_on) {
      _on = true;
      refresh();
      _timer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(minutes: 5), (_) => refresh());
    } else if (!signed && _on) {
      _on = false;
      _timer?.cancel();
      list = [];
      _prev.clear();
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  Future<Position?> _position() async {
    if (!session.consentOf('geolocation')) return null;
    try {
      final perm = await Geolocator.checkPermission();
      if (perm != LocationPermission.whileInUse && perm != LocationPermission.always) return null;
      return await Geolocator.getCurrentPosition(
        locationSettings:
            const LocationSettings(accuracy: LocationAccuracy.low, timeLimit: Duration(seconds: 8)),
      );
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  Future<void> refresh({bool sync = true}) async {
    if (session.user == null) return;
    loading = true;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      final pos = sync ? await _position() : null;
      final r = pos != null ? await api.missionsSync(pos.latitude, pos.longitude) : await api.missions();
      list = r;
      error = null;
      _notify(r);
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      error = e.code;
    }
    loading = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void _notify(List<Mission> r) {
    final seen = (session.prefs.getStringList(_notifKey) ?? []).toSet();
    var changed = false;
    for (final m in r) {
      if (m.status == 'offered' && seen.add(m.id)) {
        changed = true;
        notifier.proposal(
          m.isRoad ? 'Mission route' : 'Nouvelle mission de mesure',
          m.isRoad
              ? '${m.roadName ?? 'Une route'} · à ${m.distanceKm.toStringAsFixed(1)} km · avant ${fmtTime(m.offerExpiresAt)}'
              : '${m.requiredCount} mesure(s) à ${m.distanceKm.toStringAsFixed(1)} km · à accepter avant ${fmtDay(m.offerExpiresAt)} ${fmtTime(m.offerExpiresAt)}',
        );
      }
      if (_prev[m.id] == 'accepted' && m.status == 'completed') {
        notifier.result('Mission accomplie', '+${m.rewardCredits.toStringAsFixed(1)} crédit(s) ajouté(s) à votre solde.');
      }
      _prev[m.id] = m.status;
    }
    if (changed) {
      final trimmed = seen.toList();
      session.prefs.setStringList(_notifKey, trimmed.length > 200 ? trimmed.sublist(trimmed.length - 200) : trimmed);
    }
  }

  Future<void> respond(String id, bool accept) async {
    await api.missionRespond(id, accept);
    await refresh(sync: false);
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    session.removeListener(_onSession);
    _timer?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: state/proposals_controller.dart
################################################################################

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:battery_plus/battery_plus.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:geolocator/geolocator.dart';

import '../core/api.dart';
import '../core/config.dart';
import '../core/models.dart';
import '../core/notifier.dart';
import '../core/util.dart';
import 'session_controller.dart';

class ProposalsController extends ChangeNotifier {
  ProposalsController(this.api, this.session, this.notifier) {
    session.addResetHook(() {
      list = [];
      lastReason = null;
      error = null;
      _sched = null;
      _schedLoaded = false;
      _notified.clear();
      stopPolling();
      notifyListeners();
    });
  }

  final ApiClient api;
  final SessionController session;
  final Notifier notifier;

  List<ProposalOut> list = [];
  bool loading = false;
  String? lastReason;
  String? error;

  Timer? _timer;
  ScheduleModel? _sched;
  bool _schedLoaded = false;
  final Set<String> _notified = {};

  void startPolling() {
    _timer?.cancel();
    _timer = Timer.periodic(Duration(minutes: AppConfig.proposalPollMinutes), (_) => poll());
    poll();
  }

  void stopPolling() {
    _timer?.cancel();
    _timer = null;
  }

  void scheduleChanged() {
    _schedLoaded = false;
    _sched = null;
  }

  Future<void> refresh() async {
    loading = true;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      list = await api.proposals();
      error = null;
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      error = e.code;
    }
    loading = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<ProposalDecision> _ask(String trigger) async {
    if (!await ensureLocationPermission()) throw ApiException(null, 'location_denied');
    final p = await Geolocator.getCurrentPosition(
      locationSettings:
          const LocationSettings(accuracy: LocationAccuracy.high, timeLimit: Duration(seconds: 15)),
    );
    final bat = await Battery().batteryLevel;
    return api.requestProposal(trigger, p.latitude, p.longitude, bat);
  }

  /// Demande manuelle depuis l'écran des propositions.
  Future<void> requestNow(String trigger) async {
    loading = true;
    error = null;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      final d = await _ask(trigger);
      lastReason = d.reason;
      list = await api.proposals();
      if (d.proposed && d.proposal != null) _notifyNew(d.proposal!);
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      error = e.code;
    } catch (_) {
      error = 'gps_unavailable';
    }
    loading = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<ProposalOut> respond(String id, bool accept) async {
    final p = await api.respondProposal(id, accept);
    await refresh();
    return p;
  }

  void _notifyNew(ProposalOut p) {
    if (_notified.add(p.id) && p.status == 'pending') {
      notifier.proposal('Mesure proposée près de vous',
          'Zone prioritaire · à accepter avant ${fmtTime(p.expiresAt)}');
    }
  }

  /// Sondage périodique (application ouverte) : demande une proposition selon le planning de l'utilisateur.
  Future<void> poll() async {
    final u = session.user;
    if (u == null || !u.hybridCollectionEnabled || u.participationPaused || !session.consentsGranted) {
      return;
    }
    try {
      list = await api.proposals();
      notifyListeners();
      for (final p in list) {
        _notifyNew(p);
      }
      if (list.isNotEmpty) return;
      if (!_schedLoaded) {
        _sched = await api.getSchedule();
        _schedLoaded = true;
      }
      final s = _sched;
      if (s == null || !s.active) return;
      for (final t in [
        if (s.allowsScheduled) 'scheduled',
        if (s.allowsContextual) 'contextual',
      ]) {
        final d = await _ask(t);
        lastReason = d.reason;
        if (d.proposed && d.proposal != null) {
          list = [d.proposal!];
          _notifyNew(d.proposal!);
          break;
        }
      }
      notifyListeners();
    } catch (_) {}
  }
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: state/quota_controller.dart
################################################################################

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';

import '../core/api.dart';
import '../core/api_ext.dart';
import '../core/models_ext.dart';
import 'session_controller.dart';

class QuotaController extends ChangeNotifier {
  QuotaController(this.api, this.session) {
    session.addListener(_onSession);
  }

  final ApiClient api;
  final SessionController session;

  QuotaState? state;
  String? error;
  Timer? _timer;
  bool _on = false;

  void _onSession() {
    final signed = session.status == AuthStatus.signedIn;
    if (signed && !_on) {
      _on = true;
      refresh();
      _timer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(minutes: 5), (_) => refresh());
    } else if (!signed && _on) {
      _on = false;
      _timer?.cancel();
      state = null;
      error = null;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  Future<void> refresh() async {
    if (session.user == null) return;
    try {
      state = await api.quotaMe();
      error = null;
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      error = e.code;
    }
    notifyListeners();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    session.removeListener(_onSession);
    _timer?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: state/recorder_controller.dart
################################################################################

import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:math';
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:geolocator/geolocator.dart';
import 'package:record/record.dart';
import 'package:uuid/uuid.dart';
import 'package:wakelock_plus/wakelock_plus.dart';
import '../core/api_ext.dart';
import '../core/api.dart';
import '../core/audio_meter.dart';
import '../core/config.dart';
import '../core/local_store.dart';
import '../core/models.dart';
import '../core/notifier.dart';
import '../core/util.dart';
import 'session_controller.dart';

enum RecPhase { idle, preparing, recording, sending, done, failed }

class RecError implements Exception {
  RecError(this.code);
  final String code;
}

class RecResult {
  RecResult({
    required this.laeq,
    required this.lmax,
    required this.lmin,
    required this.seconds,
    required this.sent,
    this.tag,
  });
  final double laeq, lmax, lmin, seconds;
  final bool sent;
  final NoiseTag? tag;
}

class RecorderController extends ChangeNotifier {
  RecorderController(this.api, this.session, this.notifier) {
    session.addResetHook(() {
      _resetValues();
      phase = RecPhase.idle;
      pendingCount = 0;
      notifyListeners();
    });
  }

  final ApiClient api;
  final SessionController session;
  final Notifier notifier;
  final AudioRecorder _rec = AudioRecorder();

  RecPhase phase = RecPhase.idle;
  double liveDb = 0, leqDb = 0, maxDb = 0;
  int elapsed = 0;
  int total = AppConfig.defaultSessionSeconds;
  String? errorCode;
  RecResult? result;
  String? qualityStatus;
  double? qualityScore;
  List<String> reasons = [];
  NoiseTag? tag;
  ProposalOut? proposal;
  Position? gps;
  int pendingCount = 0;
  int completedVersion = 0;

  NoiseAccumulator? _acc;
  StreamSubscription<Uint8List>? _sub;
  StreamSubscription<Position>? _gpsSub;
  Timer? _timer;
  Position? _origin;
  double _maxMove = 0;
  DateTime? _startAt;
  String? _deviceId;
  String? _lastServerId;
  bool _finishing = false, _flushing = false, _disposed = false;

  bool get busy =>
      phase == RecPhase.preparing || phase == RecPhase.recording || phase == RecPhase.sending;

  List<double> get wave {
    final b = _acc?.blocks ?? const <double>[];
    return b.length > 48 ? b.sublist(b.length - 48) : b;
  }

  void setTag(NoiseTag? t) {
    tag = t;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void attachProposal(ProposalOut? p) {
    proposal = p;
    total = p?.sessionSeconds ?? AppConfig.defaultSessionSeconds;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void resetForNew() {
    if (busy) return;
    _resetValues();
    phase = RecPhase.idle;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void _resetValues() {
    liveDb = leqDb = maxDb = 0;
    elapsed = 0;
    errorCode = null;
    result = null;
    qualityStatus = null;
    qualityScore = null;
    reasons = [];
    _acc = null;
    _finishing = false;
    _lastServerId = null;
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ GPS (précision en direct)
  Future<void> startGpsWatch() async {
    if (_gpsSub != null) return;
    if (!await ensureLocationPermission()) return;
    _gpsSub = Geolocator.getPositionStream(
      locationSettings: const LocationSettings(accuracy: LocationAccuracy.best, distanceFilter: 0),
    ).listen((p) {
      gps = p;
      final o = _origin;
      if (o != null && phase == RecPhase.recording) {
        final d = Geolocator.distanceBetween(o.latitude, o.longitude, p.latitude, p.longitude);
        if (d > _maxMove) _maxMove = d;
      }
      notifyListeners();
    }, onError: (_) {});
  }

  Future<void> stopGpsWatch() async {
    if (busy) return;
    await _gpsSub?.cancel();
    _gpsSub = null;
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ enregistrement
  Future<void> start() async {
    if (busy) return;
    _resetValues();
    phase = RecPhase.preparing;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      final u = session.user;
      if (u == null) throw RecError('not_authenticated');
      if (u.participationPaused) throw RecError('participation_paused');
      if (!session.consentsGranted) throw RecError('consent_required');
      if (!await _rec.hasPermission()) throw RecError('mic_denied');
      if (!await ensureLocationPermission()) throw RecError('location_denied');
      final deviceId = await session.ensureDevice();
      await startGpsWatch();

      Position origin;
      try {
        origin = await Geolocator.getCurrentPosition(
          locationSettings:
              const LocationSettings(accuracy: LocationAccuracy.best, timeLimit: Duration(seconds: 20)),
        );
      } catch (_) {
        throw RecError('gps_unavailable');
      }

      await _checkQuota(origin);
      _origin = origin;
      _maxMove = 0;
      _deviceId = deviceId;
      _startAt = DateTime.now().toUtc();
      total = proposal?.sessionSeconds ?? AppConfig.defaultSessionSeconds;
      _acc = NoiseAccumulator(AppConfig.sampleRate);

      final stream = await _rec.startStream(const RecordConfig(
        encoder: AudioEncoder.pcm16bits,
        sampleRate: AppConfig.sampleRate,
        numChannels: 1,
        autoGain: false,
        echoCancel: false,
        noiseSuppress: false,
      ));
      await WakelockPlus.enable();
      phase = RecPhase.recording;
      notifyListeners();
      _sub = stream.listen(_onAudio, onError: (_) => _fail('capture_error'), cancelOnError: true);
      _timer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 1), (_) {
        notifier.recordingProgress(elapsed, total, liveDb);
      });
    } catch (e) {
      await _stopCapture();
      errorCode = e is RecError ? e.code : (e is ApiException ? e.code : 'capture_error');
      phase = RecPhase.failed;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  /// Pré-contrôle du solde et du délai. Hors-ligne ou serveur indisponible : la mesure part en file
  /// et le serveur arbitre à la synchronisation (le solde peut alors passer en négatif).
  Future<void> _checkQuota(Position origin) async {
    try {
      final q = await api.quotaCheck(origin.latitude, origin.longitude, proposalId: proposal?.id);
      if (!q.allowed) throw RecError(q.reason);
    } on ApiException {
      // on laisse passer
    }
  }

  void _onAudio(Uint8List bytes) {
    final a = _acc;
    if (a == null || _finishing) return;
    a.add(bytes);
    liveDb = a.lastBlockDb;
    leqDb = a.leqDb;
    maxDb = a.maxDb;
    elapsed = min(a.seconds.floor(), total);
    notifyListeners();
    if (a.seconds >= total) _finish();
  }

  Future<void> _stopCapture() async {
    await _sub?.cancel();
    _sub = null;
    _timer?.cancel();
    _timer = null;
    try {
      await _rec.stop();
    } catch (_) {}
    try {
      await WakelockPlus.disable();
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  Future<void> _fail(String code) async {
    if (_finishing) return;
    _finishing = true;
    await _stopCapture();
    await notifier.cancelProgress();
    errorCode = code;
    phase = RecPhase.failed;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> cancel() async {
    if (phase != RecPhase.recording && phase != RecPhase.preparing) return;
    _finishing = true;
    await _stopCapture();
    await notifier.cancelProgress();
    _resetValues();
    phase = RecPhase.idle;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> _finish() async {
    if (_finishing) return;
    _finishing = true;
    phase = RecPhase.sending;
    notifyListeners();
    await _stopCapture();
    await notifier.cancelProgress();

    final s = _acc!.finish();
    final origin = _origin!;
    double c(double v) => v.clamp(0.0, 200.0).toDouble();
    final payload = <String, dynamic>{
      'client_measurement_id': const Uuid().v4(),
      'device_id': _deviceId,
      if (proposal != null) 'proposal_id': proposal!.id,
      'measured_at': _startAt!.toIso8601String(),
      'latitude': origin.latitude,
      'longitude': origin.longitude,
      'gps_accuracy_m': origin.accuracy,
      'duration_seconds': total.toDouble(),
      'effective_duration_seconds': min(s.seconds, total.toDouble()),
      'sample_rate_hz': AppConfig.sampleRate,
      'laeq': c(s.laeq),
      'lmax': c(s.lmax),
      'lmin': c(s.lmin),
      'saturation_ratio': s.saturation.clamp(0.0, 1.0),
      'moved_distance_m': _maxMove,
      if (tag != null) 'noise_context': tag!.name,
    };

    final store = session.store!;
    await store.addPending(payload);
    await store.addPoint(MyPoint(origin.latitude, origin.longitude, c(s.laeq), DateTime.now()));

    final res = await _flush();
    final sentId = res.accepted[payload['client_measurement_id'] as String];
    _lastServerId = sentId;
    result = RecResult(
        laeq: c(s.laeq),
        lmax: c(s.lmax),
        lmin: c(s.lmin),
        seconds: s.seconds,
        sent: sentId != null,
        tag: tag);
    proposal = null;
    phase = RecPhase.done;
    completedVersion++;
    notifyListeners();

    if (sentId != null) {
      notifier.result('Mesure envoyée',
          'LAeq ${c(s.laeq).toStringAsFixed(1)} dB(A) · évaluation en cours…');
      unawaited(_watch({sentId}));
    } else {
      notifier.result('Mesure enregistrée',
          'Elle sera envoyée dès que la connexion le permettra.');
    }
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ envoi hors-ligne
  Future<FlushResult> _flush() async {
    final store = session.store;
    if (store == null) return FlushResult();
    while (_flushing) {
      await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 200));
    }
    _flushing = true;
    try {
      final r = await store.flush(api);
      pendingCount = store.pendingCount;
      if (r.accepted.isNotEmpty) completedVersion++;
      return r;
    } finally {
      _flushing = false;
    }
  }

  Future<void> refreshPending() async {
    pendingCount = session.store?.pendingCount ?? 0;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> flushPending() async {
    if (session.store == null || busy) return;
    final before = session.store!.pendingCount;
    if (before == 0) {
      pendingCount = 0;
      notifyListeners();
      return;
    }
    final r = await _flush();
    notifyListeners();
    if (r.accepted.isNotEmpty) {
      notifier.result('Mesures envoyées',
          '${r.accepted.length} mesure(s) hors-ligne synchronisée(s).');
      unawaited(_watch(r.accepted.values.toSet()));
    }
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ suivi de l'évaluation
  Future<void> _watch(Set<String> ids) async {
    final pending = {...ids};
    for (var i = 0; i < 14 && pending.isNotEmpty && !_disposed; i++) {
      await Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 5));
      try {
        final items = await api.history(limit: 50, offset: 0);
        for (final it in items) {
          if (!pending.contains(it.id) || it.qualityStatus == 'pending') continue;
          pending.remove(it.id);
          if (it.id == _lastServerId) {
            qualityStatus = it.qualityStatus;
            qualityScore = it.qualityScore;
            reasons = it.reasons;
          }
          completedVersion++;
          final score = it.qualityScore == null ? '' : 'Score ${(it.qualityScore! * 100).round()} %';
          final why = it.reasons.isNotEmpty ? ' · ${reasonLabel(it.reasons.first)}' : '';
          notifier.result(
            switch (it.qualityStatus) {
              'validated' => 'Mesure validée',
              'indicative' => 'Mesure indicative',
              _ => 'Mesure rejetée',
            },
            '$score$why'.trim().isEmpty ? qualityLabel(it.qualityStatus) : '$score$why',
          );
          notifyListeners();
        }
      } catch (_) {}
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _disposed = true;
    _rec.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: state/reviews_controller.dart
################################################################################

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart';
import 'package:geolocator/geolocator.dart';

import '../core/api.dart';
import '../core/api_ext.dart';
import '../core/models_social.dart';
import '../core/notifier.dart';
import '../core/util.dart';
import 'account_controller.dart';
import 'session_controller.dart';

class ReviewsController extends ChangeNotifier with WidgetsBindingObserver {
  ReviewsController(this.api, this.session, this.notifier, this.account) {
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addObserver(this);
    account.addListener(_sync);
  }

  final ApiClient api;
  final SessionController session;
  final Notifier notifier;
  final AccountController account;

  List<ReviewRequest> pending = [];
  List<ReviewHistoryItem> history = [];
  Timer? _poll;
  Timer? _beat;
  bool _foreground = true;
  final Set<String> _notified = {};

  bool get active {
    final p = account.profile, a = account.analyst;
    return session.status == AuthStatus.signedIn && p != null && p.analystEnabled && !(a?.suspended ?? false);
  }

  void _sync() {
    if (active && _foreground) {
      _poll ??= Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 15), (_) => refresh());
      _beat ??= Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 60), (_) => _heartbeat());
      _heartbeat();
      refresh();
    } else {
      _poll?.cancel();
      _beat?.cancel();
      _poll = _beat = null;
      if (pending.isNotEmpty && !active) {
        pending = [];
        notifyListeners();
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  void didChangeAppLifecycleState(AppLifecycleState state) {
    _foreground = state == AppLifecycleState.resumed;
    _sync();
  }

  Future<void> _heartbeat() async {
    if (!active || !session.consentOf('geolocation')) return;
    try {
      final perm = await Geolocator.checkPermission();
      if (perm != LocationPermission.whileInUse && perm != LocationPermission.always) return;
      final p = await Geolocator.getCurrentPosition(
          locationSettings: const LocationSettings(accuracy: LocationAccuracy.medium, timeLimit: Duration(seconds: 8)));
      await api.presence(p.latitude, p.longitude);
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  Future<void> refresh() async {
    if (!active) return;
    try {
      final r = await api.reviewsPending();
      for (final q in r) {
        if (_notified.add(q.id)) {
          notifier.proposal('Avis demandé près de vous',
              'Ce niveau de ${q.levelDb.toStringAsFixed(0)} dB(A) correspond-il à ce que vous entendez ? Répondez avant ${fmtTime(q.expiresAt)}.');
        }
      }
      pending = r;
      notifyListeners();
    } on ApiException catch (_) {}
  }

  Future<void> loadHistory() async {
    try {
      history = await api.reviewsHistory();
      notifyListeners();
    } on ApiException catch (_) {}
  }

  Future<void> vote(String id, String vote, bool strong) async {
    await api.reviewVote(id, vote, strong);
    pending = pending.where((p) => p.id != id).toList();
    notifyListeners();
    account.loadAll();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    WidgetsBinding.instance.removeObserver(this);
    account.removeListener(_sync);
    _poll?.cancel();
    _beat?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: state/roads_view_model.dart
################################################################################

import 'dart:math';

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:latlong2/latlong.dart';

import '../core/api.dart';
import '../core/api_ext.dart';
import '../core/models.dart';
import '../core/models_roads.dart';
import 'session_controller.dart';

class _Net {
  _Net(this.etag, this.segs, this.truncated);
  final String? etag;
  final List<RoadSeg> segs;
  final bool truncated;
}

/// Réseau routier réel : géométrie (mise en cache + ETag) et état de bruit (rafraîchi souvent).
class RoadsViewModel extends ChangeNotifier {
  RoadsViewModel(this.api, this.session) {
    session.addResetHook(() {
      segs = const [];
      states = const {};
      _cache.clear();
      selected = null;
      notifyListeners();
    });
  }

  final ApiClient api;
  final SessionController session;

  /// En dessous, seules les très grandes routes existeraient : on n'affiche rien.
  static const double minZoom = 10.5;

  List<RoadSeg> segs = const [];
  Map<String, RoadState> states = const {};
  bool loading = false, truncated = false;
  String? error, selected;

  final Map<String, _Net> _cache = {};
  bool _busy = false, _again = false;
  MapFilters? _f;
  String? _bbox;
  double _zoom = 0;

  static int rankFor(double z) => z < 8 ? 2 : z < 11 ? 3 : z < 13 ? 4 : z < 15 ? 5 : 6;

  /// bbox calée sur 0,02° : mêmes clés de cache et mêmes ETag pour tous.
  static String _snap(String bbox) {
    final a = bbox.split(',').map(double.parse).toList();
    const s = 0.02;
    String f(double v) => v.toStringAsFixed(5);
    return [
      f((a[0] / s).floorToDouble() * s),
      f((a[1] / s).floorToDouble() * s),
      f((a[2] / s).ceilToDouble() * s),
      f((a[3] / s).ceilToDouble() * s),
    ].join(',');
  }

  void select(String? id) {
    selected = id;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> load(MapFilters f, String bbox, double zoom) async {
    _f = f;
    _bbox = bbox;
    _zoom = zoom;
    if (zoom < minZoom) {
      if (segs.isNotEmpty || states.isNotEmpty) {
        segs = const [];
        states = const {};
        truncated = false;
        notifyListeners();
      }
      return;
    }
    if (_busy) {
      _again = true;
      return;
    }
    _busy = true;
    loading = true;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      final box = _snap(bbox);
      final rank = rankFor(zoom);
      final key = '$box|$rank';
      final old = _cache[key];
      final results = await Future.wait([
        api.getConditional('/roads/network', query: {'bbox': box, 'max_rank': rank}, etag: old?.etag),
        api.roadStates(f, box, zoom, rank),
      ]);
      final r = results[0] as ({int status, dynamic data, String? etag});
      _Net net;
      if (r.status == 304 && old != null) {
        net = old;
      } else {
        final d = r.data as Map<String, dynamic>;
        net = _Net(r.etag, [for (final x in d['features'] as List) RoadSeg.fromFeature(x as Map<String, dynamic>)],
            (d['truncated'] as bool?) ?? false);
        _cache[key] = net;
        if (_cache.length > 8) _cache.remove(_cache.keys.first);
      }
      if (!identical(segs, net.segs)) segs = net.segs;
      truncated = net.truncated;
      states = results[1] as Map<String, RoadState>;
      error = null;
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      error = e.code;
    } catch (_) {
      error = 'network';
    }
    loading = false;
    _busy = false;
    notifyListeners();
    if (_again) {
      _again = false;
      load(_f!, _bbox!, _zoom);
    }
  }

  Future<void> refreshStates() async {
    final f = _f, b = _bbox;
    if (f == null || b == null) return;
    await load(f, b, _zoom);
  }

  /// Tronçon touché (tolérance ≈ 16 pixels écran), ou null.
  RoadSeg? hit(LatLng p, double zoom) {
    if (segs.isEmpty) return null;
    final cosLat = cos(p.latitude * pi / 180);
    final tol = 156543.03392 * cosLat / pow(2, zoom) * 16;
    final dLat = tol / 110574.0, dLon = tol / (111320.0 * cosLat);
    RoadSeg? best;
    var bd = tol;
    for (final s in segs) {
      if (p.latitude < s.minLat - dLat || p.latitude > s.maxLat + dLat) continue;
      if (p.longitude < s.minLon - dLon || p.longitude > s.maxLon + dLon) continue;
      for (var i = 0; i < s.points.length - 1; i++) {
        final d = _distM(p, s.points[i], s.points[i + 1], cosLat);
        if (d < bd) {
          bd = d;
          best = s;
        }
      }
    }
    return best;
  }

  static double _distM(LatLng p, LatLng a, LatLng b, double cosLat) {
    final ax = (a.longitude - p.longitude) * 111320.0 * cosLat, ay = (a.latitude - p.latitude) * 110574.0;
    final bx = (b.longitude - p.longitude) * 111320.0 * cosLat, by = (b.latitude - p.latitude) * 110574.0;
    final dx = bx - ax, dy = by - ay;
    final l2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;
    final t = l2 == 0 ? 0.0 : ((-ax * dx - ay * dy) / l2).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
    final x = ax + dx * t, y = ay + dy * t;
    return sqrt(x * x + y * y);
  }
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: state/session_controller.dart
################################################################################

import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:device_info_plus/device_info_plus.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';
import 'package:uuid/uuid.dart';

import '../core/api.dart';
import '../core/config.dart';
import '../core/local_store.dart';
import '../core/models.dart';

enum AuthStatus { booting, signedOut, signedIn }

class SessionController extends ChangeNotifier {
  SessionController(this.api, this.prefs)
      : themeMode = _parseTheme(prefs.getString('theme')),
        serverUrl = prefs.getString('server_url') ?? AppConfig.defaultApiBase {
    api.onSessionExpired = () async {
      await _clearLocal();
      status = AuthStatus.signedOut;
      notifyListeners();
    };
  }

  final ApiClient api;
  final SharedPreferences prefs;

  AuthStatus status = AuthStatus.booting;
  UserMe? user;
  List<ConsentItem> consents = [];
  ThemeMode themeMode;
  String serverUrl;
  bool consentSkipped = false;
  String? _deviceId;
  final List<VoidCallback> _hooks = [];

  static ThemeMode _parseTheme(String? v) =>
      v == 'light' ? ThemeMode.light : (v == 'dark' ? ThemeMode.dark : ThemeMode.system);

  bool get consentsGranted =>
      ['geolocation', 'microphone'].every((t) => consents.any((c) => c.type == t && c.granted));
  bool consentOf(String type) => consents.any((c) => c.type == type && c.granted);
  bool get needsConsent => status == AuthStatus.signedIn && !consentsGranted && !consentSkipped;

  LocalStore? get store => user == null ? null : LocalStore(prefs, user!.id);

  void addResetHook(VoidCallback cb) => _hooks.add(cb);

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ démarrage
  Future<void> boot() async {
    final started = DateTime.now();
    var next = AuthStatus.signedOut;
    if (api.tokens.refresh != null) {
      try {
        await _loadAccount();
        next = AuthStatus.signedIn;
      } on ApiException catch (e) {
        if (e.code == 'network' && _loadCache()) next = AuthStatus.signedIn;
      }
    }
    final elapsed = DateTime.now().difference(started);
    if (elapsed < const Duration(milliseconds: 2000)) {
      await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 2000) - elapsed);
    }
    status = next;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> _loadAccount() async {
    user = await api.me();
    consents = await api.consents();
    await prefs.setString('account_cache', jsonEncode({
      'user': user!.toJson(),
      'consents': [for (final c in consents) c.toJson()],
    }));
  }

  bool _loadCache() {
    final raw = prefs.getString('account_cache');
    if (raw == null) return false;
    final m = jsonDecode(raw) as Map<String, dynamic>;
    user = UserMe.fromJson(m['user'] as Map<String, dynamic>);
    consents = [
      for (final c in m['consents'] as List) ConsentItem.fromJson((c as Map).cast<String, dynamic>())
    ];
    return true;
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ auth
  Future<void> signIn(String email, String password) async {
    await api.login(email, password);
    await _loadAccount();
    consentSkipped = false;
    status = AuthStatus.signedIn;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> signUp(String email, String password) async {
    await api.register(email, password);
    await signIn(email, password);
  }

  Future<void> signOut() async {
    try {
      await api.logout();
    } catch (_) {}
    await _clearLocal();
    status = AuthStatus.signedOut;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> afterAccountDeleted() async {
    final uid = user?.id;
    await _clearLocal();
    if (uid != null) {
      await prefs.remove('pending_$uid');
      await prefs.remove('points_$uid');
      await prefs.remove('install_$uid');
    }
    status = AuthStatus.signedOut;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> _clearLocal() async {
    await api.tokens.clear();
    user = null;
    consents = [];
    _deviceId = null;
    consentSkipped = false;
    await prefs.remove('account_cache');
    for (final h in _hooks) {
      h();
    }
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ préférences
  Future<void> setThemeMode(ThemeMode m) async {
    themeMode = m;
    await prefs.setString('theme', m == ThemeMode.light ? 'light' : (m == ThemeMode.dark ? 'dark' : 'system'));
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> setServerUrl(String url) async {
    final clean = url.trim();
    if (clean.isEmpty || clean == serverUrl) return;
    serverUrl = clean;
    await prefs.setString('server_url', clean);
    api.setBase(clean);
    notifyListeners();
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ compte
  Future<void> setConsent(String type, bool granted) async {
    consents = await api.setConsent(type, granted);
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> saveConsents({required bool geolocation, required bool microphone}) async {
    if (geolocation != consentOf('geolocation')) await setConsent('geolocation', geolocation);
    if (microphone != consentOf('microphone')) await setConsent('microphone', microphone);
  }

  void skipConsents() {
    consentSkipped = true;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> setHybrid(bool v) async {
    user = await api.patchMe(hybrid: v);
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> setPaused(bool v) async {
    user = await api.patchMe(paused: v);
    notifyListeners();
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ appareil
  Future<String> ensureDevice() async {
    if (_deviceId != null) return _deviceId!;
    final uid = user!.id;
    var install = prefs.getString('install_$uid');
    if (install == null) {
      install = const Uuid().v4();
      await prefs.setString('install_$uid', install);
    }
    final info = await DeviceInfoPlugin().androidInfo;
    final dev = await api.registerDevice(
      installId: install,
      manufacturer: info.manufacturer,
      model: info.model,
      androidVersion: info.version.release,
      sampleRate: AppConfig.sampleRate,
    );
    _deviceId = dev.id;
    return dev.id;
  }
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: state/tomtom_view_model.dart
################################################################################

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:latlong2/latlong.dart';

import '../core/api.dart';
import '../core/models.dart';
import 'session_controller.dart';

class TomTomViewModel extends ChangeNotifier {
  TomTomViewModel(this.api, this.session);

  final ApiClient api;
  final SessionController session;

  bool _tilesUnavailable = false;
  bool _tilesRequested = false;
  String? _flowUrl;
  String? get flowUrl => _flowUrl;

  List<TrafficIncident> incidents = const [];
  RouteResult? route;

  DateTime? _incidentsAt;
  String? _incidentsBbox;
  bool _loadingIncidents = false;

  Future<void> ensureTiles() async {
    if (_tilesUnavailable || _tilesRequested) return;
    _tilesRequested = true;
    try {
      final s = await api.tileSession();
      // Le backend renvoie plusieurs couches ; on prend 'flow' si dispo, sinon la 1re.
      _flowUrl = s.layers['flow'] ?? (s.layers.isNotEmpty ? s.layers.values.first : null);
      if (_flowUrl == null) {
        _tilesUnavailable = true;
        debugPrint('[TomTom] tile-session vide → désactivé');
      }
      notifyListeners();
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      if (e.status == 404) {
        _tilesUnavailable = true;
        debugPrint('[TomTom] tile-session 404 → désactivé');
      } else {
        _tilesRequested = false;
      }
    } catch (_) {
      _tilesRequested = false;
    }
  }

  Future<void> loadIncidents(String bbox) async {
    if (_tilesUnavailable || _loadingIncidents) return;
    if (_incidentsAt != null && _incidentsBbox == bbox &&
        DateTime.now().difference(_incidentsAt!) < const Duration(seconds: 60)) {
      return;
    }
    _loadingIncidents = true;
    try {
      incidents = await api.trafficIncidents(bbox);
      _incidentsAt = DateTime.now();
      _incidentsBbox = bbox;
      notifyListeners();
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      if (e.status == 404) _tilesUnavailable = true;
    } catch (_) {}
    _loadingIncidents = false;
  }

  void clearIncidents() {
    if (incidents.isEmpty) return;
    incidents = const [];
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> routeTo(LatLng from, LatLng to) async {
    if (_tilesUnavailable) throw ApiException(503, 'tomtom_unavailable');
    route = await api.route(from.latitude, from.longitude, to.latitude, to.longitude);
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void clearRoute() {
    if (route == null) return;
    route = null;
    notifyListeners();
  }
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: state/view_models.dart
################################################################################

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:latlong2/latlong.dart';

import '../core/api.dart';
import '../core/config.dart';
import '../core/local_store.dart';
import '../core/models.dart';
import 'session_controller.dart';

class MapViewModel extends ChangeNotifier {
  MapViewModel(this.api, this.session) {
    session.addResetHook(() {
      cells = [];
      myPoints = [];
      selected = null;
      error = null;
      stopAuto();
      notifyListeners();
    });
  }

  final ApiClient api;
  final SessionController session;

  MapFilters filters = const MapFilters();
  List<NoiseCell> cells = [];
  List<MyPoint> myPoints = [];
  bool loading = false;
  String? error;
  DateTime? updatedAt;
  String? selected;
  String? _bbox;
  Timer? _auto;
  bool _loadingNow = false;

  List<NoiseCell> get hot => cells.where((c) => c.isHot).toList();

  void select(String? h3) {
    selected = h3;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> load({String? bbox}) async {
    if (bbox != null) _bbox = bbox;
    if (_loadingNow) return;
    _loadingNow = true;
    loading = true;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      cells = await api.mapNoise(filters, bbox: _bbox);
      error = null;
      updatedAt = DateTime.now();
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      error = e.code;
    }
    myPoints = session.store?.loadPoints() ?? [];
    loading = false;
    _loadingNow = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void setFilters(MapFilters f) {
    filters = f;
    load();
  }

  void startAuto() {
    _auto?.cancel();
    _auto = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: AppConfig.mapRefreshSeconds), (_) => load());
  }

  void stopAuto() {
    _auto?.cancel();
    _auto = null;
  }

  void refreshMine() {
    myPoints = session.store?.loadPoints() ?? [];
    notifyListeners();
  }

  NoiseCell? cellAt(LatLng p) {
    for (final c in cells) {
      if (_inPolygon(p, c.polygon)) return c;
    }
    return null;
  }

  static bool _inPolygon(LatLng p, List<LatLng> poly) {
    var inside = false;
    for (var i = 0, j = poly.length - 1; i < poly.length; j = i++) {
      final xi = poly[i].longitude, yi = poly[i].latitude;
      final xj = poly[j].longitude, yj = poly[j].latitude;
      final cross = ((yi > p.latitude) != (yj > p.latitude)) &&
          (p.longitude < (xj - xi) * (p.latitude - yi) / (yj - yi) + xi);
      if (cross) inside = !inside;
    }
    return inside;
  }
}

class HistoryViewModel extends ChangeNotifier {
  HistoryViewModel(this.api, this.session) {
    session.addResetHook(() {
      items = [];
      stats = null;
      hasMore = true;
      error = null;
      notifyListeners();
    });
  }

  final ApiClient api;
  final SessionController session;
  static const _page = 30;

  List<HistoryItem> items = [];
  Contributions? stats;
  bool loading = false, hasMore = true;
  String? error;

  Future<void> reload() async {
    loading = true;
    error = null;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      final r = await Future.wait([api.history(limit: _page, offset: 0), api.contributions()]);
      items = r[0] as List<HistoryItem>;
      stats = r[1] as Contributions;
      hasMore = items.length == _page;
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      error = e.code;
    }
    loading = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> loadMore() async {
    if (loading || !hasMore) return;
    loading = true;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      final more = await api.history(limit: _page, offset: items.length);
      items = [...items, ...more];
      hasMore = more.length == _page;
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      error = e.code;
    }
    loading = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

import '../core/api.dart';
import '../core/api_ext.dart';
//import '../core/config.dart';
import '../core/theme.dart';
import '../core/util.dart';
import '../state/session_controller.dart';
import 'widgets.dart';

bool passwordPolicyOk(String p) {
  final classes = [RegExp(r'[a-z]'), RegExp(r'[A-Z]'), RegExp(r'\d'), RegExp(r'[^A-Za-z0-9]')]
      .where((r) => r.hasMatch(p))
      .length;
  return p.length >= 10 && RegExp(r'[A-Za-z]').hasMatch(p) && RegExp(r'\d').hasMatch(p) && (p.length >= 14 || classes >= 3);
}

({double value, String label, Color color}) passwordStrength(String p) {
  var s = 0;
  if (p.length >= 10) s++;
  if (p.length >= 14) s++;
  if (RegExp(r'[a-z]').hasMatch(p) && RegExp(r'[A-Z]').hasMatch(p)) s++;
  if (RegExp(r'\d').hasMatch(p)) s++;
  if (RegExp(r'[^A-Za-z0-9]').hasMatch(p)) s++;
  return switch (s) {
    <= 1 => (value: 0.2, label: 'Faible', color: const Color(0xFFEF4444)),
    2 => (value: 0.45, label: 'Moyen', color: const Color(0xFFF59E0B)),
    3 => (value: 0.7, label: 'Bon', color: const Color(0xFF84CC16)),
    _ => (value: 1.0, label: 'Excellent', color: const Color(0xFF22C55E)),
  };
}

class AuthScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const AuthScreen({super.key});
  @override
  State<AuthScreen> createState() => _AuthScreenState();
}

class _AuthScreenState extends State<AuthScreen> {
  final _keys = [GlobalKey<FormState>(), GlobalKey<FormState>(), GlobalKey<FormState>()];
  final _email = TextEditingController();
  final _pass = TextEditingController();
  final _pass2 = TextEditingController();
  final _name = TextEditingController();
  final _first = TextEditingController();
  final _last = TextEditingController();
  final _phone = TextEditingController();
  final _city = TextEditingController();
  final _district = TextEditingController();
  final _invite = TextEditingController();
  int? _year;
  bool _register = false, _busy = false, _hide = true, _accept = false;
  int _step = 0;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _pass.addListener(() => setState(() {}));
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    for (final c in [_email, _pass, _pass2, _name, _first, _last, _phone, _city, _district, _invite]) {
      c.dispose();
    }
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _showCodes(List<String> codes) async {
    var saved = false;
    await showDialog<void>(
      context: context,
      barrierDismissible: false,
      builder: (ctx) => StatefulBuilder(
        builder: (ctx, setD) => AlertDialog(
          title: const Text('Codes de récupération'),
          content: SingleChildScrollView(
            child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
              const Text(
                  'Conservez ces 8 codes en lieu sûr. Chacun permet de réinitialiser votre mot de passe une seule fois (votre e-mail n\'est pas stocké en clair, aucun mail de récupération n\'est possible). Ils ne seront plus affichés.',
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13)),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              SelectableText(codes.join('\n'), style: const TextStyle(fontFamily: 'monospace', fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 16, height: 1.6)),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              TextButton.icon(
                onPressed: () {
                  Clipboard.setData(ClipboardData(text: codes.join('\n')));
                  showToast(ctx, 'Codes copiés');
                },
                icon: const Icon(Icons.copy_rounded),
                label: const Text('Copier'),
              ),
              CheckboxListTile(
                contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                value: saved,
                onChanged: (v) => setD(() => saved = v ?? false),
                title: const Text("J'ai conservé mes codes", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13)),
              ),
            ]),
          ),
          actions: [FilledButton(onPressed: saved ? () => Navigator.pop(ctx) : null, child: const Text('Continuer'))],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _submit() async {
    final s = context.read<SessionController>();
    final api = context.read<ApiClient>();
    setState(() => _busy = true);
    try {
      final email = _email.text.trim();
      if (_register) {
        final res = await api.registerAccount({
          'email': email,
          'password': _pass.text,
          'display_name': _name.text.trim(),
          if (_first.text.trim().isNotEmpty) 'first_name': _first.text.trim(),
          if (_last.text.trim().isNotEmpty) 'last_name': _last.text.trim(),
          if (_phone.text.trim().isNotEmpty) 'phone': _phone.text.trim(),
          if (_city.text.trim().isNotEmpty) 'city': _city.text.trim(),
          if (_district.text.trim().isNotEmpty) 'district': _district.text.trim(),
          if (_year != null) 'birth_year': _year,
          if (_invite.text.trim().isNotEmpty) 'invite_code': _invite.text.trim(),
          'accept_terms': _accept,
        });
        if (!mounted) return;
        await _showCodes((res['recovery_codes'] as List).cast<String>());
      }
      await s.signIn(email, _pass.text);
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) showToast(context, errorMessage(e));
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
    }
  }

  void _next() {
    if (!_keys[_step].currentState!.validate()) return;
    if (_step < 2) {
      setState(() => _step++);
    } else if (!_accept) {
      showToast(context, "Acceptez les conditions d'utilisation pour continuer.");
    } else {
      _submit();
    }
  }

  Widget _gap([double h = 14]) => SizedBox(height: h);

  List<Widget> _loginFields() => [
        TextFormField(
          controller: _email,
          keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
          autocorrect: false,
          textInputAction: TextInputAction.next,
          decoration: fieldDecoration(context, 'E-mail', Icons.alternate_email_rounded),
          validator: (v) => (v == null || !v.contains('@')) ? 'E-mail invalide' : null,
        ),
        _gap(),
        TextFormField(
          controller: _pass,
          obscureText: _hide,
          onFieldSubmitted: (_) => _submit(),
          decoration: fieldDecoration(context, 'Mot de passe', Icons.lock_outline_rounded,
              suffix: IconButton(
                icon: Icon(_hide ? Icons.visibility_off_outlined : Icons.visibility_outlined),
                onPressed: () => setState(() => _hide = !_hide),
              )),
          validator: (v) => (v == null || v.isEmpty) ? 'Mot de passe requis' : null,
        ),
      ];

  List<Widget> _step0() {
    final st = passwordStrength(_pass.text);
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    return [
      TextFormField(
        controller: _email,
        keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
        autocorrect: false,
        decoration: fieldDecoration(context, 'E-mail', Icons.alternate_email_rounded),
        validator: (v) => (v == null || !v.contains('@') || !v.contains('.')) ? 'E-mail invalide' : null,
      ),
      _gap(),
      TextFormField(
        controller: _pass,
        obscureText: _hide,
        decoration: fieldDecoration(context, 'Mot de passe', Icons.lock_outline_rounded,
            suffix: IconButton(
              icon: Icon(_hide ? Icons.visibility_off_outlined : Icons.visibility_outlined),
              onPressed: () => setState(() => _hide = !_hide),
            )),
        validator: (v) => passwordPolicyOk(v ?? '')
            ? null
            : '10 caractères min., lettres + chiffres, et 3 types de caractères (ou 14 caractères)',
      ),
      const SizedBox(height: 8),
      ClipRRect(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4), child: LinearProgressIndicator(value: _pass.text.isEmpty ? 0 : st.value, minHeight: 6, color: st.color, backgroundColor: cs.outlineVariant.withValues(alpha: 0.4))),
      Align(alignment: Alignment.centerLeft, child: Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 4), child: Text(_pass.text.isEmpty ? 'Minuscules, majuscules, chiffres, symboles' : 'Force : ${st.label}', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: cs.onSurfaceVariant)))),
      _gap(),
      TextFormField(
        controller: _pass2,
        obscureText: _hide,
        decoration: fieldDecoration(context, 'Confirmer le mot de passe', Icons.lock_reset_rounded),
        validator: (v) => v == _pass.text ? null : 'Les mots de passe ne correspondent pas',
      ),
    ];
  }

  List<Widget> _step1() => [
        TextFormField(
          controller: _name,
          maxLength: 24,
          decoration: fieldDecoration(context, 'Pseudo public (classement)', Icons.badge_outlined),
          validator: (v) => RegExp(r"^[\w .'-]{3,24}$").hasMatch((v ?? '').trim()) ? null : '3 à 24 caractères (lettres, chiffres, . - \' _)',
        ),
        Row(children: [
          Expanded(child: TextFormField(controller: _first, decoration: fieldDecoration(context, 'Prénom (facultatif)', Icons.person_outline_rounded))),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          Expanded(child: TextFormField(controller: _last, decoration: fieldDecoration(context, 'Nom (facultatif)', Icons.person_outline_rounded))),
        ]),
        _gap(),
        TextFormField(
          controller: _phone,
          keyboardType: TextInputType.phone,
          decoration: fieldDecoration(context, 'Téléphone (facultatif)', Icons.phone_outlined),
          validator: (v) => (v == null || v.trim().isEmpty || RegExp(r'^\+?[0-9 ()-]{6,20}$').hasMatch(v.trim())) ? null : 'Numéro invalide',
        ),
        _gap(),
        Row(children: [
          Expanded(child: TextFormField(controller: _city, decoration: fieldDecoration(context, 'Ville', Icons.location_city_rounded))),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          Expanded(child: TextFormField(controller: _district, decoration: fieldDecoration(context, 'Quartier', Icons.holiday_village_outlined))),
        ]),
        _gap(),
        DropdownButtonFormField<int>(
          initialValue: _year,
          decoration: fieldDecoration(context, 'Année de naissance (facultatif)', Icons.cake_outlined),
          items: [for (var y = DateTime.now().year - 13; y >= 1930; y--) DropdownMenuItem(value: y, child: Text('$y'))],
          onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _year = v),
        ),
      ];

  List<Widget> _step2() => [
        TextFormField(
          controller: _invite,
          textCapitalization: TextCapitalization.characters,
          decoration: fieldDecoration(context, "Code d'invitation (si vous en avez un)", Icons.card_giftcard_rounded),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        Text("L'invitation peut être obligatoire selon l'organisation. Un code d'ami vous rattache à lui : il gagne des points quand vous devenez actif.",
            style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant)),
        _gap(),
        CheckboxListTile(
          contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
          value: _accept,
          onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _accept = v ?? false),
          controlAffinity: ListTileControlAffinity.leading,
          title: const Text(
              "J'accepte les conditions d'utilisation. Mon e-mail n'est conservé que sous forme chiffrée ; mon nom et mon téléphone restent privés ; seuls des niveaux en dB(A) (jamais l'audio) sont envoyés.",
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13)),
        ),
      ];

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    const titles = ['Votre compte', 'Votre profil', 'Invitation et conditions'];
    return Scaffold(
      body: SingleChildScrollView(
        child: Column(children: [
          Container(
            width: double.infinity,
            padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(24, MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top + 28, 24, 28),
            decoration: const BoxDecoration(
              gradient: LinearGradient(colors: [Color(0xFF0B1F2A), Color(0xFF0E5E63)], begin: Alignment.topLeft, end: Alignment.bottomRight),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(bottom: Radius.circular(36)),
            ),
            child: Column(children: [
              const SunuLogo(size: 72),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              const Text('SunuSound', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 26, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
              const SizedBox(height: 4),
              Text('Mesurez, cartographiez, comprenez le bruit autour de vous.', textAlign: TextAlign.center, style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.75))),
            ]),
          ),
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
            child: Column(children: [
              SegmentedButton<bool>(
                segments: const [ButtonSegment(value: false, label: Text('Connexion')), ButtonSegment(value: true, label: Text('Inscription'))],
                selected: {_register},
                onSelectionChanged: (v) => setState(() {
                  _register = v.first;
                  _step = 0;
                }),
                showSelectedIcon: false,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 20),
              if (_register) ...[
                Row(children: [
                  for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++) ...[
                    Expanded(child: Container(height: 4, decoration: BoxDecoration(color: i <= _step ? cs.primary : cs.outlineVariant, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2)))),
                    if (i < 2) const SizedBox(width: 6),
                  ]
                ]),
                const SizedBox(height: 10),
                Align(alignment: Alignment.centerLeft, child: Text('Étape ${_step + 1}/3 · ${titles[_step]}', style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 16))),
                const SizedBox(height: 14),
                Form(key: _keys[_step], child: Column(children: [if (_step == 0) ..._step0() else if (_step == 1) ..._step1() else ..._step2()])),
              ] else
                Form(key: _keys[0], child: Column(children: _loginFields())),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              if (!_register)
                Align(
                  alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
                  child: TextButton(
                    onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).push(MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => ResetPasswordScreen(email: _email.text.trim()))),
                    child: const Text('Mot de passe oublié ?'),
                  ),
                ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              Row(children: [
                if (_register && _step > 0) ...[
                  Expanded(child: OutlinedButton(onPressed: _busy ? null : () => setState(() => _step--), child: const Text('Retour'))),
                  const SizedBox(width: 12),
                ],
                Expanded(
                  flex: 2,
                  child: FilledButton(
                    onPressed: _busy
                        ? null
                        : () {
                            if (!_register) {
                              if (_keys[0].currentState!.validate()) _submit();
                            } else {
                              _next();
                            }
                          },
                    child: _busy
                        ? const SizedBox(height: 22, width: 22, child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2.5))
                        : Text(!_register ? 'Se connecter' : (_step < 2 ? 'Suivant' : 'Créer mon compte')),
                  ),
                ),
              ]),
            ]),
          ),
        ]),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class ResetPasswordScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const ResetPasswordScreen({super.key, this.email = ''});
  final String email;
  @override
  State<ResetPasswordScreen> createState() => _ResetPasswordScreenState();
}

class _ResetPasswordScreenState extends State<ResetPasswordScreen> {
  final _form = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  late final _email = TextEditingController(text: widget.email);
  final _code = TextEditingController();
  final _pass = TextEditingController();
  bool _busy = false;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _email.dispose();
    _code.dispose();
    _pass.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _go() async {
    if (!_form.currentState!.validate()) return;
    setState(() => _busy = true);
    try {
      await context.read<ApiClient>().resetPassword(_email.text.trim(), _code.text.trim(), _pass.text);
      if (!mounted) return;
      showToast(context, 'Mot de passe modifié. Connectez-vous.');
      Navigator.pop(context);
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) showToast(context, errorMessage(e));
    }
    if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('Mot de passe oublié')),
      body: ListView(padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24), children: [
        Text('Saisissez un de vos codes de récupération (remis à l\'inscription). Chaque code ne sert qu\'une fois.', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant)),
        const SizedBox(height: 18),
        Form(
          key: _form,
          child: Column(children: [
            TextFormField(controller: _email, keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress, decoration: fieldDecoration(context, 'E-mail', Icons.alternate_email_rounded), validator: (v) => (v == null || !v.contains('@')) ? 'E-mail invalide' : null),
            const SizedBox(height: 14),
            TextFormField(controller: _code, textCapitalization: TextCapitalization.characters, decoration: fieldDecoration(context, 'Code de récupération (XXXXX-XXXXX)', Icons.key_rounded), validator: (v) => (v == null || v.trim().length < 8) ? 'Code invalide' : null),
            const SizedBox(height: 14),
            TextFormField(controller: _pass, obscureText: true, decoration: fieldDecoration(context, 'Nouveau mot de passe', Icons.lock_outline_rounded), validator: (v) => passwordPolicyOk(v ?? '') ? null : '10 caractères min., lettres + chiffres, 3 types (ou 14 caractères)'),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            FilledButton(onPressed: _busy ? null : _go, child: _busy ? const SizedBox(height: 22, width: 22, child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2.5)) : const Text('Réinitialiser')),
          ]),
        ),
      ]),
    );
  }
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: ui/cell_sheet.dart
################################################################################

import 'package:fl_chart/fl_chart.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

import '../core/api.dart';
import '../core/models.dart';
import '../core/util.dart';
import 'widgets.dart';

class CellSheet extends StatefulWidget {
  const CellSheet({super.key, required this.cell, required this.filters, this.store});
  final NoiseCell cell;
  final MapFilters filters;
  final dynamic store; // Optionnel

  @override
  State<CellSheet> createState() => _CellSheetState();
}

class _CellSheetState extends State<CellSheet> {
  late final Future<CellDetail> _f = context.read<ApiClient>().cellDetail(widget.cell.h3, widget.filters);
  late final Future<TrafficFlow?> _traffic = context.read<ApiClient>().cellTraffic(widget.cell.h3);

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = widget.cell;
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final col = noiseColor(c.laeq);
    return SafeArea(
      child: ListView(padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 0, 20, 24), shrinkWrap: true, children: [
        Row(children: [
          Expanded(
            child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
              Row(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end, children: [
                Text(c.laeq.toStringAsFixed(1),
                    style: TextStyle(fontSize: 52, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: col, height: 1)),
                const Padding(
                    padding: EdgeInsets.only(left: 6, bottom: 6),
                    child: Text('dB(A)', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700))),
              ]),
              const SizedBox(height: 6),
              StatusChip(noiseLabel(c.laeq), col, icon: Icons.graphic_eq_rounded),
            ]),
          ),
          Column(children: [
            ScoreRing(
              value: c.confidence,
              color: cs.primary,
              size: 72,
              stroke: 7,
              child: Text('${(c.confidence * 100).round()}%',
                  style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 4),
            Text('Confiance', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12)),
          ]),
        ]),
        const SizedBox(height: 18),
        Wrap(spacing: 8, runSpacing: 8, children: [
          _stat(context, Icons.vertical_align_top_rounded, 'Lmax', '${c.lmax.toStringAsFixed(1)} dB'),
          _stat(context, Icons.straighten_rounded, 'Mesures', '${c.count}'),
          _stat(context, Icons.people_alt_rounded, 'Contributeurs', '${c.devices}'),
          _stat(context, Icons.tune_rounded, 'Calibrées', '${(c.calibratedShare * 100).round()} %'),
          if (c.last != null) _stat(context, Icons.schedule_rounded, 'Dernière', fmtAgo(c.last!)),
        ]),
        FutureBuilder<TrafficFlow?>(
          future: _traffic,
          builder: (context, snap) {
            final t = snap.data;
            if (t == null) return const SizedBox.shrink();
            final col = switch (t.level) {
              'free' => const Color(0xFF22C55E),
              'moderate' => const Color(0xFFF59E0B),
              _ => const Color(0xFFEF4444),
            };
            return Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 12),
              child: Row(children: [
                Icon(Icons.traffic_rounded, color: col),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                Text('Trafic en ce moment : ', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 13)),
                StatusChip('${t.levelLabel} · ${t.current.round()}/${t.freeFlow.round()} km/h', col),
              ]),
            );
          },
        ),
        FutureBuilder<CellDetail>(
          future: _f,
          builder: (context, snap) {
            if (snap.connectionState != ConnectionState.done) {
              return const Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(32), child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()));
            }
            if (snap.hasError) {
              return Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 24),
                child: Text(errorMessage(snap.error!), style: TextStyle(color: cs.error)),
              );
            }
            final d = snap.data!;
            return Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
              if (d.context.isNotEmpty) ...[
                const SectionTitle('Contexte sonore déclaré'),
                Wrap(spacing: 8, runSpacing: 8, children: [
                  for (final e in d.context.entries)
                    if (NoiseTagX.parse(e.key) != null) NoiseTagChip(NoiseTagX.parse(e.key)!, count: e.value),
                ]),
              ],
              if (d.byDayPart.isNotEmpty) ...[
                const SectionTitle('Par plage horaire'),
                SizedBox(height: 170, child: _DayPartChart(d)),
              ],
              if (d.daily.length > 1) ...[
                const SectionTitle('Évolution journalière'),
                SizedBox(height: 190, child: _DailyChart(d)),
              ],
            ]);
          },
        ),
      ]),
    );
  }

  Widget _stat(BuildContext context, IconData i, String l, String v) {
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 8),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(color: cs.surfaceContainerHighest, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
      child: Row(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
        Icon(i, size: 16, color: cs.primary),
        const SizedBox(width: 6),
        Text('$l ', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12)),
        Text(v, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 13)),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

class _DayPartChart extends StatelessWidget {
  const _DayPartChart(this.d);
  final CellDetail d;
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    const order = ['morning', 'afternoon', 'evening', 'night'];
    final byPart = {for (final p in d.byDayPart) p.part: p};
    final maxY = (d.byDayPart.map((e) => e.laeq).reduce((a, b) => a > b ? a : b) + 10).ceilToDouble();
    return BarChart(BarChartData(
      maxY: maxY,
      minY: 0,
      gridData: const FlGridData(show: false),
      borderData: FlBorderData(show: false),
      titlesData: FlTitlesData(
        topTitles: const AxisTitles(sideTitles: SideTitles(showTitles: false)),
        rightTitles: const AxisTitles(sideTitles: SideTitles(showTitles: false)),
        leftTitles: const AxisTitles(sideTitles: SideTitles(showTitles: false)),
        bottomTitles: AxisTitles(
          sideTitles: SideTitles(
            showTitles: true,
            reservedSize: 26,
            getTitlesWidget: (v, meta) => Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 6),
              child: Text(dayPartLabel(order[v.toInt()]), style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11)),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
      barGroups: [
        for (var i = 0; i < order.length; i++)
          BarChartGroupData(x: i, barRods: [
            BarChartRodData(
              toY: byPart[order[i]]?.laeq ?? 0,
              width: 28,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
              color: byPart[order[i]] == null ? Colors.transparent : noiseColor(byPart[order[i]]!.laeq),
            ),
          ], showingTooltipIndicators: byPart[order[i]] == null ? [] : [0]),
      ],
      barTouchData: BarTouchData(
        enabled: false,
        touchTooltipData: BarTouchTooltipData(
          getTooltipColor: (_) => Colors.transparent,
          tooltipPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
          getTooltipItem: (g, gi, rod, ri) => BarTooltipItem(
            rod.toY.toStringAsFixed(0),
            TextStyle(color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurface, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 12),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    ));
  }
}

class _DailyChart extends StatelessWidget {
  const _DailyChart(this.d);
  final CellDetail d;
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final spots = [for (var i = 0; i < d.daily.length; i++) FlSpot(i.toDouble(), d.daily[i].laeq)];
    final vals = d.daily.map((e) => e.laeq);
    final minY = (vals.reduce((a, b) => a < b ? a : b) - 8).floorToDouble();
    final maxY = (vals.reduce((a, b) => a > b ? a : b) + 8).ceilToDouble();
    final step = (d.daily.length / 4).ceil().clamp(1, 100);
    return LineChart(LineChartData(
      minY: minY < 0 ? 0 : minY,
      maxY: maxY,
      gridData: FlGridData(
          drawVerticalLine: false,
          getDrawingHorizontalLine: (_) => FlLine(color: cs.outlineVariant.withValues(alpha: 0.4), strokeWidth: 1)),
      borderData: FlBorderData(show: false),
      titlesData: FlTitlesData(
        topTitles: const AxisTitles(sideTitles: SideTitles(showTitles: false)),
        rightTitles: const AxisTitles(sideTitles: SideTitles(showTitles: false)),
        leftTitles: AxisTitles(
          sideTitles: SideTitles(
            showTitles: true,
            reservedSize: 34,
            getTitlesWidget: (v, meta) => Text(v.toStringAsFixed(0), style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 10)),
          ),
        ),
        bottomTitles: AxisTitles(
          sideTitles: SideTitles(
            showTitles: true,
            interval: 1,
            reservedSize: 24,
            getTitlesWidget: (v, meta) {
              final i = v.toInt();
              if (i < 0 || i >= d.daily.length || i % step != 0) return const SizedBox.shrink();
              return Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 6),
                child: Text(fmtDay(d.daily[i].date), style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 10)),
              );
            },
          ),
        ),
      ),
      lineBarsData: [
        LineChartBarData(
          spots: spots,
          isCurved: true,
          barWidth: 3,
          color: cs.primary,
          dotData: const FlDotData(show: true),
          belowBarData: BarAreaData(show: true, color: cs.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.12)),
        ),
      ],
    ));
  }
}
