# FICHIER: ui/consent_screen.dart
################################################################################

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

import '../core/util.dart';
import '../state/session_controller.dart';
import 'widgets.dart';

class ConsentScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const ConsentScreen({super.key});
  @override
  State<ConsentScreen> createState() => _ConsentScreenState();
}

class _ConsentScreenState extends State<ConsentScreen> {
  bool _geo = true, _mic = true, _busy = false;

  Future<void> _save() async {
    final s = context.read<SessionController>();
    setState(() => _busy = true);
    try {
      await s.saveConsents(geolocation: _geo, microphone: _mic);
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) showToast(context, errorMessage(e));
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    return Scaffold(
      body: SafeArea(
        child: ListView(padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24), children: [
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Icon(Icons.verified_user_rounded, size: 48, color: cs.primary),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          Text('Vos données, votre choix',
              style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall?.copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          Text(
            "Pour contribuer à la carte sonore, l'application a besoin de deux autorisations. "
            "Le son est analysé sur votre téléphone : seuls des niveaux en dB(A) sont envoyés, jamais l'audio.",
            style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, height: 1.4),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          SoftCard(
            child: SwitchListTile(
              contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
              secondary: const Icon(Icons.place_rounded),
              title: const Text('Géolocalisation', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
              subtitle: const Text('Situer la mesure dans une zone de la carte.'),
              value: _geo,
              onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _geo = v),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          SoftCard(
            child: SwitchListTile(
              contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
              secondary: const Icon(Icons.mic_rounded),
              title: const Text('Microphone', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
              subtitle: const Text('Mesurer le niveau sonore pendant quelques secondes.'),
              value: _mic,
              onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _mic = v),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 28),
          FilledButton(
            onPressed: _busy ? null : _save,
            child: _busy
                ? const SizedBox(height: 22, width: 22, child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2.5))
                : const Text('Continuer'),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => context.read<SessionController>().skipConsents(),
            child: const Text('Plus tard (consultation de la carte uniquement)'),
          ),
        ]),
      ),
    );
  }
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: ui/guide_screen.dart
################################################################################

import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

import '../core/api.dart';
import '../core/api_ext.dart';
import '../core/util.dart';
import '../state/session_controller.dart';
import 'widgets.dart';

IconData guideIcon(String? n) => switch (n) {
      'waving_hand' => Icons.waving_hand_rounded,
      'mic' => Icons.mic_rounded,
      'map' => Icons.map_rounded,
      'route' => Icons.add_road_rounded,
      'verified' => Icons.verified_rounded,
      'calculate' => Icons.calculate_rounded,
      'bolt' => Icons.bolt_rounded,
      'auto_awesome' => Icons.auto_awesome_rounded,
      'flag' => Icons.flag_rounded,
      'hearing' => Icons.hearing_rounded,
      'emoji_events' => Icons.emoji_events_rounded,
      'lock' => Icons.lock_rounded,
      'person' => Icons.person_rounded,
      'admin' => Icons.admin_panel_settings_rounded,
      'help' => Icons.help_rounded,
      _ => Icons.menu_book_rounded,
    };

Color? _hex(dynamic v) {
  if (v is! String || !v.startsWith('#') || v.length != 7) return null;
  final n = int.tryParse(v.substring(1), radix: 16);
  return n == null ? null : Color(0xFF000000 | n);
}

class GuideScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const GuideScreen({super.key, this.section});
  final String? section;
  @override
  State<GuideScreen> createState() => _GuideScreenState();
}

class _GuideScreenState extends State<GuideScreen> {
  final _scroll = ScrollController();
  final _q = TextEditingController();
  final Map<String, GlobalKey> _keys = {};
  Map<String, dynamic>? _doc;
  bool _loading = true, _offline = false;
  String? _error;
  String _query = '';

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _load();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _scroll.dispose();
    _q.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _load() async {
    setState(() => _loading = true);
    final prefs = context.read<SessionController>().prefs;
    try {
      final d = await context.read<ApiClient>().guide();
      await prefs.setString('guide_cache', jsonEncode(d));
      _doc = d;
      _offline = false;
      _error = null;
    } catch (e) {
      final raw = prefs.getString('guide_cache');
      if (raw != null) {
        _doc = (jsonDecode(raw) as Map).cast<String, dynamic>();
        _offline = true;
      } else {
        _error = errorMessage(e);
      }
    }
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() => _loading = false);
    if (widget.section != null) WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) => _jump(widget.section!));
  }

  void _jump(String id) {
    final c = _keys[id]?.currentContext;
    if (c != null) Scrollable.ensureVisible(c, duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 450), curve: Curves.easeOutCubic, alignment: 0.02);
  }

  List<Map<String, dynamic>> get _sections => ((_doc?['sections'] as List?) ?? const []).cast<Map<String, dynamic>>();

  static void _collect(dynamic v, StringBuffer b) {
    if (v is String) {
      b.write('${v.toLowerCase()} ');
    } else if (v is List) {
      for (final x in v) {
        _collect(x, b);
      }
    } else if (v is Map) {
      for (final e in v.entries) {
        if (e.key == 'type' || e.key == 'color' || e.key == 'icon') continue;
        _collect(e.value, b);
      }
    }
  }

  bool _match(Map<String, dynamic> s) {
    if (_query.isEmpty) return true;
    final b = StringBuffer();
    _collect(s, b);
    return b.toString().contains(_query);
  }

  Widget _rich(String t, {TextStyle? style}) {
    final parts = t.split('**');
    return Text.rich(
      TextSpan(children: [
        for (var i = 0; i < parts.length; i++) TextSpan(text: parts[i], style: i.isOdd ? const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800) : null),
      ]),
      style: style ?? const TextStyle(height: 1.5, fontSize: 14),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final secs = _sections.where(_match).toList();
    return Scaffold(
      body: _loading && _doc == null
          ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
          : _doc == null
              ? Center(
                  child: Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(32),
                    child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
                      Icon(Icons.cloud_off_rounded, size: 44, color: cs.error),
                      const SizedBox(height: 10),
                      Text(_error ?? 'Guide indisponible', textAlign: TextAlign.center),
                      const SizedBox(height: 14),
                      FilledButton(onPressed: _load, child: const Text('Réessayer')),
                    ]),
                  ),
                )
              : RefreshIndicator(
                  onRefresh: _load,
                  child: CustomScrollView(controller: _scroll, physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(), slivers: [
                    SliverToBoxAdapter(child: _hero(context, cs)),
                    SliverToBoxAdapter(child: _toc(cs)),
                    if (_query.isNotEmpty)
                      SliverToBoxAdapter(
                        child: Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 6, 20, 0),
                          child: Text('${secs.length} section(s) pour « $_query »', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant)),
                        ),
                      ),
                    SliverPadding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 12, 16, 60),
                      sliver: SliverList.builder(
                        itemCount: secs.length + 1,
                        itemBuilder: (_, i) => i == secs.length ? _footer(cs) : _section(secs[i], cs),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ]),
                ),
    );
  }

  Widget _hero(BuildContext context, ColorScheme cs) {
    return Container(
      padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(8, MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top + 4, 16, 22),
      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
        gradient: LinearGradient(colors: [Color(0xFF0B1F2A), Color(0xFF0E5E63)], begin: Alignment.topLeft, end: Alignment.bottomRight),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(bottom: Radius.circular(32)),
      ),
      child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
        Row(children: [
          IconButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context), icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back_rounded, color: Colors.white)),
          const Spacer(),
          if (_offline) const StatusChip('Hors-ligne', Color(0xFFF59E0B), icon: Icons.cloud_off_rounded),
          IconButton(onPressed: _load, icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh_rounded, color: Colors.white)),
        ]),
        Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 12),
          child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
            const Row(children: [SunuLogo(size: 36), SizedBox(width: 10), Text('Guide', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 16))]),
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            Text((_doc!['title'] as String?) ?? 'Guide', style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 28, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, letterSpacing: -0.5)),
            const SizedBox(height: 4),
            Text((_doc!['subtitle'] as String?) ?? '', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.78), height: 1.35)),
          ]),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 8),
          child: TextField(
            controller: _q,
            onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _query = v.trim().toLowerCase()),
            style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white),
            cursorColor: Colors.white,
            decoration: InputDecoration(
              hintText: 'Rechercher : crédits, mission, GPS, routes…',
              hintStyle: TextStyle(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.6)),
              prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search_rounded, color: Colors.white70),
              suffixIcon: _query.isEmpty ? null : IconButton(icon: const Icon(Icons.close_rounded, color: Colors.white70), onPressed: () => setState(() { _q.clear(); _query = ''; })),
              filled: true,
              fillColor: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.14),
              border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18), borderSide: BorderSide.none),
            ),
          ),
        ),
        if ((_doc!['note'] as String?) != null)
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(12, 10, 0, 0),
            child: Row(children: [
              const Icon(Icons.circle, size: 8, color: Color(0xFF22C55E)),
              const SizedBox(width: 6),
              Expanded(child: Text(_doc!['note'] as String, style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.7), fontSize: 11))),
            ]),
          ),
      ]),
    );
  }

  Widget _toc(ColorScheme cs) {
    return SizedBox(
      height: 54,
      child: ListView(scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal, padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 10, 16, 6), children: [
        for (final s in _sections)
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 8),
            child: ActionChip(
              avatar: Icon(guideIcon(s['icon'] as String?), size: 16, color: _hex(s['color']) ?? cs.primary),
              label: Text(s['title'] as String, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
              onPressed: () {
                if (_query.isNotEmpty) setState(() { _q.clear(); _query = ''; });
                WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) => _jump(s['id'] as String));
              },
            ),
          ),
      ]),
    );
  }

  Widget _footer(ColorScheme cs) => Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8),
        child: Text('Mis à jour par le serveur · ${_doc!['generated_at'] ?? ''}'.split('.').first.replaceAll('T', ' '),
            textAlign: TextAlign.center, style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 11)),
      );

  Widget _section(Map<String, dynamic> s, ColorScheme cs) {
    final color = _hex(s['color']) ?? cs.primary;
    final id = s['id'] as String;
    final blocks = (s['blocks'] as List).cast<Map<String, dynamic>>();
    return Container(
      key: _keys.putIfAbsent(id, () => GlobalKey()),
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 16),
      child: SoftCard(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 16, 16, 12),
        child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
          Row(children: [
            Container(
              width: 46,
              height: 46,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.15), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(15)),
              child: Icon(guideIcon(s['icon'] as String?), color: color),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 12),
            Expanded(
              child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
                Text(s['title'] as String, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 18, letterSpacing: -0.2)),
                Text(s['summary'] as String, style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12.5, height: 1.3)),
              ]),
            ),
          ]),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          for (final b in blocks) _block(b, color, cs),
        ]),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _block(Map<String, dynamic> b, Color color, ColorScheme cs) {
    switch (b['type']) {
      case 'h':
        return Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 14, bottom: 6), child: Text(b['text'] as String, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 15)));
      case 'p':
        return Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8), child: _rich(b['text'] as String));
      case 'list':
        return Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
          child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
            for (final t in (b['items'] as List).cast<String>())
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 3),
                child: Row(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
                  Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 7, right: 10), child: Icon(Icons.circle, size: 6, color: color)),
                  Expanded(child: _rich(t)),
                ]),
              ),
          ]),
        );
      case 'steps':
        final items = (b['items'] as List).cast<Map<String, dynamic>>();
        return Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
          child: Column(children: [
            for (var i = 0; i < items.length; i++)
              IntrinsicHeight(
                child: Row(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch, children: [
                  SizedBox(
                    width: 34,
                    child: Column(children: [
                      CircleAvatar(radius: 13, backgroundColor: color, child: Text('${i + 1}', style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 12))),
                      if (i < items.length - 1) Expanded(child: Container(width: 2, margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 3), color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.25))),
                    ]),
                  ),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12, left: 4),
                      child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
                        Text(items[i]['title'] as String, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
                        const SizedBox(height: 2),
                        _rich(items[i]['text'] as String, style: TextStyle(height: 1.45, fontSize: 13.5, color: cs.onSurfaceVariant)),
                      ]),
                    ),
                  ),
                ]),
              ),
          ]),
        );
      case 'facts':
        final items = (b['items'] as List).cast<Map<String, dynamic>>();
        return Container(
          margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(color: cs.surfaceContainerHighest.withValues(alpha: 0.6), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
          child: Column(children: [
            for (var i = 0; i < items.length; i++) ...[
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 10),
                child: Row(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
                  Expanded(
                    flex: 5,
                    child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
                      Text(items[i]['label'] as String, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w600, fontSize: 13)),
                      if (items[i]['hint'] != null) Text(items[i]['hint'] as String, style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 11.5)),
                    ]),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 10),
                  Expanded(
                    flex: 5,
                    child: Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.end, crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
                      if (items[i]['live'] == true) const Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: 5, right: 6), child: Icon(Icons.circle, size: 7, color: Color(0xFF22C55E))),
                      Flexible(child: Text(items[i]['value'] as String, textAlign: TextAlign.right, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 13, color: color))),
                    ]),
                  ),
                ]),
              ),
              if (i < items.length - 1) Divider(height: 1, color: cs.outlineVariant.withValues(alpha: 0.5)),
            ],
          ]),
        );
      case 'callout':
        final tone = b['tone'] as String? ?? 'info';
        final (c, ic) = switch (tone) {
          'tip' => (const Color(0xFF16A34A), Icons.lightbulb_rounded),
          'warn' => (const Color(0xFFD97706), Icons.warning_amber_rounded),
          _ => (const Color(0xFF2563EB), Icons.info_rounded),
        };
        return Container(
          margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 6),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(color: c.withValues(alpha: 0.10), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14), border: Border(left: BorderSide(color: c, width: 4))),
          child: Row(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
            Icon(ic, color: c, size: 20),
            const SizedBox(width: 10),
            Expanded(
              child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
                Text(b['title'] as String, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: c)),
                const SizedBox(height: 2),
                _rich(b['text'] as String, style: const TextStyle(height: 1.4, fontSize: 13)),
              ]),
            ),
          ]),
        );
      case 'example':
        return Container(
          margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 6),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.08), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16), border: Border.all(color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.35))),
          child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
            Row(children: [Icon(Icons.science_rounded, size: 16, color: color), const SizedBox(width: 6), Text(b['title'] as String, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: color))]),
            const SizedBox(height: 6),
            _rich(b['text'] as String, style: const TextStyle(height: 1.45, fontSize: 13.5)),
          ]),
        );
      case 'scale':
        return Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
          child: Column(children: [
            for (final r in (b['items'] as List).cast<Map<String, dynamic>>())
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4),
                child: Row(children: [
                  Container(width: 14, height: 34, decoration: BoxDecoration(color: _hex(r['color']), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(5))),
                  const SizedBox(width: 12),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
                      Row(children: [Text(r['label'] as String, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)), const SizedBox(width: 8), Text(r['range'] as String, style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12))]),
                      Text(r['hint'] as String, style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12)),
                    ]),
                  ),
                ]),
              ),
          ]),
        );
      case 'faq':
        final items = (b['items'] as List).cast<Map<String, dynamic>>().where((x) {
          if (_query.isEmpty) return true;
          return '${x['q']} ${x['a']}'.toLowerCase().contains(_query);
        });
        return Theme(
          data: Theme.of(context).copyWith(dividerColor: Colors.transparent),
          child: Column(children: [
            for (final x in items)
              Container(
                margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 6),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(color: cs.surfaceContainerHighest.withValues(alpha: 0.6), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
                child: ExpansionTile(
                  initiallyExpanded: _query.isNotEmpty,
                  tilePadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14),
                  childrenPadding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(14, 0, 14, 12),
                  expandedCrossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  title: Text(x['q'] as String, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 14)),
                  children: [_rich(x['a'] as String)],
                ),
              ),
          ]),
        );
      default:
        return const SizedBox.shrink();
    }
  }
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: ui/history_screen.dart
################################################################################

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

import '../core/models.dart';
import '../core/util.dart';
import '../state/recorder_controller.dart';
import '../state/view_models.dart';
import 'widgets.dart';

class HistoryScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const HistoryScreen({super.key});
  @override
  State<HistoryScreen> createState() => _HistoryScreenState();
}

class _HistoryScreenState extends State<HistoryScreen> {
  final _scroll = ScrollController();
  int _seen = -1;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _scroll.addListener(() {
      if (_scroll.position.pixels > _scroll.position.maxScrollExtent - 300) {
        context.read<HistoryViewModel>().loadMore();
      }
    });
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) => context.read<HistoryViewModel>().reload());
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _scroll.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final vm = context.watch<HistoryViewModel>();
    final rec = context.watch<RecorderController>();
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;

    if (rec.completedVersion != _seen) {
      _seen = rec.completedVersion;
      WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
        if (mounted) context.read<HistoryViewModel>().reload();
      });
    }

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('Mes mesures')),
      body: RefreshIndicator(
        onRefresh: () async {
          await context.read<RecorderController>().flushPending();
          await vm.reload();
        },
        child: ListView(
          controller: _scroll,
          physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 4, 16, 120),
          children: [
            if (rec.pendingCount > 0)
              SoftCard(
                margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
                child: Row(children: [
                  const Icon(Icons.cloud_upload_outlined, color: Color(0xFFF59E0B)),
                  const SizedBox(width: 12),
                  Expanded(
                      child: Text('${rec.pendingCount} mesure(s) en attente d\'envoi',
                          style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700))),
                  TextButton(
                      onPressed: () => context.read<RecorderController>().flushPending(),
                      child: const Text('Envoyer')),
                ]),
              ),
            if (vm.stats != null) _SummaryCard(vm.stats!),
            const SectionTitle('Historique'),
            if (vm.error != null)
              SoftCard(child: Text(apiErrorLabel(vm.error!), style: TextStyle(color: cs.error))),
            if (vm.items.isEmpty && !vm.loading && vm.error == null)
              SoftCard(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(28),
                child: Column(children: [
                  Icon(Icons.graphic_eq_rounded, size: 40, color: cs.primary),
                  const SizedBox(height: 10),
                  const Text('Aucune mesure pour le moment', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Text('Appuyez sur « Mesurer » pour contribuer à la carte.',
                      textAlign: TextAlign.center, style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant)),
                ]),
              ),
            for (final it in vm.items) _MeasurementTile(it),
            if (vm.loading)
              const Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(20), child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _SummaryCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _SummaryCard(this.s);
  final Contributions s;
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    return SoftCard(
      child: Row(children: [
        ScoreRing(
          value: s.validationRate,
          color: qualityColor('validated'),
          size: 84,
          stroke: 9,
          child: Text('${(s.validationRate * 100).round()}%',
              style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 17)),
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 18),
        Expanded(
          child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
            const Text('Taux de validation', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
            const SizedBox(height: 2),
            Text('${s.total} mesure(s) · ${s.cellsCovered} zone(s)',
                style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant)),
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            Wrap(spacing: 6, runSpacing: 6, children: [
              for (final k in const ['validated', 'indicative', 'rejected', 'pending'])
                if (s.count(k) > 0) StatusChip('${qualityLabel(k)} ${s.count(k)}', qualityColor(k)),
            ]),
          ]),
        ),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

class _MeasurementTile extends StatelessWidget {
  const _MeasurementTile(this.it);
  final HistoryItem it;
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final col = noiseColor(it.laeq);
    final tag = NoiseTagX.parse(it.noiseContext);
    return SoftCard(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
      onTap: () => showModalBottomSheet(context: context, isScrollControlled: true, builder: (_) => _DetailSheet(it)),
      child: Row(children: [
        Container(
          width: 62,
          height: 62,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(color: col.withValues(alpha: 0.16), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18)),
          child: Column(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center, children: [
            Text(it.laeq.toStringAsFixed(0),
                style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 22, color: col, height: 1)),
            Text('dB(A)', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10, color: col, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
          ]),
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 14),
        Expanded(
          child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
            Text(fmtDateTime(it.measuredAt), style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
            const SizedBox(height: 2),
            Text('${dayPartLabel(it.dayPart)} · Lmax ${it.lmax.toStringAsFixed(0)}',
                style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12)),
            const SizedBox(height: 6),
            Wrap(spacing: 6, runSpacing: 4, children: [
              StatusChip(qualityLabel(it.qualityStatus), qualityColor(it.qualityStatus)),
              if (tag != null) Icon(tag.icon, size: 18, color: cs.primary),
            ]),
          ]),
        ),
        if (it.qualityScore != null)
          ScoreRing(
            value: it.qualityScore!,
            color: qualityColor(it.qualityStatus),
            size: 46,
            stroke: 5,
            child: Text('${(it.qualityScore! * 100).round()}', style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 12)),
          ),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

class _DetailSheet extends StatelessWidget {
  const _DetailSheet(this.it);
  final HistoryItem it;
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final col = noiseColor(it.laeq);
    return SafeArea(
      child: ListView(shrinkWrap: true, padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 0, 20, 24), children: [
        Row(children: [
          Text(it.laeq.toStringAsFixed(1), style: TextStyle(fontSize: 46, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: col)),
          const SizedBox(width: 6),
          const Text('dB(A)', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
          const Spacer(),
          StatusChip(qualityLabel(it.qualityStatus), qualityColor(it.qualityStatus)),
        ]),
        const SizedBox(height: 4),
        Text(fmtDateTime(it.measuredAt), style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant)),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        if (it.qualityScore != null)
          SoftCard(
            child: Row(children: [
              ScoreRing(
                value: it.qualityScore!,
                color: qualityColor(it.qualityStatus),
                size: 70,
                stroke: 8,
                child: Text('${(it.qualityScore! * 100).round()}%', style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              const Expanded(
                  child: Text('Score de qualité : fiabilité de cette mesure (GPS, durée, saturation, mouvement, calibration, cohérence avec les zones voisines).',
                      style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13))),
            ]),
          ),
        const SectionTitle('Détails'),
        _row('Lmax', '${it.lmax.toStringAsFixed(1)} dB(A)'),
        _row('Plage horaire', dayPartLabel(it.dayPart)),
        _row('Téléphone', it.calibrationStatus == 'calibrated' ? 'Calibré' : 'Non calibré'),
        _row('Position', it.locationAnonymized ? 'Arrondie au centre de la zone' : 'Précise (arrondie après la période de rétention)'),
        if (NoiseTagX.parse(it.noiseContext) != null)
          Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8),
              child: Align(alignment: Alignment.centerLeft, child: NoiseTagChip(NoiseTagX.parse(it.noiseContext)!))),
        if (it.reasons.isNotEmpty) ...[
          const SectionTitle('Pourquoi ce statut ?'),
          for (final code in it.reasons)
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
              child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
                Text('• ${reasonLabel(code)}', style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
                if (reasonTip(code) != null)
                  Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 12, top: 2),
                      child: Text(reasonTip(code)!, style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 13))),
              ]),
            ),
        ],
      ]),
    );
  }

  Widget _row(String k, String v) => Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 6),
        child: Row(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
          SizedBox(width: 110, child: Text(k, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w600))),
          Expanded(child: Text(v)),
        ]),
      );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: ui/home_shell.dart
################################################################################

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:connectivity_plus/connectivity_plus.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

import '../core/notifier.dart';
import '../state/proposals_controller.dart';
import '../state/recorder_controller.dart';
import 'history_screen.dart';
import 'map_screen.dart';
import 'profile_screen.dart';
import 'record_screen.dart';

class HomeShell extends StatefulWidget {
  const HomeShell({super.key});
  @override
  State<HomeShell> createState() => _HomeShellState();
}

class _HomeShellState extends State<HomeShell> with WidgetsBindingObserver {
  int _i = 0;
  StreamSubscription<List<ConnectivityResult>>? _conn;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addObserver(this);
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      context.read<Notifier>().requestPermission();
      final rec = context.read<RecorderController>();
      rec.refreshPending();
      rec.flushPending();
      context.read<ProposalsController>().startPolling();
    });
    _conn = Connectivity().onConnectivityChanged.listen((r) {
      if (!r.contains(ConnectivityResult.none) && mounted) {
        context.read<RecorderController>().flushPending();
      }
    });
  }

  @override
  void didChangeAppLifecycleState(AppLifecycleState state) {
    if (state == AppLifecycleState.resumed && mounted) {
      context.read<RecorderController>().flushPending();
      context.read<ProposalsController>().poll();
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    WidgetsBinding.instance.removeObserver(this);
    _conn?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _openRecord() {
    Navigator.of(context).push(PageRouteBuilder(
      pageBuilder: (_, __, ___) => const RecordScreen(),
      transitionsBuilder: (_, a, __, child) => FadeTransition(
          opacity: a,
          child: SlideTransition(
              position: Tween(begin: const Offset(0, 0.06), end: Offset.zero)
                  .animate(CurvedAnimation(parent: a, curve: Curves.easeOutCubic)),
              child: child)),
    ));
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      body: IndexedStack(index: _i, children: const [MapScreen(), HistoryScreen(), ProfileScreen()]),
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton.extended(
        onPressed: _openRecord,
        icon: const Icon(Icons.graphic_eq_rounded),
        label: const Text('Mesurer'),
      ),
      floatingActionButtonLocation: FloatingActionButtonLocation.centerFloat,
      bottomNavigationBar: NavigationBar(
        selectedIndex: _i,
        onDestinationSelected: (v) => setState(() => _i = v),
        destinations: const [
          NavigationDestination(
              icon: Icon(Icons.map_outlined), selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.map_rounded), label: 'Carte'),
          NavigationDestination(
              icon: Icon(Icons.history_rounded),
              selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.history_toggle_off_rounded),
              label: 'Mesures'),
          NavigationDestination(
              icon: Icon(Icons.person_outline_rounded),
              selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.person_rounded),
              label: 'Moi'),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: ui/leaderboard_screen.dart
################################################################################

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

import '../core/api.dart';
import '../core/api_ext.dart';
import '../core/models_social.dart';
import '../core/util.dart';
import 'widgets.dart';

// ---------- helpers partagés (utilisés par profile_screen.dart) ----------
Color levelColor(int index) => const [
      Color(0xFF94A3B8), Color(0xFF0E9F9A), Color(0xFF2563EB),
      Color(0xFF7C3AED), Color(0xFFF59E0B), Color(0xFFDB2777),
    ][index.clamp(0, 5)];

IconData badgeIcon(String icon) => switch (icon) {
      'mic' => Icons.mic_rounded,
      'streak' || 'fire' => Icons.local_fire_department_rounded,
      'star' => Icons.star_rounded,
      'trophy' => Icons.emoji_events_rounded,
      'compass' => Icons.explore_rounded,
      'map' => Icons.map_rounded,
      'shield' => Icons.shield_rounded,
      'verified' => Icons.verified_rounded,
      'hearing' => Icons.hearing_rounded,
      'road' => Icons.add_road_rounded,
      'target' => Icons.my_location_rounded,
      'award' => Icons.military_tech_rounded,
      'diamond' => Icons.diamond_rounded,
      _ => Icons.workspace_premium_rounded,
    };

class LevelAvatar extends StatelessWidget {
  const LevelAvatar(this.name, this.level, {super.key, this.radius = 24});
  final String name;
  final Level level;
  final double radius;
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final col = levelColor(level.index);
    return CircleAvatar(
      radius: radius,
      backgroundColor: col.withValues(alpha: 0.18),
      child: Text(
        name.isEmpty ? '?' : name.characters.first.toUpperCase(),
        style: TextStyle(color: col, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: radius * 0.8),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class LevelChip extends StatelessWidget {
  const LevelChip(this.level, {super.key});
  final Level level;
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final col = levelColor(level.index);
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 5),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(color: col.withValues(alpha: 0.15), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)),
      child: Row(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
        Icon(Icons.military_tech_rounded, size: 14, color: col),
        const SizedBox(width: 4),
        Text('Niv. ${level.index + 1} · ${level.name}',
            style: TextStyle(color: col, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 12)),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

// ---------- écran ----------
class LeaderboardScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const LeaderboardScreen({super.key});
  @override
  State<LeaderboardScreen> createState() => _LeaderboardScreenState();
}

class _LeaderboardScreenState extends State<LeaderboardScreen> {
  String _period = 'week';
  LeaderBoard? _board;
  bool _loading = true;
  String? _error;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _load();
  }

  Future<void> _load() async {
    setState(() { _loading = true; _error = null; });
    try {
      final b = await context.read<ApiClient>().leaderboard(_period);
      if (mounted) setState(() => _board = b);
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _error = errorMessage(e));
    }
    if (mounted) setState(() => _loading = false);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('Classement')),
      body: Column(children: [
        Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 8, 16, 4),
          child: SegmentedButton<String>(
            segments: const [
              ButtonSegment(value: 'week', label: Text('Semaine')),
              ButtonSegment(value: 'month', label: Text('Mois')),
              ButtonSegment(value: 'season', label: Text('Saison')),
            ],
            selected: {_period},
            onSelectionChanged: (v) {
              setState(() => _period = v.first);
              _load();
            },
            showSelectedIcon: false,
          ),
        ),
        if (_loading) const Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(24), child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
        if (_error != null) Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16), child: Text(_error!, style: TextStyle(color: cs.error))),
        if (!_loading && _board != null)
          Expanded(
            child: ListView(padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 8, 16, 24), children: [
              if (_board!.me != null) ...[
                SoftCard(
                  child: Row(children: [
                    LevelAvatar(_board!.me!.name, _board!.me!.level, radius: 26),
                    const SizedBox(width: 14),
                    Expanded(child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
                      Text('Moi · #${_board!.me!.rank} / ${_board!.total}', style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
                      Text('${_board!.me!.score.toStringAsFixed(0)} pts', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant)),
                    ])),
                  ]),
                ),
                const SectionTitle('Autour de moi'),
                for (final e in _board!.around) _entry(e, cs, highlight: e.isMe),
                SectionTitle('Top ${_board!.entries.length}'),
              ],
              for (final e in _board!.entries) _entry(e, cs, highlight: e.isMe),
            ]),
          ),
      ]),
    );
  }

  Widget _entry(LeaderEntry e, ColorScheme cs, {bool highlight = false}) {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: highlight ? cs.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.10) : cs.surfaceContainerHigh,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: highlight ? cs.primary : cs.outlineVariant.withValues(alpha: 0.4)),
      ),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
      child: Row(children: [
        SizedBox(width: 32, child: Text('#${e.rank}', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: e.rank <= 3 ? levelColor(e.rank - 1) : cs.onSurfaceVariant))),
        LevelAvatar(e.name, e.level, radius: 20),
        const SizedBox(width: 12),
        Expanded(child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
          Text(e.name, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
          Text('${e.validated} validées · ${e.missions} missions', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12)),
        ])),
        Text('${e.score.toStringAsFixed(0)} pts', style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================================
// Palette du bruit : bandes de 5 dB, plus foncé = plus bruyant
// ============================================================================
const _bandColors = <Color>[
  Color(0xFFBFDCE0), // < 40
  Color(0xFFCFE6CE), // 40-45
  Color(0xFFE4F0BE), // 45-50
  Color(0xFFF6E3A0), // 50-55
  Color(0xFFF2BD78), // 55-60
  Color(0xFFE8884F), // 60-65
  Color(0xFFCC4A3C), // 65-70
  Color(0xFF9E1B4C), // 70-75
  Color(0xFF6A0B63), // 75-80
  Color(0xFF3E0A4F), // >= 80
];

int noiseBandIndex(double db) => ((db - 35) / 5).floor().clamp(0, 9).toInt();
double noiseBandAlpha(double db) => 0.50 + 0.04 * noiseBandIndex(db);

/// Couleur d'un niveau : bande (fond clair) ou dégradé lumineux (fond sombre).
Color noiseBandColor(double db, {bool glow = false}) =>
    glow ? noiseHeat(db, glow: true) : _bandColors[noiseBandIndex(db)];

Color noiseHeat(double db, {bool glow = false}) {
  const stops = <double>[35, 50, 60, 70, 80, 90, 100];
  const onLight = <Color>[
    Color(0xFFFDE047),
    Color(0xFFFBBF24),
    Color(0xFFF97316),
    Color(0xFFDC2626),
    Color(0xFF9F1239),
    Color(0xFF4C0519),
    Color(0xFF1E0A2E),
  ];
  const onDark = <Color>[
    Color(0xFF164E63),
    Color(0xFF0891B2),
    Color(0xFF22D3EE),
    Color(0xFFFDE047),
    Color(0xFFFB923C),
    Color(0xFFF43F5E),
    Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
  ];
  final cols = glow ? onDark : onLight;
  if (db <= stops.first) return cols.first;
  if (db >= stops.last) return cols.last;
  for (var i = 0; i < stops.length - 1; i++) {
    if (db <= stops[i + 1]) {
      return Color.lerp(cols[i], cols[i + 1], (db - stops[i]) / (stops[i + 1] - stops[i]))!;
    }
  }
  return cols.last;
}

enum _NoiseMode { cloud, grid }

enum _Veil { none, temp, rain, cloud, soil }

// ============================================================================
// Voiles météo : champs continus interpolés
// ============================================================================
double? Function(WeatherPoint) _pick(_Veil v) => switch (v) {
      _Veil.temp => (WeatherPoint w) => w.temp,
      _Veil.rain => (WeatherPoint w) => w.precip,
      _Veil.cloud => (WeatherPoint w) => w.cloud.toDouble(),
      _Veil.soil => (WeatherPoint w) => w.soil,
      _Veil.none => (WeatherPoint w) => null,
    };

Color _rainRamp(double t) => Color.lerp(const Color(0xFF7DD3FC), const Color(0xFF4F46E5), t)!;

Color? _veilColor(_Veil v, double x, bool dark) {
  switch (v) {
    case _Veil.temp:
    case _Veil.soil:
      return tempColor(x).withValues(alpha: 0.52);
    case _Veil.rain:
      if (x < 0.05) return null;
      final t = (log(1 + x) / log(21)).clamp(0.0, 1.0).toDouble();
      return _rainRamp(t).withValues(alpha: 0.22 + 0.55 * t);
    case _Veil.cloud:
      final t = (x / 100).clamp(0.0, 1.0).toDouble();
      if (t < 0.08) return null;
      final base = dark ? const Color(0xFFE2E8F0) : const Color(0xFF475569);
      return base.withValues(alpha: 0.6 * pow(t, 1.3).toDouble());
    case _Veil.none:
      return null;
  }
}

({String title, List<Color> colors, String lo, String hi}) _veilInfo(_Veil v) => switch (v) {
      _Veil.temp => (
          title: 'Température',
          colors: [for (final t in const [10.0, 18.0, 26.0, 34.0, 42.0]) tempColor(t)],
          lo: '10°',
          hi: '42°C'
        ),
      _Veil.soil => (
          title: 'Température du sol',
          colors: [for (final t in const [10.0, 18.0, 26.0, 34.0, 42.0]) tempColor(t)],
          lo: '10°',
          hi: '42°C'
        ),
      _Veil.rain => (
          title: 'Précipitations',
          colors: [_rainRamp(0), _rainRamp(0.5), _rainRamp(1)],
          lo: '0',
          hi: '20 mm'
        ),
      _Veil.cloud => (
          title: 'Couverture nuageuse',
          colors: [const Color(0xFFE2E8F0), const Color(0xFF475569)],
          lo: '0',
          hi: '100 %'
        ),
      _Veil.none => (title: '', colors: const [Colors.transparent, Colors.transparent], lo: '', hi: ''),
    };

String _veilValue(_Veil v, double x) => switch (v) {
      _Veil.rain => '${x.toStringAsFixed(1)} mm',
      _Veil.cloud => '${x.round()} %',
      _ => '${x.round()} °C',
    };

class _WxField {
  _WxField(this.pts, this.area, this.cols, this.rows);
  final List<WeatherPoint> pts;
  final LatLngBounds area;
  final int cols, rows;

  bool get _lattice => pts.length == cols * rows;

  double? at(double lat, double lon, double? Function(WeatherPoint) pick) {
    if (pts.isEmpty) return null;
    if (_lattice) {
      final fx = ((lon - area.west) / (area.east - area.west) * cols - 0.5).clamp(0.0, cols - 1.0).toDouble();
      final fy = ((lat - area.south) / (area.north - area.south) * rows - 0.5).clamp(0.0, rows - 1.0).toDouble();
      final x0 = fx.floor(), y0 = fy.floor();
      final x1 = min(x0 + 1, cols - 1), y1 = min(y0 + 1, rows - 1);
      final tx = fx - x0, ty = fy - y0;
      final v00 = pick(pts[y0 * cols + x0]), v10 = pick(pts[y0 * cols + x1]);
      final v01 = pick(pts[y1 * cols + x0]), v11 = pick(pts[y1 * cols + x1]);
      if (v00 == null || v10 == null || v01 == null || v11 == null) return null;
      return (v00 * (1 - tx) + v10 * tx) * (1 - ty) + (v01 * (1 - tx) + v11 * tx) * ty;
    }
    var sum = 0.0, wsum = 0.0;
    for (final w in pts) {
      final v = pick(w);
      if (v == null) continue;
      final dx = lon - w.pos.longitude, dy = lat - w.pos.latitude;
      final k = 1 / (dx * dx + dy * dy + 1e-6);
      sum += v * k;
      wsum += k;
    }
    return wsum == 0 ? null : sum / wsum;
  }
}

// ============================================================================
// Simulation du flux (particules portées par le vent du serveur)
// ============================================================================
class _Src {
  _Src(this.lat, this.lon, this.db, this.rM);
  final double lat, lon, db, rM;
}

class _WindSample {
  _WindSample(this.lat, this.lon, this.u, this.v);
  final double lat, lon, u, v;
}

class _Particle {
  double lat = 0, lon = 0, age = 0, life = 1, db = 50, u = 0, v = 0;
  bool alive = false;
}

class _FlowSim {
  /// 1 seconde réelle = 45 secondes simulées (sinon le mouvement serait invisible).
  static const double timeLapse = 45.0;
  static const int maxParticles = 650;

  final Random _rnd = Random();
  final List<_Src> sources = [];
  final List<double> _cum = [];
  final List<_WindSample> _wind = [];
  final List<_Particle> ps = [];
  final Stopwatch _sw = Stopwatch()..start();

  double _last = 0, elapsed = 0, dt = 0;
  double meanU = 0, meanV = 0;

  bool get hasWind => _wind.isNotEmpty;
  bool get hasSources => sources.isNotEmpty;
  double get meanSpeedMs => sqrt(meanU * meanU + meanV * meanV);
  double get bearingTo => (atan2(meanU, meanV) * 180 / pi + 360) % 360;

  void tick() {
    elapsed = _sw.elapsedMicroseconds / 1e6;
    dt = (elapsed - _last).clamp(0.0, 0.05).toDouble();
    _last = elapsed;
  }

  void setCells(List<NoiseCell> cells) {
    sources.clear();
    _cum.clear();
    for (final c in cells) {
      if (c.laeq < 50 || c.polygon.length < 4) continue;
      final n = c.polygon.length - 1;
      var la = 0.0, lo = 0.0;
      for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        la += c.polygon[i].latitude;
        lo += c.polygon[i].longitude;
      }
      la /= n;
      lo /= n;
      final dLat = (c.polygon[0].latitude - la) * 111320.0;
      final dLon = (c.polygon[0].longitude - lo) * 111320.0 * cos(la * pi / 180);
      sources.add(_Src(la, lo, c.laeq, max(30.0, sqrt(dLat * dLat + dLon * dLon))));
    }
    sources.sort((a, b) => b.db.compareTo(a.db));
    if (sources.length > 400) sources.removeRange(400, sources.length);
    var acc = 0.0;
    for (final s in sources) {
      acc += pow(s.db - 45, 2).toDouble();
      _cum.add(acc);
    }
    if (sources.isEmpty) {
      ps.clear();
      return;
    }
    final target = min(maxParticles, 80 + sources.length * 8);
    while (ps.length < target) {
      ps.add(_Particle());
    }
    if (ps.length > target) ps.removeRange(target, ps.length);
  }

  /// Vent fourni par le serveur (identique pour tous) : un seul vecteur pour la zone.
  void setUniform(double lat, double lon, double u, double v) {
    _wind
      ..clear()
      ..add(_WindSample(lat, lon, u, v));
    meanU = u;
    meanV = v;
  }

  /// Vent d'une grille Open-Meteo récupérée par le client (repli si le serveur n'en fournit pas).
  void setWind(List<WeatherPoint> pts) {
    _wind.clear();
    var su = 0.0, sv = 0.0;
    for (final w in pts) {
      final to = (w.windDir + 180) * pi / 180;
      final ms = w.wind / 3.6;
      final u = sin(to) * ms;
      final v = cos(to) * ms;
      _wind.add(_WindSample(w.pos.latitude, w.pos.longitude, u, v));
      su += u;
      sv += v;
    }
    if (_wind.isEmpty) {
      meanU = 0;
      meanV = 0;
    } else {
      meanU = su / _wind.length;
      meanV = sv / _wind.length;
    }
  }

  (double, double) windAt(double lat, double lon) {
    if (_wind.isEmpty) return (0.0, 0.0);
    if (_wind.length == 1) return (_wind.first.u, _wind.first.v);
    var wu = 0.0, wv = 0.0, ws = 0.0;
    for (final w in _wind) {
      final dx = lon - w.lon, dy = lat - w.lat;
      final k = 1 / (dx * dx + dy * dy + 1e-8);
      wu += w.u * k;
      wv += w.v * k;
      ws += k;
    }
    return (wu / ws, wv / ws);
  }

  void _spawn(_Particle p, {required bool stagger}) {
    final r = _rnd.nextDouble() * _cum.last;
    var lo = 0, hi = _cum.length - 1;
    while (lo < hi) {
      final mid = (lo + hi) >> 1;
      if (_cum[mid] >= r) {
        hi = mid;
      } else {
        lo = mid + 1;
      }
    }
    final s = sources[lo];
    final ang = _rnd.nextDouble() * 2 * pi;
    final rad = sqrt(_rnd.nextDouble()) * s.rM;
    p.lat = s.lat + (sin(ang) * rad) / 111320.0;
    p.lon = s.lon + (cos(ang) * rad) / (111320.0 * cos(s.lat * pi / 180));
    p.db = s.db;
    p.life = 3.5 + _rnd.nextDouble() * 3.5;
    p.age = stagger ? _rnd.nextDouble() * p.life : 0;
    p.alive = true;
  }

  void advance() {
    if (sources.isEmpty) return;
    final k = dt * timeLapse;
    for (final p in ps) {
      if (!p.alive || p.age >= p.life) _spawn(p, stagger: !p.alive);
      final w = windAt(p.lat, p.lon);
      p.u = w.$1;
      p.v = w.$2;
      final cosLat = cos(p.lat * pi / 180);
      p.lat += ((p.v + (_rnd.nextDouble() - 0.5) * 1.2) * k) / 111320.0;
      p.lon += ((p.u + (_rnd.nextDouble() - 0.5) * 1.2) * k) / (111320.0 * cosLat);
      p.age += dt;
    }
  }
}

// ============================================================================
// Projection Web Mercator (tuiles de 256 px), identique à celle de flutter_map
// ============================================================================
class _Proj {
  _Proj(LatLng center, this.zoom, this.size) : _scale = pow(2.0, zoom).toDouble() {
    _ws = 256.0 * _scale;
    _cx = _x(center.longitude);
    _cy = _y(center.latitude);
  }

  final double zoom;
  final Size size;
  final double _scale;
  late final double _ws, _cx, _cy;

  double _x(double lon) => (lon + 180) / 360 * _ws;

  double _y(double lat) {
    final s = sin(lat * pi / 180).clamp(-0.9999, 0.9999).toDouble();
    return (0.5 - log((1 + s) / (1 - s)) / (4 * pi)) * _ws;
  }

  Offset at(double lat, double lon) =>
      Offset(size.width / 2 + _x(lon) - _cx, size.height / 2 + _y(lat) - _cy);

  double lonAt(double sx) => (_cx + (sx - size.width / 2)) / _ws * 360 - 180;

  double latAt(double sy) {
    final wy = _cy + (sy - size.height / 2);
    final n = pi - 2 * pi * wy / _ws;
    return 180 / pi * atan(0.5 * (exp(n) - exp(-n)));
  }

  double px(double meters, double lat) => meters * _scale / (156543.03392 * cos(lat * pi / 180));
}

// ============================================================================
// Scène dessinée par le calque (la carte du bruit vient uniquement du serveur)
// ============================================================================
class _Scene {
  _Scene({
    required this.clock,
    required this.sim,
    required this.cells,
    required this.selected,
    required this.mode,
    required this.showNoise,
    required this.showFlow,
    required this.dark,
    required this.field,
    required this.fieldPrev,
    required this.fieldSwapAt,
    required this.veil,
    required this.wxField,
    required this.tl,
    required this.tlPos,
    required this.missions,
    required this.me,
    required this.route,
    required this.accent,
    required this.roads,
    required this.roadStates,
    required this.showRoads,
    required this.selectedRoad,
    required this.mTarget,
  });
  final Listenable clock;
  final _FlowSim sim;
  final List<NoiseCell> cells;
  final String? selected;
  final _NoiseMode mode;
  final bool showNoise, showFlow, dark;
  final FieldGrid? field, fieldPrev;
  final double fieldSwapAt;
  final _Veil veil;
  final _WxField? wxField;
  final Timelapse? tl;
  final ValueNotifier<double> tlPos;
  final List<Mission> missions;
  final LatLng? me;
  final List<RoadSeg> roads;
  final Map<String, RoadState> roadStates;
  final bool showRoads;
  final String? selectedRoad;
  final MissionTarget? mTarget;
  final List<LatLng>? route;
  final Color accent;
}

class _Drawn {
  _Drawn(this.cell, this.path, this.rect);
  final NoiseCell cell;
  final Path path;
  final Rect rect;
}

class _FlowLayer extends StatelessWidget {
  const _FlowLayer({required this.scene});
  final _Scene scene;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final cam = MapCamera.of(context);
    return IgnorePointer(
      child: SizedBox.expand(child: CustomPaint(painter: _ScenePainter(cam, scene))),
    );
  }
}

class _ScenePainter extends CustomPainter {
  _ScenePainter(this.camera, this.s) : super(repaint: s.clock);
  final MapCamera camera;
  final _Scene s;

  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    s.sim.tick();
    final pr = _Proj(camera.center, camera.zoom, size);
    final clip = (Offset.zero & size).inflate(60);
    if (s.veil != _Veil.none) _veil(canvas, pr, size, clip);
    if (s.showNoise) {
      if (s.tl != null) {
        _noise(canvas, pr, clip, s.tl!.cellsAt(s.tlPos.value), blur: true);
      } else if (s.mode == _NoiseMode.cloud) {
        if (s.field != null) {
          _cloud(canvas, pr, size, clip);
        } else {
          _noise(canvas, pr, clip, s.cells, blur: true);
        }
      } else {
        _noise(canvas, pr, clip, s.cells, blur: false);
      }
    }
    if (s.showRoads) _roads(canvas, pr, size, clip);
    _missions(canvas, pr, clip);
    if (s.showFlow) _flow(canvas, pr, clip);
    _route(canvas, pr);
    _target(canvas, pr);
    _me(canvas, pr);
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(covariant _ScenePainter old) => true;

  void _text(Canvas c, String t, Offset center,
      {double size = 10, Color color = Colors.white, bool shadow = true}) {
    final tp = TextPainter(
      text: TextSpan(
        text: t,
        style: TextStyle(
          color: color,
          fontSize: size,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
          shadows: shadow ? const [Shadow(color: Colors.black54, blurRadius: 3)] : null,
        ),
      ),
      textDirection: TextDirection.ltr,
    )..layout();
    tp.paint(c, center - Offset(tp.width / 2, tp.height / 2));
  }

  // -------------------------------------------------------------- nuage de bruit (champ du serveur)
  void _cloud(Canvas canvas, _Proj pr, Size size, Rect clip) {
    final cur = s.field!;
    final blend = ((s.sim.elapsed - s.fieldSwapAt) / 1.5).clamp(0.0, 1.0).toDouble();
    final prev = blend < 1 ? s.fieldPrev : null;
    const step = 6.0;
    canvas.saveLayer(
      clip,
      Paint()
        ..imageFilter = ui.ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 2.4, sigmaY: 2.4, tileMode: ui.TileMode.decal),
    );
    final xs = <double>[], lons = <double>[];
    for (var x = -step; x <= size.width + step; x += step) {
      xs.add(x);
      lons.add(pr.lonAt(x));
    }
    final p = Paint()..style = PaintingStyle.fill;
    for (var y = -step; y <= size.height + step; y += step) {
      final lat = pr.latAt(y);
      for (var i = 0; i < xs.length; i++) {
        final a = cur.sample(lat, lons[i]);
        var lv = a.$1, sup = a.$2;
        if (prev != null) {
          final b = prev.sample(lat, lons[i]);
          if (b.$2 > 0 && sup > 0) {
            lv = b.$1 + (lv - b.$1) * blend;
            sup = b.$2 + (sup - b.$2) * blend;
          } else if (b.$2 > 0) {
            lv = b.$1;
            sup = b.$2 * (1 - blend);
          } else {
            sup = sup * blend;
          }
        }
        if (sup < 0.04 || lv.isNaN || lv < 36) continue;
        final alpha = (s.dark ? 0.8 : noiseBandAlpha(lv)) * pow(sup, 0.5).toDouble();
        p.color = noiseBandColor(lv, glow: s.dark).withValues(alpha: alpha);
        canvas.drawRect(Rect.fromLTWH(xs[i] - step / 2, y - step / 2, step + 0.5, step + 0.5), p);
      }
    }
    canvas.restore();
  }


    // -------------------------------------------------------------- routes réelles (nuage + trait net)
  static Object? _rkSegs, _rkStates;
  static List<double>? _rkCam;
  static Map<int, Path>? _rkBuckets;

  Map<int, Path> _roadBuckets(_Proj pr, Size size) {
    final cam = [camera.center.latitude, camera.center.longitude, camera.zoom, size.width, size.height];
    bool same() {
      final k = _rkCam;
      if (k == null || !identical(_rkSegs, s.roads) || !identical(_rkStates, s.roadStates)) return false;
      for (var i = 0; i < cam.length; i++) {
        if (k[i] != cam[i]) return false;
      }
      return true;
    }

    if (same() && _rkBuckets != null) return _rkBuckets!;
    final vb = camera.visibleBounds;
    const m = 0.002;
    final out = <int, Path>{};
    for (final seg in s.roads) {
      if (seg.maxLat < vb.south - m || seg.minLat > vb.north + m || seg.maxLon < vb.west - m || seg.minLon > vb.east + m) {
        continue;
      }
      final st = s.roadStates[seg.id];
      final ci = (st == null || !st.hasData) ? 0 : 1 + ((st.laeq - 35) / 5).floor().clamp(0, 11).toInt();
      final wc = seg.rank <= 2 ? 0 : (seg.rank <= 4 ? 1 : 2);
      final path = out.putIfAbsent(ci * 3 + wc, () => Path());
      for (var i = 0; i < seg.points.length; i++) {
        final o = pr.at(seg.points[i].latitude, seg.points[i].longitude);
        i == 0 ? path.moveTo(o.dx, o.dy) : path.lineTo(o.dx, o.dy);
      }
    }
    _rkSegs = s.roads;
    _rkStates = s.roadStates;
    _rkCam = cam;
    _rkBuckets = out;
    return out;
  }

  void _roads(Canvas canvas, _Proj pr, Size size, Rect clip) {
    if (s.roads.isEmpty) return;
    final buckets = _roadBuckets(pr, size);
    if (buckets.isEmpty) return;
    final keys = buckets.keys.toList()..sort();
    final z = camera.zoom;
    final base = (1.4 + (z - 11) * 0.45).clamp(1.4, 6.5).toDouble();
    double wOf(int wc) => base * (wc == 0 ? 1.7 : wc == 1 ? 1.25 : 1.0);
    Color cOf(int ci) => ci == 0 ? const Color(0xFF22C55E) : noiseColor(37.5 + 5.0 * (ci - 1));
    final paint = Paint()
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
      ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.round
      ..strokeJoin = StrokeJoin.round;

    if (s.mode == _NoiseMode.cloud) {
      final sigma = max(3.0, base * 1.6);
      canvas.saveLayer(
        clip,
        Paint()..imageFilter = ui.ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: sigma, sigmaY: sigma, tileMode: ui.TileMode.decal),
      );
      for (final k in keys) {
        final ci = k ~/ 3;
        paint
          ..color = cOf(ci).withValues(alpha: ci == 0 ? 0.22 : 0.55)
          ..strokeWidth = wOf(k % 3) * 3.2;
        canvas.drawPath(buckets[k]!, paint);
      }
      canvas.restore();
    }
    for (final k in keys) {
      final ci = k ~/ 3;
      paint
        ..color = cOf(ci).withValues(alpha: ci == 0 ? 0.55 : 0.95)
        ..strokeWidth = wOf(k % 3);
      canvas.drawPath(buckets[k]!, paint);
    }

    final sel = s.selectedRoad;
    if (sel != null) {
      for (final seg in s.roads) {
        if (seg.id != sel) continue;
        final path = Path();
        for (var i = 0; i < seg.points.length; i++) {
          final o = pr.at(seg.points[i].latitude, seg.points[i].longitude);
          i == 0 ? path.moveTo(o.dx, o.dy) : path.lineTo(o.dx, o.dy);
        }
        canvas.drawPath(path, paint..color = Colors.black54..strokeWidth = base * 2.6 + 4);
        canvas.drawPath(path, paint..color = Colors.white..strokeWidth = base * 2.6);
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  // -------------------------------------------------------------- point cible d'une mission route
  void _target(Canvas canvas, _Proj pr) {
    final t = s.mTarget;
    if (t == null) return;
    final o = pr.at(t.lat, t.lon);
    final r = max(pr.px(t.radiusM, t.lat), 14.0);
    final pulse = (s.sim.elapsed % 1.8) / 1.8;
    canvas.drawCircle(o, r + 14 * pulse, Paint()..color = s.accent.withValues(alpha: 0.30 * (1 - pulse)));
    canvas.drawCircle(o, r, Paint()..color = s.accent.withValues(alpha: 0.16));
    canvas.drawCircle(o, r, Paint()
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
      ..strokeWidth = 2.5
      ..color = s.accent);
    canvas.drawCircle(o, 11, Paint()..color = Colors.white);
    canvas.drawCircle(o, 8.5, Paint()..color = s.accent);
    _text(canvas, '⚑', o, size: 11);
  }

  // -------------------------------------------------------------- voile météo
  void _veil(Canvas canvas, _Proj pr, Size size, Rect clip) {
    final f = s.wxField;
    if (f == null) return;
    const step = 24.0;
    final pick = _pick(s.veil);
    final t = s.sim.elapsed;
    canvas.saveLayer(
      clip,
      Paint()
        ..imageFilter =
            ui.ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: step * 0.8, sigmaY: step * 0.8, tileMode: ui.TileMode.decal),
    );
    final xs = <double>[], lons = <double>[];
    for (var x = -step; x <= size.width + step; x += step) {
      xs.add(x);
      lons.add(pr.lonAt(x));
    }
    final p = Paint()..style = PaintingStyle.fill;
    for (var y = -step; y <= size.height + step; y += step) {
      final lat = pr.latAt(y);
      for (var i = 0; i < xs.length; i++) {
        final v = f.at(lat, lons[i], pick);
        if (v == null) continue;
        var c = _veilColor(s.veil, v, s.dark);
        if (c == null) continue;
        if (s.veil == _Veil.rain) {
          c = c.withValues(alpha: c.a * (0.9 + 0.1 * sin(xs[i] * 0.02 + y * 0.015 + t * 1.5)));
        }
        p.color = c;
        canvas.drawRect(Rect.fromLTWH(xs[i] - step / 2, y - step / 2, step + 0.5, step + 0.5), p);
      }
    }
    canvas.restore();
  }

  // -------------------------------------------------------------- cellules (grille, repli, historique)
  void _noise(Canvas canvas, _Proj pr, Rect clip, List<NoiseCell> cells, {required bool blur}) {
    final items = <_Drawn>[];
    for (var i = cells.length - 1; i >= 0 && items.length < 1500; i--) {
      final c = cells[i];
      final path = Path();
      var x0 = double.infinity, y0 = double.infinity;
      var x1 = -double.infinity, y1 = -double.infinity;
      for (var k = 0; k < c.polygon.length; k++) {
        final o = pr.at(c.polygon[k].latitude, c.polygon[k].longitude);
        if (k == 0) {
          path.moveTo(o.dx, o.dy);
        } else {
          path.lineTo(o.dx, o.dy);
        }
        if (o.dx < x0) x0 = o.dx;
        if (o.dy < y0) y0 = o.dy;
        if (o.dx > x1) x1 = o.dx;
        if (o.dy > y1) y1 = o.dy;
      }
      path.close();
      final rect = Rect.fromLTRB(x0, y0, x1, y1);
      if (!rect.overlaps(clip)) continue;
      items.add(_Drawn(c, path, rect));
    }
    if (items.isEmpty) return;
    final ordered = items.reversed.toList();
    final r = ordered.first.rect.shortestSide / 2;
    Color fillOf(NoiseCell c) => noiseBandColor(c.laeq, glow: s.dark)
        .withValues(alpha: min(0.92, (s.dark ? 0.8 : noiseBandAlpha(c.laeq)) * (0.8 + 0.2 * c.confidence.clamp(0.0, 1.0))));

    if (blur) {
      final sigma = max(2.5, r * 0.42);
      canvas.saveLayer(
        clip,
        Paint()
          ..imageFilter =
              ui.ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: sigma, sigmaY: sigma, tileMode: ui.TileMode.decal),
      );
      final p = Paint()..style = PaintingStyle.fill;
      for (final d in ordered) {
        p.color = fillOf(d.cell);
        canvas.drawPath(d.path, p);
      }
      canvas.restore();
    } else {
      final fill = Paint()..style = PaintingStyle.fill;
      final border = Paint()
        ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
        ..strokeWidth = 1.2
        ..strokeJoin = StrokeJoin.round;
      for (final d in ordered) {
        fill.color = fillOf(d.cell);
        border.color = noiseBandColor(d.cell.laeq, glow: s.dark).withValues(alpha: 0.9);
        canvas.drawPath(d.path, fill);
        canvas.drawPath(d.path, border);
      }
    }

    if (s.selected != null && s.tl == null) {
      for (final d in ordered) {
        if (d.cell.h3 != s.selected) continue;
        canvas.drawPath(
            d.path,
            Paint()
              ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
              ..strokeWidth = 6
              ..color = Colors.black54
              ..strokeJoin = StrokeJoin.round);
        canvas.drawPath(
            d.path,
            Paint()
              ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
              ..strokeWidth = 3
              ..color = Colors.white
              ..strokeJoin = StrokeJoin.round);
      }
    }

    if (r > 26 && ordered.length <= 80) {
      for (final d in ordered) {
        final lowOnLight = noiseBandIndex(d.cell.laeq) <= 3 && !s.dark;
        _text(canvas, d.cell.laeq.toStringAsFixed(0), d.rect.center,
            size: min(18.0, r * 0.5),
            color: lowOnLight ? const Color(0xFF5B2A00) : Colors.white,
            shadow: !lowOnLight);
      }
    }
  }

  // -------------------------------------------------------------- zones de missions
  void _missions(Canvas canvas, _Proj pr, Rect clip) {
    for (final m in s.missions) {
      if (!m.isActive) continue;
      //final o = pr.at(m.centerLat, m.centerLon);
      //final r = pr.px(m.radiusM, m.centerLat);
      //if (!clip.inflate(r).contains(o)) continue;
      final o = pr.at(m.centerLat, m.centerLon);
      final r = max(pr.px(m.radiusM, m.centerLat), m.isRoad ? 12.0 : 0.0);
      final acc = m.status == 'accepted';
      final col = acc ? s.accent : const Color(0xFFF59E0B);
      canvas.drawCircle(o, r, Paint()..color = col.withValues(alpha: acc ? 0.10 : 0.06));
      final ring = Paint()
        ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
        ..strokeWidth = 2
        ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.round
        ..color = col.withValues(alpha: 0.9);
      if (acc) {
        canvas.drawCircle(o, r, ring);
      } else {
        final rect = Rect.fromCircle(center: o, radius: r);
        const n = 36;
        for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
          canvas.drawArc(rect, i * 2 * pi / n, pi / n, false, ring);
        }
      }
      _text(canvas, '${m.confirmedCount}/${m.requiredCount}', o,
          size: 12, color: Color.lerp(col, Colors.black, 0.35)!, shadow: false);
    }
  }

  // -------------------------------------------------------------- flux du vent
  void _flow(Canvas canvas, _Proj pr, Rect clip) {
    final sim = s.sim;
    if (!sim.hasSources) return;
    final t = sim.elapsed;

    final ripple = Paint()
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
      ..strokeWidth = 2;
    var count = 0;
    for (var i = 0; i < sim.sources.length && count < 24; i++) {
      final src = sim.sources[i];
      final c0 = pr.at(src.lat, src.lon);
      if (!clip.contains(c0)) continue;
      count++;
      final w = sim.windAt(src.lat, src.lon);
      final speed = sqrt(w.$1 * w.$1 + w.$2 * w.$2);
      final ang = speed < 0.05 ? 0.0 : atan2(-w.$2, w.$1);
      final r0 = max(6.0, pr.px(src.rM, src.lat) * 0.55);
      final stretch = 1 + min(speed / 5.0, 1.6);
      final lvl = ((src.db - 45) / 45).clamp(0.25, 1.0).toDouble();
      for (var k = 0; k < 2; k++) {
        final ph = (t * 0.30 + i * 0.173 + k * 0.5) % 1.0;
        final shift = pr.px(speed * _FlowSim.timeLapse * 2.2 * ph, src.lat);
        final ctr = c0 + Offset(cos(ang), sin(ang)) * shift;
        final a = r0 * (1 + 2.2 * ph) * stretch;
        final b = r0 * (1 + 1.8 * ph);
        ripple.color = noiseBandColor(max(src.db, 62), glow: s.dark).withValues(alpha: (1 - ph) * 0.6 * lvl);
        canvas.save();
        canvas.translate(ctr.dx, ctr.dy);
        canvas.rotate(ang);
        canvas.drawOval(Rect.fromCenter(center: Offset.zero, width: a * 2, height: b * 2), ripple);
        canvas.restore();
      }
    }

    sim.advance();
    final line = Paint()
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
      ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.round;
    final dot = Paint()..style = PaintingStyle.fill;
    const trail = _FlowSim.timeLapse * 0.55;
    for (final p in sim.ps) {
      if (!p.alive) continue;
      final pos = pr.at(p.lat, p.lon);
      if (!clip.contains(pos)) continue;
      final f = (p.age / p.life).clamp(0.0, 1.0).toDouble();
      final a = sin(pi * f) * 0.85;
      if (a <= 0.02) continue;
      final col = noiseBandColor(max(p.db - 8 * f, 66), glow: s.dark).withValues(alpha: a);
      final tail = Offset(pr.px(p.u * trail, p.lat), -pr.px(p.v * trail, p.lat));
      line
        ..color = col
        ..strokeWidth = 1.2 + 2.0 * (1 - f);
      canvas.drawLine(pos - tail, pos, line);
      dot.color = col;
      canvas.drawCircle(pos, 1.3 + 1.6 * (1 - f), dot);
    }
  }

  // -------------------------------------------------------------- itinéraire
  void _route(Canvas canvas, _Proj pr) {
    final pts = s.route;
    if (pts == null || pts.length < 2) return;
    final path = Path();
    for (var i = 0; i < pts.length; i++) {
      final o = pr.at(pts[i].latitude, pts[i].longitude);
      if (i == 0) {
        path.moveTo(o.dx, o.dy);
      } else {
        path.lineTo(o.dx, o.dy);
      }
    }
    final base = Paint()
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
      ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.round
      ..strokeJoin = StrokeJoin.round;
    canvas.drawPath(path, base..strokeWidth = 9..color = Colors.white);
    canvas.drawPath(path, base..strokeWidth = 5..color = s.accent);
  }

  void _me(Canvas canvas, _Proj pr) {
    final me = s.me;
    if (me == null) return;
    final o = pr.at(me.latitude, me.longitude);
    final t = (s.sim.elapsed % 2.4) / 2.4;
    canvas.drawCircle(o, 10 + 36 * t, Paint()..color = s.accent.withValues(alpha: 0.35 * (1 - t)));
    canvas.drawCircle(o, 9, Paint()..color = Colors.white);
    canvas.drawCircle(o, 6.5, Paint()..color = s.accent);
  }
}

// ============================================================================
// Écran
// ============================================================================
class MapScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const MapScreen({super.key});
  @override
  State<MapScreen> createState() => _MapScreenState();
}

class _MapScreenState extends State<MapScreen> with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  static const int _gridCols = 5, _gridRows = 5;

  final MapController _map = MapController();
  late final AnimationController _clock =
      AnimationController(vsync: this, duration: const Duration(seconds: 1))..repeat();
  final _FlowSim _sim = _FlowSim();
  late final MapViewModel _vm;

  StreamSubscription<Position>? _posSub;
  Timer? _debounce;
  Timer? _windTimer;
  Timer? _fieldTimer;

  LatLng? _me;
  bool _ready = false;
  bool _centered = false;

  bool _showNoise = true;
  bool _showFlow = true;
  bool _darkBase = false;
  _NoiseMode _mode = _NoiseMode.cloud;
  _Veil _veil = _Veil.none;
  bool _showRoads = true;
  MissionRoute? _mRoute;

  // nuage de bruit du serveur
  FieldGrid? _fieldGrid;
  FieldGrid? _fieldPrev;
  double _fieldSwapAt = -10;
  bool _fieldBusy = false;
  String? _fieldError;

  // météo (voiles)
  List<WeatherPoint> _weather = const [];
  _WxField? _wxField;
  LatLngBounds? _windArea;
  DateTime? _windAt;
  DateTime? _windTry;
  bool _windBusy = false;
  bool _windFailed = false;

  OsrmRoute? _route;

  List<NoiseCell>? _cellsKey;
  List<NoiseCell> _sorted = const [];
  int _seenVersion = -1;

  // historique animé
  bool _tlOpen = false;
  Timelapse? _tl;
  bool _tlPlaying = true;
  bool _tlLoading = false;
  double _tlSpeed = 1;
  String _tlStep = 'day';
  String? _tlError;
  int _tlFrame = -1;
  DateTime _tlLast = DateTime.now();
  final ValueNotifier<double> _tlPos = ValueNotifier(0);

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _vm = context.read<MapViewModel>();
    _vm.startAuto();
    _initLocation();
    _clock.addListener(_tlTick);
    _windTimer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(minutes: 10), (_) => _loadWind(force: true));
    _fieldTimer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 30), (_) {
      _loadField();
      _reloadRoads();
    });
    //_fieldTimer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 30), (_) => _loadField());
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _debounce?.cancel();
    _windTimer?.cancel();
    _fieldTimer?.cancel();
    _posSub?.cancel();
    _clock.removeListener(_tlTick);
    _clock.dispose();
    _tlPos.dispose();
    _vm.stopAuto();
    super.dispose();
  }
  void _reloadRoads() {
    if (!_ready || !mounted || !_showRoads) return;
    context.read<RoadsViewModel>().load(_vm.filters, _bbox(), _map.camera.zoom);
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ position
  Future<void> _initLocation() async {
    if (!await ensureLocationPermission()) return;
    try {
      final p = await Geolocator.getCurrentPosition(
          locationSettings: const LocationSettings(accuracy: LocationAccuracy.high));
      _setMe(p, recenter: true);
    } catch (_) {}
    _posSub = Geolocator.getPositionStream(
      locationSettings: const LocationSettings(accuracy: LocationAccuracy.high, distanceFilter: 8),
    ).listen((p) => _setMe(p), onError: (_) {});
  }

  void _setMe(Position p, {bool recenter = false}) {
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() => _me = LatLng(p.latitude, p.longitude));
    if ((recenter || !_centered) && _ready) {
      _centered = true;
      _map.move(_me!, 15);
      Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 350), _reload);
    }
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ données (source de vérité : serveur)
  String _bbox() {
    final b = _map.camera.visibleBounds;
    final dLat = (b.north - b.south) * 0.25;
    final dLon = (b.east - b.west) * 0.25;
    double cl(double v, double lo, double hi) => v.clamp(lo, hi).toDouble();
    return '${cl(b.west - dLon, -179.9, 179.9)},${cl(b.south - dLat, -89.9, 89.9)},'
        '${cl(b.east + dLon, -179.9, 179.9)},${cl(b.north + dLat, -89.9, 89.9)}';
  }

  void _reload() {
    if (!_ready || !mounted) return;
    _vm.load(bbox: _bbox());
    _loadField();
    _loadWind();
    _reloadRoads();
  }

  void _scheduleReload() {
    _debounce?.cancel();
    _debounce = Timer(const Duration(milliseconds: 600), _reload);
  }

  void _onReady() {
    _ready = true;
    if (_me != null) {
      _centered = true;
      _map.move(_me!, 15);
    }
    _reload();
  }

  Future<void> _loadField() async {
    if (!_ready || !mounted || _fieldBusy || !_showNoise || _tlOpen || _mode != _NoiseMode.cloud) return;
    _fieldBusy = true;
    try {
      final g = await context.read<ApiClient>().fieldGrid(_vm.filters, _bbox());
      if (!mounted) return;
