      setState(() {
        _fieldPrev = _fieldGrid;
        _fieldGrid = g;
        _fieldSwapAt = _sim.elapsed;
        _fieldError = null;
      });
      final w = g.wind;
      if (w != null) {
        final c = _map.camera.center;
        _sim.setUniform(c.latitude, c.longitude, w.u, w.v);
      }
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _fieldError = e.code);
    } finally {
      _fieldBusy = false;
    }
  }

  Future<void> _loadWind({bool force = false}) async {
    if (!_ready || !mounted || _windBusy) return;
    if (_veil == _Veil.none && !(_showFlow && _fieldGrid?.wind == null)) return;
    final b = _map.camera.visibleBounds;
    final a = _windArea;
    final covered =
        a != null && b.south >= a.south && b.north <= a.north && b.west >= a.west && b.east <= a.east;
    final fresh = _windAt != null && DateTime.now().difference(_windAt!) < const Duration(minutes: 10);
    if (!force && covered && fresh) return;
    if (!force && _windTry != null && DateTime.now().difference(_windTry!) < const Duration(seconds: 30)) {
      return;
    }
    _windTry = DateTime.now();
    _windBusy = true;
    double lo(double v) => (v / 0.1).floor() * 0.1;
    double hi(double v) => (v / 0.1).ceil() * 0.1;
    final wide = LatLngBounds(
      LatLng((lo(b.south) - 0.1).clamp(-89.9, 89.9).toDouble(),
          (lo(b.west) - 0.1).clamp(-179.9, 179.9).toDouble()),
      LatLng((hi(b.north) + 0.1).clamp(-89.9, 89.9).toDouble(),
          (hi(b.east) + 0.1).clamp(-179.9, 179.9).toDouble()),
    );
    try {
      final pts = await LiveWeatherService.fetchGrid(wide,
          cols: _gridCols, rows: _gridRows, soil: _veil == _Veil.soil);
      if (!mounted) return;
      if (pts.isNotEmpty) {
        if (_fieldGrid?.wind == null) _sim.setWind(pts);
        setState(() {
          _weather = pts;
          _wxField = _WxField(pts, wide, _gridCols, _gridRows);
          _windArea = wide;
          _windAt = DateTime.now();
          _windFailed = false;
        });
      } else {
        setState(() => _windFailed = true);
      }
    } finally {
      _windBusy = false;
    }
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ historique animé
  void _tlTick() {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final dt = now.difference(_tlLast).inMicroseconds / 1e6;
    _tlLast = now;
    final tl = _tl;
    if (tl == null || !_tlPlaying || tl.frameCount < 2) return;
    var p = _tlPos.value + min(dt, 0.1) * 1.5 * _tlSpeed;
    if (p >= tl.frameCount - 1) p = 0;
    _tlSet(p);
  }

  void _tlSet(double p) {
    _tlPos.value = p;
    final f = p.round();
    final tl = _tl;
    if (tl != null && f != _tlFrame) {
      _tlFrame = f;
      _sim.setCells(tl.cellsAt(f.toDouble()));
    }
  }

  Future<void> _openTimelapse([String? step]) async {
    if (!_ready) return;
    if (step != null) _tlStep = step;
    setState(() {
      _tlOpen = true;
      _tlLoading = true;
      _tlError = null;
    });
    try {
      var f = _vm.filters;
      if (f.period == 'today') f = f.copyWith(period: '7d');
      final tl = await context.read<ApiClient>().timelapse(f, step: _tlStep, bbox: _bbox());
      if (!mounted) return;
      _tlFrame = -1;
      _tlPlaying = true;
      _tlLast = DateTime.now();
      setState(() {
        _tl = tl;
        _tlLoading = false;
      });
      _tlSet(0);
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _tlLoading = false;
        _tlError = errorMessage(e);
      });
    }
  }

  void _closeTimelapse() {
    setState(() {
      _tlOpen = false;
      _tl = null;
      _tlError = null;
      _tlLoading = false;
    });
    _sim.setCells(_vm.cells);
    _loadField();
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ interactions
  void _onTap(LatLng p) {
    if (_tlOpen) return;
    if (_showNoise) {
      final c = _vm.cellAt(p);
      if (c != null) {
        _vm.select(c.h3);
        showModalBottomSheet(
          context: context,
          isScrollControlled: true,
          constraints: BoxConstraints(maxHeight: MediaQuery.of(context).size.height * 0.88),
          builder: (_) => CellSheet(
            cell: c,
            filters: _vm.filters,
            store: context.read<SessionController>().store,
          ),
        ).whenComplete(() => _vm.select(null));
        return;
      }
    }
    if (_showRoads) {
      final rv = context.read<RoadsViewModel>();
      final seg = rv.hit(p, _map.camera.zoom);
      if (seg != null) {
        rv.select(seg.id);
        showModalBottomSheet(
          context: context,
          isScrollControlled: true,
          constraints: BoxConstraints(maxHeight: MediaQuery.of(context).size.height * 0.8),
          builder: (_) => RoadSheet(seg: seg, state: rv.states[seg.id], filters: _vm.filters),
        ).whenComplete(() => rv.select(null));
        return;
      }
    }
    for (final m in context.read<MissionsController>().list) {
      if (!m.isActive) continue;
      if (const Distance().as(LengthUnit.Meter, p, LatLng(m.centerLat, m.centerLon)) <= max(m.radiusM, 60.0)) {
        _openMissions();
        return;
      }
    }
    _vm.select(null);
  }

  Future<void> _openMissions() async {
    final r = await Navigator.of(context)
        .push<MissionFocus>(MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const MissionsScreen()));
    if (r == null || !mounted || !_ready) return;
    final m = r.mission;
    final target = LatLng(m.centerLat, m.centerLon);
    if (!r.route) {
      setState(() => _mRoute = null);
      _map.move(target, m.isRoad ? 16 : 14);
      _reload();
      return;
    }
    await _routeToMission(m);
  }

  /// Trace le chemin réel par les routes jusqu'au point cible (TomTom via le serveur, OSRM en secours).
  Future<void> _routeToMission(Mission m) async {
    final target = LatLng(m.centerLat, m.centerLon);
    if (_me == null) {
      _map.move(target, 16);
      showToast(context, apiErrorLabel('gps_unavailable'));
      return;
    }
    final api = context.read<ApiClient>();
    MissionRoute? mr;
    try {
      mr = await api.missionRoute(m.id, _me!.latitude, _me!.longitude);
    } catch (_) {
      try {
        final o = await api.getOsrmRoute(_me!, target);
        mr = MissionRoute(
            missionId: m.id,
            target: MissionTarget(lat: m.centerLat, lon: m.centerLon, radiusM: m.radiusM, roadName: m.roadName, roadClass: m.roadClass, dayPart: m.targetDayPart),
            points: o.points,
            lengthM: o.distanceM.round(),
            timeS: o.durationS.round());
      } catch (e) {
        if (!mounted) return;
        _map.move(target, 16);
        showToast(context, errorMessage(e));
        return;
      }
    }
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() {
      _mRoute = mr;
      _route = null;
    });
    _map.fitCamera(CameraFit.bounds(
      bounds: LatLngBounds.fromPoints([...mr.points, target]),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(40, 170, 40, 200),
    ));
    _reload();
  }

  Future<void> _openSearch() async {
    final r = await showModalBottomSheet<(PlaceResult, bool)>(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      builder: (_) => SearchSheet(near: _me ?? _map.camera.center),
    );
    if (r == null || !mounted) return;
    final (p, go) = r;
    final dest = LatLng(p.lat, p.lon);

    if (!go) {
      _map.move(dest, 16);
      _reload();
      return;
    }
    if (_me == null) {
      _map.move(dest, 16);
      showToast(context, apiErrorLabel('gps_unavailable'));
      return;
    }
    try {
      final route = await context.read<ApiClient>().getOsrmRoute(_me!, dest);
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() => _route = route);
      _map.fitCamera(CameraFit.bounds(
        bounds: LatLngBounds.fromPoints(route.points),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(40, 150, 40, 180),
      ));
      _reload();
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      _map.move(dest, 16);
      showToast(context, errorMessage(e));
    }
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ feuilles
  void _showLayersSheet() {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      showDragHandle: true,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      builder: (_) => StatefulBuilder(builder: (ctx, setM) {
        void both(VoidCallback f) {
          setState(f);
          setM(() {});
        }

        const veilLabels = {
          _Veil.none: 'Aucun',
          _Veil.temp: 'Température',
          _Veil.rain: 'Pluie',
          _Veil.cloud: 'Nuages',
          _Veil.soil: 'Sol',
        };

        return SafeArea(
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 0, 20, 20),
            child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
              const Text('Couches de la carte', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 18)),
              const SizedBox(height: 4),
              Text('La carte du bruit est calculée par le serveur : identique pour tous les utilisateurs.',
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant)),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              SwitchListTile(
                contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                secondary: const Icon(Icons.graphic_eq_rounded),
                title: const Text('Bruit'),
                value: _showNoise,
                onChanged: (v) {
                  both(() => _showNoise = v);
                  if (v) _loadField();
                },
              ),
              if (_showNoise)
                SegmentedButton<_NoiseMode>(
                  segments: const [
                    ButtonSegment(value: _NoiseMode.cloud, label: Text('Nuage'), icon: Icon(Icons.cloud_rounded)),
                    ButtonSegment(value: _NoiseMode.grid, label: Text('Grille'), icon: Icon(Icons.hexagon_outlined)),
                  ],
                  selected: {_mode},
                  onSelectionChanged: (v) {
                    both(() => _mode = v.first);
                    _loadField();
                  },
                  showSelectedIcon: false,
                ),
              SwitchListTile(
                contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                secondary: const Icon(Icons.air_rounded),
                title: const Text('Flux du vent animé'),
                subtitle: const Text('Propagation indicative du bruit selon le vent'),
                value: _showFlow,
                onChanged: (v) {
                  both(() => _showFlow = v);
                  if (v) _loadWind(force: true);
                },
              ),
              SwitchListTile(
                contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                secondary: const Icon(Icons.add_road_rounded),
                title: const Text('Routes (tracé réel)'),
                subtitle: const Text('Vert = pas encore mesuré · zoomez pour voir les petites rues'),
                value: _showRoads,
                onChanged: (v) {
                  both(() => _showRoads = v);
                  if (v) _reloadRoads();
                },
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              const Text('Voile météo', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 16)),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              Wrap(spacing: 8, runSpacing: 8, children: [
                for (final v in _Veil.values)
                  ChoiceChip(
                    label: Text(veilLabels[v]!),
                    selected: _veil == v,
                    onSelected: (_) {
                      both(() => _veil = v);
                      if (v != _Veil.none) _loadWind(force: true);
                    },
                  ),
              ]),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              const Text('Fond de carte', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 16)),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              SegmentedButton<bool>(
                segments: const [
                  ButtonSegment(value: false, label: Text('Clair'), icon: Icon(Icons.light_mode_rounded)),
                  ButtonSegment(value: true, label: Text('Sombre'), icon: Icon(Icons.dark_mode_rounded)),
                ],
                selected: {_darkBase},
                onSelectionChanged: (v) => both(() => _darkBase = v.first),
                showSelectedIcon: false,
              ),
            ]),
          ),
        );
      }),
    );
  }

  void _showFiltersSheet() {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      showDragHandle: true,
      builder: (_) => _FiltersSheet(
        initial: _vm.filters,
        onApply: (f) {
          _vm.setFilters(f);
          Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 200), _loadField);
          Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 250), _reloadRoads);
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  WeatherPoint? _nearestWeather() {
    if (_weather.isEmpty || !_ready) return null;
    final c = _map.camera.center;
    WeatherPoint? best;
    var bd = double.infinity;
    for (final w in _weather) {
      final dx = w.pos.latitude - c.latitude;
      final dy = w.pos.longitude - c.longitude;
      final d = dx * dx + dy * dy;
      if (d < bd) {
        bd = d;
        best = w;
      }
    }
    return best;
  }
  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ build
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final cells = context.select<MapViewModel, List<NoiseCell>>((v) => v.cells);
    final selected = context.select<MapViewModel, String?>((v) => v.selected);
    final loading = context.select<MapViewModel, bool>((v) => v.loading);
    final error = context.select<MapViewModel, String?>((v) => v.error);
    final version = context.select<RecorderController, int>((r) => r.completedVersion);
    final proposalCount = context.select<ProposalsController, int>((p) => p.list.length);
    final missions = context.select<MissionsController, List<Mission>>((c) => c.list);
    final offeredCount = context.select<MissionsController, int>((c) => c.offeredCount);
    final quota = context.select<QuotaController, QuotaState?>((c) => c.state);

    final roadSegs = context.select<RoadsViewModel, List<RoadSeg>>((v) => v.segs);
    final roadStates = context.select<RoadsViewModel, Map<String, RoadState>>((v) => v.states);
    final roadSel = context.select<RoadsViewModel, String?>((v) => v.selected);

    if (version != _seenVersion) {
      _seenVersion = version;
      WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
        if (!mounted) return;
        context.read<QuotaController>().refresh();
        context.read<MissionsController>().refresh(sync: false);
      });
    }
    if (!identical(_cellsKey, cells)) {
      _cellsKey = cells;
      _sorted = [...cells]..sort((a, b) => a.laeq.compareTo(b.laeq));
      if (!_tlOpen) _sim.setCells(cells);
    }

    final scene = _Scene(
      clock: _clock,
      sim: _sim,
      cells: _sorted,
      selected: selected,
      mode: _mode,
      showNoise: _showNoise,
      showFlow: _showFlow,
      dark: _darkBase,
      field: _fieldGrid,
      fieldPrev: _fieldPrev,
      fieldSwapAt: _fieldSwapAt,
      veil: _veil,
      wxField: _wxField,
      tl: _tl,
      tlPos: _tlPos,
      missions: missions,
      me: _me,
      accent: cs.primary,
      roads: roadSegs,
      roadStates: roadStates,
      showRoads: _showRoads,
      selectedRoad: roadSel,
      mTarget: _mRoute?.target,
      route: _mRoute?.points ?? _route?.points,
    );

    final badge = proposalCount + offeredCount;
    final near = (_showFlow && _sim.hasWind) ? _nearestWeather() : null;
    double? veilNow;
    if (_veil != _Veil.none && _wxField != null && _ready) {
      final c = _map.camera.center;
      veilNow = _wxField!.at(c.latitude, c.longitude, _pick(_veil));
    }
    final bottomShift = _tlOpen ? 250.0 : 30.0;

    return Scaffold(
      body: Stack(children: [
        FlutterMap(
          mapController: _map,
          options: MapOptions(
            initialCenter: AppConfig.fallbackCenter,
            initialZoom: 13,
            minZoom: 3,
            maxZoom: 19,
            backgroundColor: _darkBase ? const Color(0xFF0B1116) : const Color(0xFFEFF3F5),
            interactionOptions:
                const InteractionOptions(flags: InteractiveFlag.all & ~InteractiveFlag.rotate),
            onMapReady: _onReady,
            onTap: (_, p) => _onTap(p),
            onPositionChanged: (cam, gesture) {
              if (gesture) _scheduleReload();
            },
          ),
          children: [
            TileLayer(
              urlTemplate: _darkBase ? AppConfig.tileDark : AppConfig.tileLight,
              subdomains: const ['a', 'b', 'c', 'd'],
              userAgentPackageName: AppConfig.packageName,
              maxNativeZoom: 19,
              panBuffer: 2,
            ),
            _FlowLayer(scene: scene),
          ],
        ),

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- barre du haut
        SafeArea(
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 8, 16, 0),
            child: Row(children: [
              SoftCard(
                glass: true,
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 8),
                child: Row(children: [
                  const SunuLogo(size: 24),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  const Text('SunuSound', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 16)),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  _LiveDot(clock: _clock),
                ]),
              ),
              if (quota != null) ...[
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                SoftCard(
                  glass: true,
                  onTap: _openMissions,
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 8),
                  child: Row(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
                    Icon(Icons.bolt_rounded, size: 18, color: quota.balance < 1 ? const Color(0xFFEF4444) : cs.primary),
                    const SizedBox(width: 2),
                    Text(quota.balance.toStringAsFixed(1), style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
                  ]),
                ),
              ],
              const Spacer(),
              FloatingActionButton.small(
                heroTag: 'search',
                onPressed: _openSearch,
                child: const Icon(Icons.search_rounded),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              FloatingActionButton.small(
                heroTag: 'missions',
                onPressed: _openMissions,
                child: Badge(
                  isLabelVisible: badge > 0,
                  label: Text('$badge'),
                  child: const Icon(Icons.notifications_none_rounded),
                ),
              ),
            ]),
          ),
        ),

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- infos sous la barre
        Positioned(
          top: MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top + 64,
          left: 16,
          right: 16,
          child: Column(children: [
            if (loading)
              ClipRRect(
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(3),
                child: const LinearProgressIndicator(minHeight: 3),
              ),
            if (_route != null)
              SoftCard(
                glass: true,
                margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8),
                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 12, 8, 12),
                child: Row(children: [
                  Icon(Icons.directions_car_rounded, color: cs.primary, size: 28),
                  const SizedBox(width: 12),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
                      Text(
                        '${(_route!.durationS / 60).round()} min · ${(_route!.distanceM / 1000).toStringAsFixed(1)} km',
                        style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 16),
                      ),
                      const Text('Itinéraire le plus rapide', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12)),
                    ]),
                  ),
                  IconButton(
                    onPressed: () => setState(() => _route = null),
                    icon: const Icon(Icons.close_rounded),
                  ),
                ]),
              ),

            if (_mRoute != null)
              SoftCard(
                glass: true,
                margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8),
                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 12, 8, 12),
                child: Row(children: [
                  Icon(Icons.flag_rounded, color: cs.primary, size: 28),
                  const SizedBox(width: 12),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
                      Text(
                          _mRoute!.target.roadName != null
                              ? 'Mission : ${_mRoute!.target.roadName}'
                              : 'Mission : point cible',
                          maxLines: 1,
                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                          style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 15)),
                      Text(
                          '${(_mRoute!.timeS / 60).round()} min · ${(_mRoute!.lengthM / 1000).toStringAsFixed(1)} km par la route'
                          '${_mRoute!.target.dayPart != null ? ' · mesurer en ${dayPartLabel(_mRoute!.target.dayPart!).toLowerCase()}' : ''}',
                          style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12)),
                    ]),
                  ),
                  IconButton(onPressed: () => setState(() => _mRoute = null), icon: const Icon(Icons.close_rounded)),
                ]),
              ),


            if (error != null)
              SoftCard(
                glass: true,
                margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8),
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 10),
                child: Row(children: [
                  Icon(Icons.cloud_off_rounded, color: cs.error, size: 20),
                  const SizedBox(width: 10),
                  Expanded(child: Text(apiErrorLabel(error), style: TextStyle(color: cs.error, fontSize: 13))),
                ]),
              ),
            if (_showNoise && !_tlOpen && _mode == _NoiseMode.cloud && _fieldError == 'bbox_too_large')
              SoftCard(
                glass: true,
                margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8),
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 10),
                child: Row(children: [
                  Icon(Icons.zoom_in_rounded, color: cs.primary, size: 20),
                  const SizedBox(width: 10),
                  const Expanded(
                    child: Text('Zoomez pour afficher le nuage de bruit (cellules affichées en attendant).',
                        style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13)),
                  ),
                ]),
              ),
            if (!loading && error == null && _showNoise && !_tlOpen && cells.isEmpty)
              SoftCard(
                glass: true,
                margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8),
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 10),
                child: Row(children: [
                  Icon(Icons.info_outline_rounded, color: cs.primary, size: 20),
                  const SizedBox(width: 10),
                  const Expanded(
                    child: Text('Aucune mesure validée dans cette zone pour la période choisie.',
                        style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13)),
                  ),
                ]),
              ),
          ]),
        ),

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- cartes en bas à gauche
        Positioned(
          left: 16,
          bottom: bottomShift,
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            spacing: 8,
            children: [
              if (near != null) _windCard(cs, near),
              if (_showFlow && !_sim.hasWind && _windFailed)
                const SoftCard(
                  glass: true,
                  padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 8),
                  child: Text('Vent indisponible : ondes sans direction', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12)),
                ),
              if (_veil != _Veil.none) _veilCard(cs, veilNow),
              if (_showNoise) _noiseLegend(cs),
            ],
          ),
        ),

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- lecteur d'historique
        if (_tlOpen) Positioned(left: 16, right: 16, bottom: 84, child: _timelapseBar(cs)),

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- boutons
        Positioned(
          right: 16,
          bottom: bottomShift,
          child: Column(children: [
            FloatingActionButton.small(
              heroTag: 'layers',
              onPressed: _showLayersSheet,
              child: const Icon(Icons.layers_rounded),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            FloatingActionButton.small(
              heroTag: 'filters',
              onPressed: _showFiltersSheet,
              child: const Icon(Icons.tune_rounded),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            FloatingActionButton.small(
              heroTag: 'history',
              onPressed: _tlOpen ? _closeTimelapse : () => _openTimelapse(),
              child: Icon(_tlOpen ? Icons.history_toggle_off_rounded : Icons.history_rounded),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            FloatingActionButton.small(
              heroTag: 'locate',
              onPressed: () async {
                if (_me == null) {
                  await _initLocation();
                } else if (_ready) {
                  _map.move(_me!, 16);
                  _reload();
                }
              },
              child: const Icon(Icons.my_location_rounded),
            ),
          ]),
        ),
      ]),
    );
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ widgets annexes
  Widget _windCard(ColorScheme cs, WeatherPoint near) {
    return SoftCard(
      glass: true,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 8),
      child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
        Row(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
          Transform.rotate(
            angle: _sim.bearingTo * pi / 180,
            child: Icon(Icons.navigation_rounded, color: windColor(_sim.meanSpeedMs * 3.6), size: 24),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          Text('${(_sim.meanSpeedMs * 3.6).round()} km/h',
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
        ]),
        Text('${compass((_sim.bearingTo + 180) % 360)} → ${compass(_sim.bearingTo)}',
            style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
        Text('${near.temp.round()}°C · ${weatherLabel(near.code)}', style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12)),
        const SizedBox(height: 2),
        Text('Flux indicatif · animation accélérée',
            style: TextStyle(fontSize: 9, color: cs.onSurfaceVariant)),
      ]),
    );
  }

  Widget _veilCard(ColorScheme cs, double? value) {
    final info = _veilInfo(_veil);
    final soilMissing = _veil == _Veil.soil && _weather.isNotEmpty && _weather.first.soil == null;
    return SoftCard(
      glass: true,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(10, 8, 10, 8),
      child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
        Row(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
          Text(info.title, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
          if (value != null) ...[
            const SizedBox(width: 8),
            Text(_veilValue(_veil, value), style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
          ],
        ]),
        const SizedBox(height: 4),
        Container(
          width: 130,
          height: 8,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
            gradient: LinearGradient(colors: info.colors),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 2),
        SizedBox(
          width: 130,
          child: Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween, children: [
            Text(info.lo, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 9)),
            Text(info.hi, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 9)),
          ]),
        ),
        if (soilMissing)
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 2),
            child: Text('Donnée sol indisponible', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 9, color: cs.error)),
          ),
      ]),
    );
  }

  Widget _noiseLegend(ColorScheme cs) {
    return SoftCard(
      glass: true,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(10, 8, 10, 8),
      child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
        const Text('Niveau sonore dB(A)', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
        const SizedBox(height: 4),
        ClipRRect(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(3),
          child: SizedBox(
            width: 140,
            height: 9,
            child: _darkBase
                ? DecoratedBox(
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      gradient: LinearGradient(colors: [
                        for (final v in const [35, 50, 60, 70, 80, 90, 100]) noiseHeat(v.toDouble(), glow: true)
                      ]),
                    ),
                  )
                : Row(children: [for (final c in _bandColors) Expanded(child: ColoredBox(color: c, child: const SizedBox.expand()))]),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 2),
        const SizedBox(
          width: 140,
          child: Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween, children: [
            Text('<40', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 9)),
            Text('60', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 9)),
            Text('80+', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 9)),
          ]),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 2),
        if (_showRoads)
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 2),
            child: Row(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
              Container(width: 18, height: 3, decoration: BoxDecoration(color: const Color(0xFF22C55E), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2))),
              const SizedBox(width: 6),
              const Text('Routes · vert = non mesuré', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 9)),
            ]),
          ),
        Text(_darkBase ? 'Plus clair = plus bruyant' : 'Plus foncé = plus bruyant',
            style: TextStyle(fontSize: 9, color: cs.onSurfaceVariant)),
      ]),
    );
  }

  Widget _timelapseBar(ColorScheme cs) {
    final tl = _tl;
    final hasFrames = tl != null && tl.frameCount > 0;
    return SoftCard(
      glass: true,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(14, 10, 8, 10),
      child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
        Row(children: [
          Icon(Icons.history_rounded, color: cs.primary),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          Expanded(
            child: ValueListenableBuilder<double>(
              valueListenable: _tlPos,
              builder: (_, p, __) {
                final label = hasFrames ? tl.labels[p.round().clamp(0, tl.frameCount - 1)] : 'Historique';
                return Text(label, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 15));
              },
            ),
          ),
          ChoiceChip(
            label: const Text('Jour'),
            selected: _tlStep == 'day',
            onSelected: (_) {
              if (_tlStep != 'day') _openTimelapse('day');
            },
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 6),
          ChoiceChip(
            label: const Text('Plage'),
            selected: _tlStep == 'daypart',
            onSelected: (_) {
              if (_tlStep != 'daypart') _openTimelapse('daypart');
            },
          ),
          IconButton(onPressed: _closeTimelapse, icon: const Icon(Icons.close_rounded)),
        ]),
        if (_tlLoading)
          const Padding(
            padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: 6, right: 6),
            child: LinearProgressIndicator(minHeight: 3),
          )
        else if (_tlError != null)
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 6, right: 6),
            child: Text(_tlError!, style: TextStyle(color: cs.error, fontSize: 13)),
          )
        else if (!hasFrames)
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 6, right: 6),
            child: Text('Aucune donnée sur cette période et cette zone.',
                style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 13)),
          )
        else ...[
          if (tl.frameCount >= 2)
            ValueListenableBuilder<double>(
              valueListenable: _tlPos,
              builder: (_, p, __) => Slider(
                value: p.clamp(0.0, tl.frameCount - 1.0).toDouble(),
                min: 0,
                max: tl.frameCount - 1.0,
                onChanged: _tlSet,
              ),
            ),
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 6),
            child: Row(children: [
              IconButton.filledTonal(
                onPressed: () => setState(() => _tlPlaying = !_tlPlaying),
                icon: Icon(_tlPlaying ? Icons.pause_rounded : Icons.play_arrow_rounded),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              SegmentedButton<double>(
                segments: const [
                  ButtonSegment(value: 1.0, label: Text('×1')),
                  ButtonSegment(value: 2.0, label: Text('×2')),
                  ButtonSegment(value: 4.0, label: Text('×4')),
                ],
                selected: {_tlSpeed},
                onSelectionChanged: (v) => setState(() => _tlSpeed = v.first),
                showSelectedIcon: false,
                style: const ButtonStyle(visualDensity: VisualDensity.compact),
              ),
              const Spacer(),
              if (tl.minDb != null && tl.maxDb != null)
                Text('${tl.minDb!.toStringAsFixed(0)}–${tl.maxDb!.toStringAsFixed(0)} dB(A)',
                    style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12)),
            ]),
          ),
        ],
      ]),
    );
  }
}

class _LiveDot extends StatelessWidget {
  const _LiveDot({required this.clock});
  final Animation<double> clock;
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => AnimatedBuilder(
        animation: clock,
        builder: (_, __) {
          final a = 0.4 + 0.6 * (0.5 - 0.5 * cos(2 * pi * clock.value));
          return Container(
            width: 8,
            height: 8,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: const Color(0xFF22C55E).withValues(alpha: a),
              shape: BoxShape.circle,
              boxShadow: [BoxShadow(color: const Color(0xFF22C55E).withValues(alpha: a * 0.6), blurRadius: 4)],
            ),
          );
        },
      );
}

class _FiltersSheet extends StatefulWidget {
  const _FiltersSheet({required this.initial, required this.onApply});
  final MapFilters initial;
  final Function(MapFilters) onApply;

  @override
  State<_FiltersSheet> createState() => _FiltersSheetState();
}

class _FiltersSheetState extends State<_FiltersSheet> {
  late MapFilters f = widget.initial;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return SafeArea(
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 4, 20, 20),
        child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
          Text('Filtres de la carte',
              style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge?.copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          const Text('Période', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Wrap(spacing: 8, children: [
            for (final p in const [('today', "Aujourd'hui"), ('7d', '7 jours'), ('30d', '30 jours')])
              ChoiceChip(
                label: Text(p.$2),
                selected: f.period == p.$1,
                onSelected: (_) => setState(() => f = f.copyWith(period: p.$1)),
              ),
          ]),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          const Text('Plage horaire', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Wrap(spacing: 8, children: [
            for (final d in const ['all', 'morning', 'afternoon', 'evening', 'night'])
              ChoiceChip(
                label: Text(dayPartLabel(d)),
                selected: f.dayPart == d,
                onSelected: (_) => setState(() => f = f.copyWith(dayPart: d)),
              ),
          ]),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          SwitchListTile(
            contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
            title: const Text('Inclure les mesures indicatives'),
            value: f.includeIndicative,
            onChanged: (v) => setState(() => f = f.copyWith(includeIndicative: v)),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          FilledButton(
            onPressed: () {
              widget.onApply(f);
              Navigator.pop(context);
            },
            child: const Text('Appliquer'),
          ),
        ]),
      ),
    );
  }
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: ui/missions_screen.dart
################################################################################

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:latlong2/latlong.dart' hide Path;
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

import '../core/models_ext.dart';
import '../core/util.dart';
import '../state/missions_controller.dart';
import '../state/quota_controller.dart';
import 'proposals_screen.dart';
import 'widgets.dart';
import '../core/models_roads.dart';

class MissionsScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const MissionsScreen({super.key});
  @override
  State<MissionsScreen> createState() => _MissionsScreenState();
}

class _MissionsScreenState extends State<MissionsScreen> {
  Timer? _tick;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _tick = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 30), (_) {
      if (mounted) setState(() {});
    });
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      context.read<QuotaController>().refresh();
      context.read<MissionsController>().refresh();
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _tick?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _refresh() async {
    await context.read<QuotaController>().refresh();
    if (mounted) await context.read<MissionsController>().refresh();
  }

  Future<void> _respond(Mission m, bool accept) async {
    try {
      await context.read<MissionsController>().respond(m.id, accept);
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) showToast(context, errorMessage(e));
    }
  }

  String _left(DateTime t) {
    final d = t.difference(DateTime.now());
    if (d.isNegative) return 'expirée';
    if (d.inHours >= 24) return '${d.inDays} j ${d.inHours % 24} h';
    return '${d.inHours} h ${(d.inMinutes % 60).toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final q = context.watch<QuotaController>().state;
    final mc = context.watch<MissionsController>();
    final offered = mc.list.where((m) => m.status == 'offered').toList();
    final running = mc.list.where((m) => m.status == 'accepted').toList();
    final finished = mc.list.where((m) => !m.isActive).toList();

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('Missions et solde')),
      body: RefreshIndicator(
        onRefresh: _refresh,
        child: ListView(padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 4, 16, 40), children: [
          _quotaCard(cs, q),
          if (offered.isNotEmpty) ...[
            SectionTitle('À accepter (${offered.length})'),
            for (final m in offered) _card(cs, m),
          ],
          if (running.isNotEmpty) ...[
            SectionTitle('En cours (${running.length})'),
            for (final m in running) _card(cs, m),
          ],
          if (offered.isEmpty && running.isEmpty) ...[
            const SectionTitle('Missions'),
            SoftCard(
              child: Text(
                mc.loading
                    ? 'Recherche de zones à couvrir…'
                    : 'Aucune mission pour le moment. Elles sont générées selon les zones en manque de mesures près de vous, votre fiabilité et votre historique.',
                style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant),
              ),
            ),
          ],
          if (finished.isNotEmpty) ...[
            const SectionTitle('Récentes'),
            for (final m in finished) _card(cs, m),
          ],
          const SectionTitle('Autres'),
          SoftCard(
            padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
            child: ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.notifications_active_outlined),
              title: const Text('Propositions de mesure'),
              trailing: const Icon(Icons.chevron_right_rounded),
              onTap: () => Navigator.of(context).push(MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const ProposalsScreen())),
            ),
          ),
        ]),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _quotaCard(ColorScheme cs, QuotaState? q) {
    if (q == null) {
      return const SoftCard(child: Center(child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(24), child: CircularProgressIndicator())));
    }
    final ratio = q.cap <= 0 ? 0.0 : (q.balance / q.cap).clamp(0.0, 1.0).toDouble();
    final low = q.balance < 1;
    final col = low ? const Color(0xFFEF4444) : cs.primary;
    return SoftCard(
      child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
        Row(children: [
          ScoreRing(
            value: ratio,
            color: col,
            size: 92,
            stroke: 10,
            child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
              Text(q.balance.toStringAsFixed(1), style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 22, color: col)),
              Text('crédits', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10, color: cs.onSurfaceVariant)),
            ]),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 16),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
              Text('${q.measurementsLeft} prélèvement(s) disponible(s)',
                  style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 16)),
              const SizedBox(height: 4),
              Text(
                'Recharge actuelle : +${q.ratePerHour.toStringAsFixed(2)} crédit/h · objectif du jour ${q.dailyTarget.toStringAsFixed(1)}',
                style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12),
              ),
            ]),
          ),
        ]),
        const SizedBox(height: 14),
        Wrap(spacing: 8, runSpacing: 8, children: [
          StatusChip('Fiabilité ${(q.reliability * 100).round()} %', const Color(0xFF22C55E), icon: Icons.verified_rounded),
          StatusChip('Utilisation ${(q.utilization * 100).round()} %', cs.primary, icon: Icons.speed_rounded),
          StatusChip('Missions ${(q.missionRate * 100).round()} %', const Color(0xFFF59E0B), icon: Icons.flag_rounded),
        ]),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        Text(
          'Aujourd\'hui : +${q.todayCredited.toStringAsFixed(1)} reçus · −${q.todayUsed.toStringAsFixed(1)} utilisés'
          '${q.todayDecayed > 0.05 ? ' · −${q.todayDecayed.toStringAsFixed(1)} perdus' : ''}',
          style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 13),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 6),
        Text(
          'Le solde se cumule mais diminue si vous ne l\'utilisez pas (conservation ${(q.retentionPerDay * 100).round()} % par jour). '
          'Sans mesure, il serait de ${q.projected24h.toStringAsFixed(1)} dans 24 h. '
          'Mesurer dans une zone en manque coûte moins cher ; refaire une mesure au même endroit coûte plus et impose un délai.',
          style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12, height: 1.35),
        ),
      ]),
    );
  }

  Widget _card(ColorScheme cs, Mission m) {
    final offered = m.status == 'offered';
    final running = m.status == 'accepted';
    final col = running
        ? cs.primary
        : offered
            ? const Color(0xFFF59E0B)
            : m.status == 'completed'
                ? const Color(0xFF22C55E)
                : cs.onSurfaceVariant;
    final label = switch (m.status) {
      'offered' => 'À accepter',
      'accepted' => 'En cours',
      'completed' => 'Terminée',
      'failed' => 'Échouée',
      'declined' => 'Refusée',
      _ => 'Expirée',
    };
    final progress = m.requiredCount == 0 ? 0.0 : (m.confirmedCount / m.requiredCount).clamp(0.0, 1.0).toDouble();
    final pending = (m.doneCount - m.confirmedCount).clamp(0, 999);

    return SoftCard(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
      child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
        Row(children: [
          Icon(Icons.flag_rounded, color: col),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          Expanded(
            child: Text(
              m.isRoad
                  ? (m.roadName ?? roadClassLabel(m.roadClass))
                  : '${m.requiredCount} mesure(s) · à ${m.distanceKm.toStringAsFixed(1)} km',
              maxLines: 2,
              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
              style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
          ),
          StatusChip(label, col),
        ]),
        const SizedBox(height: 10),
        Wrap(spacing: 8, runSpacing: 6, children: [
          StatusChip('+${m.rewardCredits.toStringAsFixed(1)} crédit(s)', const Color(0xFF22C55E), icon: Icons.bolt_rounded),
          StatusChip('Priorité ${m.priority.round()}/100', cs.primary),
          StatusChip('Zone ${(m.radiusM / 1000).toStringAsFixed(1)} km', cs.onSurfaceVariant),
        ]),

        if (m.isRoad) ...[
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Wrap(spacing: 8, runSpacing: 6, children: [
            StatusChip(roadClassLabel(m.roadClass), cs.primary, icon: Icons.add_road_rounded),
            if (m.targetDayPart != null) StatusChip('Plage : ${dayPartLabel(m.targetDayPart!)}', const Color(0xFF7C3AED), icon: Icons.schedule_rounded),
            StatusChip('à ${m.distanceKm.toStringAsFixed(1)} km', cs.onSurfaceVariant),
          ]),
          if (m.windowEndAt != null)
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 6),
              child: Text(
                  'Mesurez à moins de ${m.radiusM.round()} m du point cible, avant ${fmtTime(m.windowEndAt!)}. Pas besoin d\'être pile dessus.',
                  style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12)),
            ),
        ],
        if (running || m.status == 'completed') ...[
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          ClipRRect(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
            child: LinearProgressIndicator(minHeight: 8, value: progress),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 4),
          Text(
            '${m.confirmedCount}/${m.requiredCount} validée(s)${pending > 0 ? ' · $pending en évaluation' : ''}',
            style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12),
          ),
        ],
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        if (offered)
          Text('Expire dans ${_left(m.offerExpiresAt)}', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12))
        else if (running && m.deadlineAt != null)
          Text('À terminer dans ${_left(m.deadlineAt!)}', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12)),

        if (m.isActive) ...[
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          Row(children: [
            Expanded(
              child: OutlinedButton.icon(
                icon: const Icon(Icons.map_outlined),
                label: const Text('Voir'),
                onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, MissionFocus(m)),
              ),
            ),
            if (m.isRoad) ...[
              const SizedBox(width: 10),
              Expanded(
                child: FilledButton.icon(
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.directions_rounded),
                  label: const Text('Itinéraire'),
                  onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, MissionFocus(m, route: true)),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ]),
        ],

        if (offered) ...[
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Row(children: [
            Expanded(child: OutlinedButton(onPressed: () => _respond(m, false), child: const Text('Refuser'))),
            const SizedBox(width: 10),
            Expanded(child: FilledButton(onPressed: () => _respond(m, true), child: const Text('Accepter'))),
          ]),
        ],
      ]),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_map/flutter_map.dart';
import 'package:latlong2/latlong.dart' hide Path;
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

import '../core/api.dart';
import '../core/api_ext.dart';
import '../core/config.dart';
import '../core/models_field.dart';
import '../core/util.dart';
import '../state/recorder_controller.dart';
import 'map_screen.dart' show noiseBandColor;
import 'widgets.dart';

MapOptions _fit(List<LatLng> ll, {required bool interactive}) {
  final b = LatLngBounds.fromPoints(ll);
  final tiny = (b.north - b.south) < 1e-4 && (b.east - b.west) < 1e-4;
  final flags = interactive ? InteractiveFlag.all & ~InteractiveFlag.rotate : InteractiveFlag.none;
  if (tiny) {
    return MapOptions(
        initialCenter: ll.first,
        initialZoom: 16,
        minZoom: 3,
        maxZoom: 19,
        interactionOptions: InteractionOptions(flags: flags));
  }
  return MapOptions(
      initialCameraFit: CameraFit.bounds(bounds: b, padding: const EdgeInsets.all(44), maxZoom: 16),
      minZoom: 3,
      maxZoom: 19,
      interactionOptions: InteractionOptions(flags: flags));
}

TileLayer _tiles() => TileLayer(
      urlTemplate: AppConfig.tileLight,
      subdomains: const ['a', 'b', 'c', 'd'],
      userAgentPackageName: AppConfig.packageName,
      maxNativeZoom: 19,
    );

PolygonLayer _cellLayer(List<ImpactCell> cells) => PolygonLayer(polygons: [
      for (final c in cells)
        Polygon(
          points: c.polygon,
          color: c.visible && c.mapLaeq != null
              ? noiseBandColor(c.mapLaeq!).withValues(alpha: 0.72)
              : Colors.grey.withValues(alpha: 0.22),
          borderColor: c.visible && c.mapLaeq != null ? noiseBandColor(c.mapLaeq!) : Colors.grey,
          borderStrokeWidth: 1.6,
        ),
    ]);

class _Dot extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Dot(this.p, {this.size = 24, this.selected = false});
  final ImpactPoint p;
  final double size;
  final bool selected;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final col = noiseBandColor(p.laeq);
    final txt = col.computeLuminance() > 0.5 ? Colors.black87 : Colors.white;
    return Container(
      width: size,
      height: size,
      alignment: Alignment.center,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: col,
        shape: BoxShape.circle,
        border: Border.all(color: selected ? Colors.black : qualityColor(p.status), width: selected ? 3 : 2.5),
        boxShadow: const [BoxShadow(color: Colors.black38, blurRadius: 4)],
      ),
      child: Text('${p.laeq.round()}',
          style: TextStyle(fontSize: size * 0.38, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: txt)),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================================
// Carte de profil
// ============================================================================
class MyMappingCard extends StatefulWidget {
  const MyMappingCard({super.key});
  @override
  State<MyMappingCard> createState() => _MyMappingCardState();
}

class _MyMappingCardState extends State<MyMappingCard> {
  Impact? _impact;
  String? _error;
  bool _loading = true;
  int _seen = -1;

  Future<void> _load() async {
    setState(() => _loading = true);
    try {
      final r = await context.read<ApiClient>().myImpact();
      if (mounted) setState(() {
        _impact = r;
        _error = null;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _error = errorMessage(e));
    }
    if (mounted) setState(() => _loading = false);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final version = context.select<RecorderController, int>((r) => r.completedVersion);
    if (version != _seen) {
      _seen = version;
      WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
        if (mounted) _load();
      });
    }
    final im = _impact;
    if (im == null) {
      return SoftCard(
        child: _loading
            ? const Center(child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(20), child: CircularProgressIndicator()))
            : Row(children: [
                Icon(Icons.cloud_off_rounded, color: cs.error),
                const SizedBox(width: 12),
                Expanded(child: Text(_error ?? 'Indisponible', style: TextStyle(color: cs.error))),
                TextButton(onPressed: _load, child: const Text('Réessayer')),
              ]),
      );
    }
    if (im.points.isEmpty) {
      return SoftCard(
        child: Row(children: [
          Icon(Icons.map_outlined, color: cs.primary, size: 32),
          const SizedBox(width: 14),
          Expanded(
            child: Text('Aucune mesure sur les 30 derniers jours. Vos mesures apparaîtront ici avec leur effet sur la carte générale.',
                style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant)),
          ),
        ]),
      );
    }
    final ll = [for (final p in im.points) LatLng(p.lat, p.lon)];
    final deltas = [for (final c in im.cells) if (c.delta != null) c.delta!];
    final avgDelta = deltas.isEmpty ? null : deltas.reduce((a, b) => a + b) / deltas.length;
    return SoftCard(
      padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
      onTap: () => Navigator.of(context).push(MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => MyMappingScreen(impact: im))),
      child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
        ClipRRect(
          borderRadius: const BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
          child: SizedBox(
            height: 210,
            child: Stack(children: [
              IgnorePointer(
                child: FlutterMap(
                  key: ValueKey('${im.total}_${im.onMap}'),
                  options: _fit(ll, interactive: false),
                  children: [
                    _tiles(),
                    _cellLayer(im.cells),
                    MarkerLayer(markers: [
                      for (final p in im.points.take(120))
                        Marker(point: LatLng(p.lat, p.lon), width: 20, height: 20, child: _Dot(p, size: 20)),
                    ]),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              Positioned(right: 10, top: 10, child: StatusChip('Détails', cs.primary, icon: Icons.open_in_full_rounded)),
            ]),
          ),
        ),
        Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
          child: Wrap(spacing: 8, runSpacing: 8, children: [
            StatusChip('${im.total} mesure(s) · 30 j', cs.primary, icon: Icons.place_rounded),
            StatusChip('${im.count('validated')} validée(s)', qualityColor('validated')),
            if (im.count('pending') > 0) StatusChip('${im.count('pending')} en évaluation', qualityColor('pending')),
            StatusChip('${im.visibleCells} zone(s) visibles sur la carte', const Color(0xFF0E9F9A), icon: Icons.layers_rounded),
            if (avgDelta != null)
              StatusChip('Effet moyen ${avgDelta >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${avgDelta.toStringAsFixed(1)} dB', cs.onSurfaceVariant),
          ]),
        ),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================================
// Écran détaillé
// ============================================================================
class MyMappingScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const MyMappingScreen({super.key, required this.impact});
  final Impact impact;
  @override
  State<MyMappingScreen> createState() => _MyMappingScreenState();
}

class _MyMappingScreenState extends State<MyMappingScreen> {
  final MapController _ctl = MapController();
  String? _sel;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final im = widget.impact;
    final ll = [for (final p in im.points) LatLng(p.lat, p.lon)];

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('Ma cartographie')),
      body: Column(children: [
        Expanded(
          flex: 5,
          child: Stack(children: [
            FlutterMap(
              mapController: _ctl,
              options: _fit(ll, interactive: true),
              children: [
                _tiles(),
                _cellLayer(im.cells),
                MarkerLayer(markers: [
                  for (final p in im.points)
                    Marker(
                      point: LatLng(p.lat, p.lon),
                      width: _sel == p.id ? 36 : 26,
                      height: _sel == p.id ? 36 : 26,
                      child: GestureDetector(
                        onTap: () => setState(() => _sel = p.id),
                        child: _Dot(p, size: _sel == p.id ? 36 : 26, selected: _sel == p.id),
                      ),
                    ),
                ]),
              ],
            ),
            Positioned(
              left: 12,
              bottom: 12,
              child: SoftCard(
                glass: true,
                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(10, 8, 10, 8),
                child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
                  Row(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
                    Container(width: 12, height: 12, color: const Color(0xFFE8884F)),
                    const SizedBox(width: 6),
                    const Text('Zone visible sur la carte', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10)),
                  ]),
                  const SizedBox(height: 3),
                  Row(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
                    Container(width: 12, height: 12, color: Colors.grey.withValues(alpha: 0.4)),
                    const SizedBox(width: 6),
                    const Text('Pas encore visible', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10)),
                  ]),
                  const SizedBox(height: 3),
                  Text('Contour du point : statut de validation', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 9, color: cs.onSurfaceVariant)),
                ]),
              ),
            ),
          ]),
        ),
        Expanded(
          flex: 4,
          child: ListView(padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 12, 16, 24), children: [
            Text(
              'La carte générale est la même pour tous : elle est calculée par le serveur à partir des mesures validées de tous les contributeurs. Voici la part de vos mesures.',
              style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12, height: 1.35),
            ),
            const SectionTitle('Impact par zone'),
            for (final c in im.cells) _cellTile(cs, c, im),
            const SectionTitle('Mes mesures'),
            for (final p in im.points) _pointTile(cs, p),
          ]),
        ),
      ]),
    );
  }

  Widget _cellTile(ColorScheme cs, ImpactCell c, Impact im) {
    final String line1, line2;
    if (c.visible && c.mapLaeq != null) {
      line1 = 'Carte : ${c.mapLaeq!.toStringAsFixed(1)} dB(A) · ${c.mapCount} mesure(s) · ${c.mapDevices} contributeur(s)';
      final share = c.share == null ? '' : 'Votre part ${(c.share! * 100).round()} %';
      final d = c.delta == null ? '' : ' · effet de vos mesures ${c.delta! >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${c.delta!.toStringAsFixed(1)} dB';
      line2 = '$share$d';
    } else if (c.myValidated == 0) {
      line1 = 'Pas encore sur la carte';
      line2 = 'Vos ${c.myCount} mesure(s) attendent la validation.';
    } else {
      line1 = 'Pas encore sur la carte';
      line2 = 'Au moins ${im.minDevices} contributeurs sont nécessaires pour afficher cette zone.';
    }
    final col = c.visible && c.mapLaeq != null ? noiseBandColor(c.mapLaeq!) : Colors.grey;
    return SoftCard(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
      onTap: () => _ctl.move(c.center, 16.5),
      child: Row(children: [
        Container(
          width: 44,
          height: 44,
          alignment: Alignment.center,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(color: col.withValues(alpha: 0.85), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
          child: Text(c.mapLaeq == null ? '–' : c.mapLaeq!.round().toString(),
              style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: col.computeLuminance() > 0.5 ? Colors.black87 : Colors.white)),
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 12),
        Expanded(
          child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
            Text(line1, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 13)),
            if (line2.isNotEmpty) Text(line2, style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12)),
            Text('${c.myCount} mesure(s) de vous · ${c.myLaeq.toStringAsFixed(1)} dB(A) en moyenne énergétique',
                style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 11)),
          ]),
        ),
      ]),
    );
  }

  Widget _pointTile(ColorScheme cs, ImpactPoint p) {
    return SoftCard(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
      onTap: () {
        setState(() => _sel = p.id);
        _ctl.move(LatLng(p.lat, p.lon), 17);
      },
      child: Row(children: [
        _Dot(p, size: 38, selected: _sel == p.id),
        const SizedBox(width: 12),
        Expanded(
          child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
            Text(fmtDateTime(p.measuredAt), style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
            Text('${noiseLabel(p.laeq)} · ${fmtAgo(p.measuredAt)}', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12)),
          ]),
        ),
        StatusChip(qualityLabel(p.status), qualityColor(p.status)),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:fl_chart/fl_chart.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class ProfileScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const ProfileScreen({super.key});
  @override
  State<ProfileScreen> createState() => _ProfileScreenState();
}

class _ProfileScreenState extends State<ProfileScreen> {
  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      context.read<HistoryViewModel>().reload();
      context.read<AccountController>().loadAll();
    });
  }

  Future<void> _refresh() async {
    await context.read<HistoryViewModel>().reload();
    if (mounted) await context.read<AccountController>().loadAll();
  }

  Widget _kpi(BuildContext context, IconData i, String v, String l) {
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(color: cs.surfaceContainerHighest, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
      child: Row(children: [
        Icon(i, color: cs.primary),
        const SizedBox(width: 10),
        Expanded(
          child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
            Text(v, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 18)),
            Text(l, style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 11)),
          ]),
        ),
      ]),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final s = context.watch<SessionController>();
    final h = context.watch<HistoryViewModel>();
    final proposals = context.watch<ProposalsController>();
    final acc = context.watch<AccountController>();
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final u = s.user!;
    final stats = h.stats;
    final p = acc.profile;
    final board = acc.board;
    final name = p?.displayName ?? u.pseudonym;
    final level = p?.level ?? board?.level;
    final season = board?.periods['season'];
    final week = board?.periods['week'];

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('Moi'), actions: [
        IconButton(icon: const Icon(Icons.settings_rounded), onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).push(MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const SettingsScreen()))),
      ]),
      body: RefreshIndicator(
        onRefresh: _refresh,
        child: ListView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 4, 16, 120),
          physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
          children: [
            // ---------------------------------------------------------------- en-tête
            SoftCard(
              child: Column(children: [
                Row(children: [
                  if (level != null) LevelAvatar(name, level, radius: 30) else CircleAvatar(radius: 30, child: Text(name.characters.first.toUpperCase())),
                  const SizedBox(width: 16),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
                      Text(name, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 19)),
                      if (p != null && (p.city != null || p.district != null))
                        Text([p.district, p.city].whereType<String>().join(', '), style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 13)),
                      const SizedBox(height: 6),
                      Wrap(spacing: 6, runSpacing: 4, children: [
                        if (level != null) LevelChip(level),
                        StatusChip(u.roleLabel, cs.primary),
                        if (p?.analystEnabled ?? false) const StatusChip('Analyseur', Color(0xFF7C3AED), icon: Icons.hearing_rounded),
                        if (u.participationPaused) const StatusChip('En pause', Color(0xFFF59E0B)),
                      ]),
                    ]),
                  ),
                ]),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                Row(children: [
                  Expanded(child: OutlinedButton.icon(icon: const Icon(Icons.edit_outlined), label: const Text('Modifier'), onPressed: p == null ? null : () => Navigator.of(context).push(MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => EditProfileScreen(profile: p))))),
                  const SizedBox(width: 10),
                  Expanded(child: FilledButton.icon(icon: const Icon(Icons.leaderboard_rounded), label: const Text('Classement'), onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).push(MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const LeaderboardScreen())))),
                ]),
                if (p?.memberSince != null) Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8), child: Text('Membre depuis le ${fmtDay(p!.memberSince!)}', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12))),
              ]),
            ),

            // ---------------------------------------------------------------- guide
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            _GuideCard(onTap: () => Navigator.of(context).push(MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const GuideScreen()))),

            // ---------------------------------------------------------------- niveau + badges
            if (board != null) ...[
              const SectionTitle('Niveau et badges'),
              _LevelCard(board: board, week: week),
            ],

            // ---------------------------------------------------------------- contributions
            const SectionTitle('Mes contributions'),
            SoftCard(
              child: stats == null
                  ? const Center(child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(24), child: CircularProgressIndicator()))
                  : Column(children: [
                      Row(children: [
                        SizedBox(
                          width: 130,
                          height: 130,
                          child: stats.total == 0
                              ? const ScoreRing(value: 0, color: Colors.grey, size: 130, stroke: 14)
                              : PieChart(PieChartData(sectionsSpace: 3, centerSpaceRadius: 38, sections: [
                                  for (final k in const ['validated', 'indicative', 'rejected', 'pending'])
                                    if (stats.count(k) > 0) PieChartSectionData(value: stats.count(k).toDouble(), color: qualityColor(k), radius: 22, showTitle: false),
                                ])),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: 18),
                        Expanded(
                          child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
                            Text('${stats.total}', style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 38, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, height: 1)),
                            Text('mesure(s)', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant)),
                            const SizedBox(height: 10),
                            for (final k in const ['validated', 'indicative', 'rejected', 'pending'])
                              Padding(
                                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 2),
                                child: Row(children: [
                                  Container(width: 10, height: 10, decoration: BoxDecoration(color: qualityColor(k), shape: BoxShape.circle)),
                                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                                  Expanded(child: Text(qualityLabel(k), style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13))),
                                  Text('${stats.count(k)}', style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
                                ]),
                              ),
                          ]),
                        ),
                      ]),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      Row(children: [
                        Expanded(child: _kpi(context, Icons.hexagon_outlined, '${stats.cellsCovered}', 'Zones couvertes')),
                        const SizedBox(width: 10),
                        Expanded(child: _kpi(context, Icons.verified_rounded, '${(stats.validationRate * 100).round()} %', 'Taux de validation')),
                      ]),
                      if (stats.lastMeasurementAt != null) Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 12), child: Text('Dernière mesure : ${fmtAgo(stats.lastMeasurementAt!)}', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 13))),
                    ]),
            ),

            const SectionTitle('Ma cartographie'),
            const MyMappingCard(),

            const SectionTitle('Notation et historique'),
            _ScoreCard(board: board, season: season),

            const SectionTitle('Analyseur'),
            const _AnalystCard(),

            const SectionTitle('Invitations'),
            const _InviteCard(),

            const SectionTitle('Connexion et sécurité'),
            const _SecurityCard(),

            // ---------------------------------------------------------------- collecte hybride
            const SectionTitle('Collecte hybride'),
            SoftCard(
              padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
              child: Column(children: [
                SwitchListTile(
                  secondary: const Icon(Icons.auto_awesome_rounded),
                  title: const Text('Propositions de mesure', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
                  subtitle: const Text('Recevoir des suggestions de zones à mesurer.'),
                  value: u.hybridCollectionEnabled,
                  onChanged: (v) async {
                    try {
                      await s.setHybrid(v);
                      if (v) proposals.startPolling();
                    } catch (e) {
                      if (context.mounted) showToast(context, errorMessage(e));
                    }
                  },
                ),
                const Divider(height: 1),
                ListTile(
                  leading: const Icon(Icons.notifications_active_outlined),
                  title: const Text('Mes propositions'),
                  trailing: proposals.list.isEmpty ? const Icon(Icons.chevron_right_rounded) : Badge(label: Text('${proposals.list.length}')),
                  onTap: () => Navigator.of(context).push(MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const ProposalsScreen())),
                ),
                const Divider(height: 1),
                ListTile(
                  leading: const Icon(Icons.event_available_rounded),
                  title: const Text('Mon planning'),
                  trailing: const Icon(Icons.chevron_right_rounded),
                  onTap: () => Navigator.of(context).push(MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const ScheduleScreen())),
                ),
              ]),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

  @override

      color: Colors.transparent,
      child: InkWell(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        onTap: onTap,
