            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
            gradient: const LinearGradient(colors: [Color(0xFF0B1F2A), Color(0xFF0E7C7B)], begin: Alignment.topLeft, end: Alignment.bottomRight),
          ),
          child: Row(children: [
            Container(width: 48, height: 48, decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.16), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(15)), child: const Icon(Icons.menu_book_rounded, color: Colors.white)),
            const SizedBox(width: 14),
            const Expanded(
              child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
                Text('Guide complet', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 17)),
                SizedBox(height: 2),
                Text('Comment ça marche : mesures, carte, routes, missions, points, vie privée.', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 12.5, height: 1.3)),
              ]),
            ),
            const Icon(Icons.chevron_right_rounded, color: Colors.white),
          ]),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================================ niveau + badges
class _LevelCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _LevelCard({required this.board, this.week});
  final MyBoard board;
  final PeriodScore? week;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final lv = board.level;
    final col = levelColor(lv.index);
    return SoftCard(
      child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
        Row(children: [
          ScoreRing(value: lv.progress, color: col, size: 78, stroke: 8, child: Text('${lv.index + 1}', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 24, color: col))),
          const SizedBox(width: 16),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
              Text(lv.name, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 19)),
              Text(lv.nextXp == null ? '${lv.xp} XP · niveau maximum' : '${lv.xp} / ${lv.nextXp} XP', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant)),
              const SizedBox(height: 6),
              ClipRRect(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(5), child: LinearProgressIndicator(minHeight: 8, value: lv.progress, color: col, backgroundColor: cs.outlineVariant.withValues(alpha: 0.35))),
            ]),
          ),
        ]),
        if (week != null && week!.rank > 0) ...[
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Wrap(spacing: 8, children: [
            StatusChip('Rang semaine : #${week!.rank}', cs.primary, icon: Icons.emoji_events_rounded),
            StatusChip('${week!.score.toStringAsFixed(0)} pts', const Color(0xFF22C55E)),
          ]),
        ],
        const SizedBox(height: 14),
        const Text('Badges', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        Wrap(spacing: 10, runSpacing: 10, children: [for (final b in board.badges) _BadgeTile(b)]),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

class _BadgeTile extends StatelessWidget {
  const _BadgeTile(this.b);
  final BadgeInfo b;
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final col = b.earned ? const [Color(0xFF94A3B8), Color(0xFF0E9F9A), Color(0xFF2563EB), Color(0xFF7C3AED), Color(0xFFF59E0B), Color(0xFFDB2777)][b.tier.clamp(0, 5)] : cs.outlineVariant;
    return InkWell(
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
      onTap: () => showDialog<void>(
        context: context,
        builder: (_) => AlertDialog(
          icon: Icon(badgeIcon(b.icon), color: col, size: 36),
          title: Text(b.label),
          content: Text('${b.description}\n\n${b.earned ? 'Obtenu${b.awardedAt != null ? ' le ${fmtDay(b.awardedAt!)}' : ''}.' : 'Pas encore obtenu.'}'),
          actions: [TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context), child: const Text('OK'))],
        ),
      ),
      child: SizedBox(
        width: 72,
        child: Column(children: [
          Stack(alignment: Alignment.center, children: [
            Container(width: 52, height: 52, decoration: BoxDecoration(color: col.withValues(alpha: b.earned ? 0.18 : 0.12), shape: BoxShape.circle, border: Border.all(color: col, width: 2)), child: Icon(badgeIcon(b.icon), color: col)),
            if (!b.earned) Positioned(right: 0, bottom: 0, child: Icon(Icons.lock_rounded, size: 16, color: cs.onSurfaceVariant)),
          ]),
          const SizedBox(height: 4),
          Text(b.label, textAlign: TextAlign.center, maxLines: 2, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis, style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600, color: b.earned ? null : cs.onSurfaceVariant)),
        ]),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================================ notation
class _ScoreCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _ScoreCard({required this.board, required this.season});
  final MyBoard? board;
  final PeriodScore? season;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final b = board;
    if (b == null) return const SoftCard(child: Center(child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(20), child: CircularProgressIndicator())));
    const labels = {'week': 'Semaine', 'month': 'Mois', 'season': 'Saison (90 j)'};
    final comps = <(String, String, num)>[
      ('Mesures validées', 'v', 10), ('Mesures indicatives', 'i', 4), ('Mesures rejetées', 'r', -2),
      ('Zones couvertes', 'cells', 3), ("Jours d'activité", 'days', 5), ('Missions réussies', 'missions', 40),
      ('Mesures de mission', 'units', 4), ('Avis concordants', 'reviews', 2), ('Filleuls actifs', 'invites', 25),
    ];
    final sc = season;
    final hist = b.history;
    return SoftCard(
      child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
        for (final k in const ['week', 'month', 'season'])
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 5),
            child: Row(children: [
              SizedBox(width: 100, child: Text(labels[k]!, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700))),
              Expanded(
                child: b.periods[k] == null
                    ? Text('Pas encore classé', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 13))
                    : Text(b.periods[k]!.rank == 0 ? 'Rang masqué' : 'Rang #${b.periods[k]!.rank}', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 13)),
              ),
              if (b.periods[k] != null) Text('${b.periods[k]!.score.toStringAsFixed(0)} pts', style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
            ]),
          ),
        if (sc != null) ...[
          const Divider(height: 24),
          const Text('Comment votre score de saison est calculé', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          for (final c in comps)
            if (sc.comp(c.$2) != 0)
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 2),
                child: Row(children: [
                  Expanded(child: Text(c.$1, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13))),
                  Text('${sc.comp(c.$2).round()} × ${c.$3}', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12)),
                  SizedBox(width: 64, child: Text('${(sc.comp(c.$2) * c.$3).round()} pts', textAlign: TextAlign.right, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 13))),
                ]),
              ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Row(children: [
            const Expanded(child: Text('Total brut', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700))),
            Text('${sc.comp('raw').round()} pts', style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
          ]),
          Row(children: [
            Expanded(child: Text('Fiabilité ${(sc.reliability * 100).round()} % → multiplicateur', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 13))),
            Text('× ${(0.6 + 0.8 * sc.reliability).toStringAsFixed(2)}', style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
          ]),
          const SizedBox(height: 4),
          Row(children: [
            const Expanded(child: Text('Score final', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800))),
            Text('${sc.score.toStringAsFixed(0)} pts', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 18, color: cs.primary)),
          ]),
        ],
        if (hist.length >= 2) ...[
          const Divider(height: 24),
          const Text('Évolution du score (saison)', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          SizedBox(
            height: 150,
            child: LineChart(LineChartData(
              gridData: FlGridData(drawVerticalLine: false, getDrawingHorizontalLine: (_) => FlLine(color: cs.outlineVariant.withValues(alpha: 0.4), strokeWidth: 1)),
              borderData: FlBorderData(show: false),
              titlesData: FlTitlesData(
                topTitles: const AxisTitles(sideTitles: SideTitles(showTitles: false)),
                rightTitles: const AxisTitles(sideTitles: SideTitles(showTitles: false)),
                leftTitles: AxisTitles(sideTitles: SideTitles(showTitles: true, reservedSize: 34, getTitlesWidget: (v, m) => Text(v.toStringAsFixed(0), style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 10)))),
                bottomTitles: AxisTitles(
                  sideTitles: SideTitles(
                    showTitles: true,
                    reservedSize: 22,
                    interval: (hist.length / 3).ceilToDouble().clamp(1, 100),
                    getTitlesWidget: (v, m) {
                      final i = v.toInt();
                      return i < 0 || i >= hist.length ? const SizedBox.shrink() : Text(fmtDay(hist[i].date), style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 10));
                    },
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              lineBarsData: [
                LineChartBarData(
                  spots: [for (var i = 0; i < hist.length; i++) FlSpot(i.toDouble(), hist[i].score)],
                  isCurved: true,
                  barWidth: 3,
                  color: cs.primary,
                  dotData: const FlDotData(show: false),
                  belowBarData: BarAreaData(show: true, color: cs.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.12)),
                ),
              ],
            )),
          ),
        ],
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        Align(
          alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
          child: TextButton.icon(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.leaderboard_rounded),
            label: const Text('Voir le classement'),
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).push(MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const LeaderboardScreen())),
          ),
        ),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================================ analyseur
class _AnalystCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _AnalystCard();
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final acc = context.watch<AccountController>();
    final rev = context.watch<ReviewsController>();
    final a = acc.analyst;
    if (a == null) return const SoftCard(child: Center(child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(20), child: CircularProgressIndicator())));
    final canToggle = a.enabled || a.eligible;
    return SoftCard(
      child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
        Row(children: [
          const Icon(Icons.hearing_rounded, color: Color(0xFF7C3AED)),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          const Expanded(child: Text("Donner mon avis sur les mesures proches", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800))),
          Switch(
            value: a.enabled,
            onChanged: !canToggle
                ? null
                : (v) async {
                    try {
                      await acc.setAnalyst(v);
                    } catch (e) {
                      if (context.mounted) showToast(context, errorMessage(e));
                    }
                  },
          ),
        ]),
        Text("Quand quelqu'un mesure près de vous, vous confirmez ou corrigez le niveau (10 min pour répondre).", style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12.5, height: 1.3)),
        if (a.suspended) ...[
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          StatusChip('Suspendu jusqu\'au ${fmtDay(a.suspendedUntil!)}', const Color(0xFFEF4444), icon: Icons.pause_circle_rounded),
        ],
        const SizedBox(height: 10),
        const Text('Conditions', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
        const SizedBox(height: 4),
        for (final c in a.criteria)
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 3),
            child: Row(children: [
              Icon(c.ok ? Icons.check_circle_rounded : Icons.radio_button_unchecked_rounded, size: 20, color: c.ok ? const Color(0xFF22C55E) : cs.onSurfaceVariant),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Expanded(child: Text(c.label, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13))),
              Text('${c.value} / ${c.reverse ? '≤' : '≥'} ${c.required}', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, color: c.ok ? const Color(0xFF22C55E) : cs.onSurfaceVariant)),
            ]),
          ),
        if (a.votes > 0) ...[
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Wrap(spacing: 8, children: [
            StatusChip('${a.votes} avis (60 j)', cs.primary),
            StatusChip('Concordance ${(a.agreement * 100).round()} %', const Color(0xFF22C55E)),
          ]),
        ],
        if (a.enabled) ...[
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          FilledButton.icon(
            icon: Badge(isLabelVisible: rev.pending.isNotEmpty, label: Text('${rev.pending.length}'), child: const Icon(Icons.rate_review_rounded)),
            label: const Text('Mes avis'),
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).push(MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const ReviewsScreen())),
          ),
        ],
      ]),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================================ invitations
class _InviteCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _InviteCard();
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final acc = context.watch<AccountController>();
    return SoftCard(
      child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
        Text("Invitez des amis : vous gagnez 25 points quand ils deviennent actifs (3 mesures validées).", style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12.5, height: 1.3)),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        if (acc.invitations.isEmpty) Text('Aucun code pour le moment.', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant)),
        for (final i in acc.invitations)
          ListTile(
            contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
            leading: Icon(Icons.card_giftcard_rounded, color: i.active ? cs.primary : cs.onSurfaceVariant),
            title: SelectableText(i.code, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, letterSpacing: 1.4, color: i.active ? null : cs.onSurfaceVariant)),
            subtitle: Text('${i.uses}/${i.maxUses} utilisation(s)${i.expiresAt != null ? ' · jusqu\'au ${fmtDay(i.expiresAt!)}' : ''}${i.active ? '' : ' · inactif'}', style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12)),
            trailing: Row(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
              IconButton(icon: const Icon(Icons.copy_rounded), onPressed: () { Clipboard.setData(ClipboardData(text: i.code)); showToast(context, 'Code copié'); }),
              if (i.active)
                IconButton(
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.block_rounded),
                  onPressed: () async {
                    try {
                      await acc.revoke(i.id);
                    } catch (e) {
                      if (context.mounted) showToast(context, errorMessage(e));
                    }
                  },
                ),
            ]),
          ),
        const SizedBox(height: 6),
        OutlinedButton.icon(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.add_rounded),
          label: const Text('Créer un code'),
          onPressed: () async {
            try {
              await acc.newInvitation();
            } catch (e) {
              if (context.mounted) showToast(context, errorMessage(e));
            }
          },
        ),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================================ sécurité
class _SecurityCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _SecurityCard();

  Future<void> _changePassword(BuildContext context) async {
    final cur = TextEditingController(), nw = TextEditingController();
    final key = GlobalKey<FormState>();
    final ok = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('Changer le mot de passe'),
        content: Form(
          key: key,
          child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
            TextFormField(controller: cur, obscureText: true, decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Mot de passe actuel'), validator: (v) => (v == null || v.isEmpty) ? 'Requis' : null),
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            TextFormField(controller: nw, obscureText: true, decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Nouveau mot de passe'), validator: (v) => passwordPolicyOk(v ?? '') ? null : '10 caractères min., lettres + chiffres, 3 types (ou 14 car.)'),
          ]),
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, false), child: const Text('Annuler')),
          FilledButton(style: FilledButton.styleFrom(minimumSize: const Size(100, 44)), onPressed: () { if (key.currentState!.validate()) Navigator.pop(ctx, true); }, child: const Text('Changer')),
        ],
      ),
    );
    final c = cur.text, n = nw.text;
    cur.dispose();
    nw.dispose();
    if (ok != true || !context.mounted) return;
    try {
      await context.read<ApiClient>().changePassword(c, n);
      if (!context.mounted) return;
      showToast(context, 'Mot de passe modifié. Reconnectez-vous.');
      await context.read<SessionController>().signOut();
    } catch (e) {
      if (context.mounted) showToast(context, errorMessage(e));
    }
  }

  Future<void> _newCodes(BuildContext context) async {
    final pw = TextEditingController();
    final ok = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('Nouveaux codes de récupération'),
        content: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
          const Text('Les anciens codes seront annulés. Confirmez avec votre mot de passe.', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13)),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          TextField(controller: pw, obscureText: true, decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Mot de passe')),
        ]),
        actions: [
          TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, false), child: const Text('Annuler')),
          FilledButton(style: FilledButton.styleFrom(minimumSize: const Size(100, 44)), onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, true), child: const Text('Générer')),
        ],
      ),
    );
    final password = pw.text;
    pw.dispose();
    if (ok != true || !context.mounted) return;
    try {
      final codes = await context.read<ApiClient>().regenerateRecoveryCodes(password);
      if (!context.mounted) return;
      await showDialog<void>(
        context: context,
        barrierDismissible: false,
        builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
          title: const Text('Vos nouveaux codes'),
          content: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
            const Text('Conservez-les en lieu sûr : ils ne seront plus affichés.', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13)),
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            SelectableText(codes.join('\n'), style: const TextStyle(fontFamily: 'monospace', fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 16, height: 1.6)),
          ]),
          actions: [
            TextButton(onPressed: () { Clipboard.setData(ClipboardData(text: codes.join('\n'))); showToast(ctx, 'Codes copiés'); }, child: const Text('Copier')),
            FilledButton(style: FilledButton.styleFrom(minimumSize: const Size(100, 44)), onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx), child: const Text("J'ai noté")),
          ],
        ),
      );
    } catch (e) {
      if (context.mounted) showToast(context, errorMessage(e));
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final c = context.watch<AccountController>().connections;
    return SoftCard(
      child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
        if (c == null)
          const Center(child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(16), child: CircularProgressIndicator()))
        else ...[
          if (c.lastLoginAt != null) Text('Dernière connexion : ${fmtDateTime(c.lastLoginAt!)}', style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Wrap(spacing: 8, runSpacing: 6, children: [
            StatusChip('${c.sessions.length} session(s) ouverte(s)', cs.primary, icon: Icons.devices_rounded),
            StatusChip('${c.devices.length} appareil(s)', cs.onSurfaceVariant, icon: Icons.smartphone_rounded),
          ]),
          if (c.devices.isNotEmpty) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            for (final d in c.devices) Text('• ${d.name} · Android ${d.android}${d.lastSeen != null ? ' · vu ${fmtAgo(d.lastSeen!)}' : ''}', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12.5)),
          ],
          if (c.events.isNotEmpty) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            const Text('Connexions récentes', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 13)),
            for (final e in c.events.take(5))
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 3),
                child: Row(children: [
                  Icon(e.success ? Icons.check_circle_rounded : Icons.error_rounded, size: 15, color: e.success ? const Color(0xFF22C55E) : const Color(0xFFEF4444)),
                  const SizedBox(width: 6),
                  Expanded(child: Text('${e.at != null ? fmtDateTime(e.at!) : '—'} · ${e.platform}${e.success ? '' : ' · échec'}', style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12.5))),
                ]),
              ),
          ],
        ],
        const Divider(height: 24),
        Wrap(spacing: 8, runSpacing: 8, children: [
          OutlinedButton.icon(icon: const Icon(Icons.password_rounded), label: const Text('Mot de passe'), style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(minimumSize: const Size(0, 44)), onPressed: () => _changePassword(context)),
          OutlinedButton.icon(icon: const Icon(Icons.key_rounded), label: const Text('Codes de récupération'), style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(minimumSize: const Size(0, 44)), onPressed: () => _newCodes(context)),
          OutlinedButton.icon(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.logout_rounded),
            label: const Text('Déconnecter partout'),
            style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(minimumSize: const Size(0, 44)),
            onPressed: () async {
              try {
                await context.read<ApiClient>().logoutAll();
                if (context.mounted) await context.read<SessionController>().signOut();
              } catch (e) {
                if (context.mounted) showToast(context, errorMessage(e));
              }
            },
          ),
        ]),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================================ modifier le profil
class EditProfileScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const EditProfileScreen({super.key, required this.profile});
  final AccountProfile profile;
  @override
  State<EditProfileScreen> createState() => _EditProfileScreenState();
}

class _EditProfileScreenState extends State<EditProfileScreen> {
  final _form = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  late final _name = TextEditingController(text: widget.profile.displayName);
  late final _first = TextEditingController(text: widget.profile.firstName ?? '');
  late final _last = TextEditingController(text: widget.profile.lastName ?? '');
  late final _phone = TextEditingController(text: widget.profile.phone ?? '');
  late final _city = TextEditingController(text: widget.profile.city ?? '');
  late final _district = TextEditingController(text: widget.profile.district ?? '');
  late int? _year = widget.profile.birthYear;
  late bool _show = widget.profile.showInLeaderboard;
  bool _busy = false;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    for (final c in [_name, _first, _last, _phone, _city, _district]) {
      c.dispose();
    }
    super.dispose();
  }

  String? _n(TextEditingController c) => c.text.trim().isEmpty ? null : c.text.trim();

  Future<void> _save() async {
    if (!_form.currentState!.validate()) return;
    setState(() => _busy = true);
    try {
      await context.read<AccountController>().saveProfile({
        'display_name': _name.text.trim(),
        'first_name': _n(_first),
        'last_name': _n(_last),
        'phone': _n(_phone),
        'city': _n(_city),
        'district': _n(_district),
        if (_year != null) 'birth_year': _year,
        'show_in_leaderboard': _show,
      });
      if (mounted) {
        showToast(context, 'Profil enregistré.');
        Navigator.pop(context);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) showToast(context, errorMessage(e));
    }
    if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('Modifier le profil')),
      body: ListView(padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20), children: [
        Text('Seul votre pseudo est visible des autres. Le reste reste privé.', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant)),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        Form(
          key: _form,
          child: Column(children: [
            TextFormField(controller: _name, maxLength: 24, decoration: fieldDecoration(context, 'Pseudo public', Icons.badge_outlined), validator: (v) => RegExp(r"^[\w .'-]{3,24}$").hasMatch((v ?? '').trim()) ? null : '3 à 24 caractères'),
            Row(children: [
              Expanded(child: TextFormField(controller: _first, decoration: fieldDecoration(context, 'Prénom', Icons.person_outline_rounded))),
              const SizedBox(width: 10),
              Expanded(child: TextFormField(controller: _last, decoration: fieldDecoration(context, 'Nom', Icons.person_outline_rounded))),
            ]),
            const SizedBox(height: 14),
            TextFormField(controller: _phone, keyboardType: TextInputType.phone, decoration: fieldDecoration(context, 'Téléphone', Icons.phone_outlined), validator: (v) => (v == null || v.trim().isEmpty || RegExp(r'^\+?[0-9 ()-]{6,20}$').hasMatch(v.trim())) ? null : 'Numéro invalide'),
            const SizedBox(height: 14),
            Row(children: [
              Expanded(child: TextFormField(controller: _city, decoration: fieldDecoration(context, 'Ville', Icons.location_city_rounded))),
              const SizedBox(width: 10),
              Expanded(child: TextFormField(controller: _district, decoration: fieldDecoration(context, 'Quartier', Icons.holiday_village_outlined))),
            ]),
            const SizedBox(height: 14),
            DropdownButtonFormField<int>(
              initialValue: _year,
              decoration: fieldDecoration(context, 'Année de naissance', Icons.cake_outlined),
              items: [for (var y = DateTime.now().year - 13; y >= 1930; y--) DropdownMenuItem(value: y, child: Text('$y'))],
              onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _year = v),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            SwitchListTile(contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero, title: const Text('Apparaître dans le classement'), subtitle: const Text('Désactivé : vous gagnez quand même points et niveaux.'), value: _show, onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _show = v)),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            FilledButton(onPressed: _busy ? null : _save, child: _busy ? const SizedBox(height: 22, width: 22, child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2.5)) : const Text('Enregistrer')),
          ]),
        ),
      ]),
    );
  }
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: ui/proposals_screen.dart
################################################################################

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

import '../core/models.dart';
import '../core/util.dart';
import '../state/proposals_controller.dart';
import '../state/session_controller.dart';
import 'record_screen.dart';
import 'widgets.dart';

class ProposalsScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const ProposalsScreen({super.key});
  @override
  State<ProposalsScreen> createState() => _ProposalsScreenState();
}

class _ProposalsScreenState extends State<ProposalsScreen> {
  Timer? _tick;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) => context.read<ProposalsController>().refresh());
    _tick = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 1), (_) {
      if (mounted) setState(() {});
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _tick?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _respond(ProposalOut p, bool accept) async {
    try {
      await context.read<ProposalsController>().respond(p.id, accept);
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) showToast(context, errorMessage(e));
    }
  }

  String _remaining(DateTime t) {
    final d = t.difference(DateTime.now());
    if (d.isNegative) return 'expirée';
    return '${d.inMinutes}:${(d.inSeconds % 60).toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final pc = context.watch<ProposalsController>();
    final s = context.watch<SessionController>();
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final u = s.user!;

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('Propositions')),
      body: RefreshIndicator(
        onRefresh: pc.refresh,
        child: ListView(padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 4, 16, 40), children: [
          if (!u.hybridCollectionEnabled)
            SoftCard(
              child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
                const Text('Collecte hybride désactivée', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
                const SizedBox(height: 6),
                Text('Activez-la pour recevoir des propositions de zones à mesurer.',
                    style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant)),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                FilledButton(
                    onPressed: () async {
                      try {
                        await s.setHybrid(true);
                      } catch (e) {
                        if (context.mounted) showToast(context, errorMessage(e));
                      }
                    },
                    child: const Text('Activer')),
              ]),
            )
          else
            SoftCard(
              child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
                const Text('Demander une proposition', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text('Selon votre position, votre planning et votre batterie.',
                    style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant)),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                Row(children: [
                  Expanded(
                      child: OutlinedButton(
                          onPressed: pc.loading ? null : () => pc.requestNow('contextual'),
                          child: const Text('Ici et maintenant'))),
                  const SizedBox(width: 10),
                  Expanded(
                      child: OutlinedButton(
                          onPressed: pc.loading ? null : () => pc.requestNow('scheduled'),
                          child: const Text('Créneau planifié'))),
                ]),
                if (pc.lastReason != null)
                  Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 12),
                    child: Text(proposalReasonLabel(pc.lastReason!),
                        style: TextStyle(color: cs.primary, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
                  ),
                if (pc.error != null)
                  Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 12),
                    child: Text(apiErrorLabel(pc.error!), style: TextStyle(color: cs.error)),
                  ),
              ]),
            ),
          const SectionTitle('En cours'),
          if (pc.loading) const Center(child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(20), child: CircularProgressIndicator())),
          if (!pc.loading && pc.list.isEmpty)
            SoftCard(
                child: Text('Aucune proposition ouverte.', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant))),
          for (final p in pc.list)
            SoftCard(
              margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
              child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
                Row(children: [
                  Icon(p.trigger == 'scheduled' ? Icons.schedule_rounded : Icons.near_me_rounded, color: cs.primary),
                  const SizedBox(width: 10),
                  Expanded(
                      child: Text(p.trigger == 'scheduled' ? 'Créneau planifié' : 'Zone proche de vous',
                          style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800))),
                  StatusChip(p.status == 'accepted' ? 'Acceptée' : 'À répondre',
                      p.status == 'accepted' ? const Color(0xFF22C55E) : cs.primary),
                ]),
                const SizedBox(height: 10),
                Text('Priorité ${p.priority.toStringAsFixed(0)}/100 · durée ${p.sessionSeconds} s · expire dans ${_remaining(p.expiresAt)}',
                    style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant)),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                if (p.status == 'pending')
                  Row(children: [
                    Expanded(child: OutlinedButton(onPressed: () => _respond(p, false), child: const Text('Refuser'))),
                    const SizedBox(width: 10),
                    Expanded(child: FilledButton(onPressed: () => _respond(p, true), child: const Text('Accepter'))),
                  ])
                else
                  FilledButton.icon(
                    icon: const Icon(Icons.play_arrow_rounded),
                    label: const Text('Démarrer la mesure'),
                    onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context)
                        .push(MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => RecordScreen(proposal: p)))
                        .then((_) => pc.refresh()),
                  ),
              ]),
            ),
        ]),
      ),
    );
  }
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: ui/record_screen.dart
################################################################################

import 'dart:math';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

import '../core/config.dart';
import '../core/models.dart';
import '../core/util.dart';
import '../state/proposals_controller.dart';
import '../state/recorder_controller.dart';
import 'widgets.dart';

class RecordScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const RecordScreen({super.key, this.proposal});
  final ProposalOut? proposal;
  @override
  State<RecordScreen> createState() => _RecordScreenState();
}

class _RecordScreenState extends State<RecordScreen> {
  late final RecorderController _rec;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _rec = context.read<RecorderController>();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      _rec.resetForNew();
      _rec.attachProposal(widget.proposal);
      _rec.startGpsWatch();
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _rec.stopGpsWatch();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<bool> _confirmCancel() async {
    final ok = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (_) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('Annuler la mesure ?'),
        content: const Text('La mesure en cours sera perdue.'),
        actions: [
          TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, false), child: const Text('Continuer')),
          FilledButton(
              style: FilledButton.styleFrom(minimumSize: const Size(100, 44)),
              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, true),
              child: const Text('Annuler la mesure')),
        ],
      ),
    );
    if (ok == true) await _rec.cancel();
    return ok == true;
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final r = context.watch<RecorderController>();
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final recording = r.phase == RecPhase.recording;

    return PopScope(
      canPop: !r.busy,
      onPopInvokedWithResult: (didPop, _) async {
        if (didPop) return;
        if (r.phase == RecPhase.recording || r.phase == RecPhase.preparing) {
          if (await _confirmCancel() && mounted) Navigator.of(context).pop();
        }
      },
      child: Scaffold(
        appBar: AppBar(title: Text(widget.proposal != null ? 'Mesure proposée' : 'Nouvelle mesure')),
        body: SafeArea(
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 0, 20, 20),
            child: Column(children: [
              _GpsBadge(r.gps),
              if (widget.proposal != null)
                Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 10),
                  child: SoftCard(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                    child: Row(children: [
                      Icon(Icons.flag_rounded, color: cs.primary),
                      const SizedBox(width: 10),
                      Expanded(
                          child: Text(
                              'Zone prioritaire (${widget.proposal!.priority.toStringAsFixed(0)}/100) · durée ${widget.proposal!.sessionSeconds} s',
                              style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w600))),
                    ]),
                  ),
                ),
              Expanded(
                child: Center(
                  child: AspectRatio(
                    aspectRatio: 1,
                    child: TweenAnimationBuilder<double>(
                      tween: Tween(end: r.phase == RecPhase.done ? r.result!.laeq : r.liveDb),
                      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 160),
                      builder: (_, db, __) => CustomPaint(
                        painter: _GaugePainter(db: db, active: recording, track: cs.outlineVariant),
                        child: Center(child: _gaugeCenter(context, r, db)),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              SizedBox(height: 56, child: CustomPaint(size: const Size(double.infinity, 56), painter: _WavePainter(r.wave))),
              const SizedBox(height: 14),
              if (recording)
                ClipRRect(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                  child: LinearProgressIndicator(
                      minHeight: 8, value: r.total == 0 ? 0 : r.elapsed / r.total),
                ),
              if (recording)
                Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8),
                  child: Text('${r.elapsed} / ${r.total} s · LAeq ${r.leqDb.toStringAsFixed(1)} · Lmax ${r.maxDb.toStringAsFixed(1)}',
                      style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant)),
                ),
              if (r.phase == RecPhase.idle || r.phase == RecPhase.failed || r.phase == RecPhase.preparing) ...[
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Align(
                    alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
                    child: Text('Contexte sonore (facultatif)',
                        style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600))),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                SizedBox(
                  height: 42,
                  child: ListView(scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal, children: [
                    for (final t in NoiseTag.values)
                      Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 8),
                        child: ChoiceChip(
                          avatar: Icon(t.icon, size: 18),
                          label: Text(t.label),
                          selected: r.tag == t,
                          showCheckmark: false,
                          onSelected: (s) => r.setTag(s ? t : null),
                        ),
                      ),
                  ]),
                ),
              ],
              if (r.phase == RecPhase.failed)
                Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 10),
                  child: Text(apiErrorLabel(r.errorCode ?? 'capture_error'),
                      textAlign: TextAlign.center, style: TextStyle(color: cs.error)),
                ),
              if (r.phase == RecPhase.done) _ResultCard(r),
              const SizedBox(height: 14),
              _mainButton(context, r),
            ]),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _gaugeCenter(BuildContext context, RecorderController r, double db) {
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    switch (r.phase) {
      case RecPhase.preparing:
        return const CircularProgressIndicator();
      case RecPhase.sending:
        return const Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
          CircularProgressIndicator(),
          SizedBox(height: 10),
          Text('Envoi…'),
        ]);
      case RecPhase.idle:
      case RecPhase.failed:
        return Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
          Icon(Icons.mic_rounded, size: 52, color: cs.primary),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Text('${r.total} secondes', style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 18)),
          Text('Restez immobile', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant)),
        ]);
      default:
        final col = noiseColor(db);
        return Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
          Text(db.toStringAsFixed(0),
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 76, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: col, height: 1)),
          const Text('dB(A)', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
          const SizedBox(height: 6),
          StatusChip(noiseLabel(db), col),
        ]);
    }
  }

  Widget _mainButton(BuildContext context, RecorderController r) {
    switch (r.phase) {
      case RecPhase.recording:
        return OutlinedButton.icon(
            onPressed: _confirmCancel, icon: const Icon(Icons.stop_rounded), label: const Text('Annuler'));
      case RecPhase.preparing:
      case RecPhase.sending:
        return const FilledButton(onPressed: null, child: Text('Veuillez patienter…'));
      case RecPhase.done:
        return Row(children: [
          Expanded(
              child: OutlinedButton(
                  onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(), child: const Text('Fermer'))),
          const SizedBox(width: 12),
          Expanded(
              child: FilledButton(
                  onPressed: () {
                    r.resetForNew();
                    r.attachProposal(null);
                  },
                  child: const Text('Nouvelle mesure'))),
        ]);
      default:
        return FilledButton.icon(
          onPressed: () async {
            await r.start();
            if (mounted) context.read<ProposalsController>().refresh();
          },
          icon: const Icon(Icons.play_arrow_rounded),
          label: Text(r.phase == RecPhase.failed ? 'Réessayer' : 'Démarrer la mesure'),
        );
    }
  }
}

class _GpsBadge extends StatelessWidget {
  const _GpsBadge(this.pos);
  final dynamic pos;
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final acc = pos?.accuracy as double?;
    final color = acc == null
        ? const Color(0xFF94A3B8)
        : acc <= 20
            ? const Color(0xFF22C55E)
            : acc <= 100
                ? const Color(0xFFF59E0B)
                : const Color(0xFFEF4444);
    return Align(
      alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
      child: StatusChip(acc == null ? 'GPS : recherche…' : 'GPS ± ${acc.toStringAsFixed(0)} m', color,
          icon: Icons.gps_fixed_rounded),
    );
  }
}

class _ResultCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _ResultCard(this.r);
  final RecorderController r;
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final res = r.result!;
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final q = r.qualityStatus;
    return SoftCard(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 10),
      child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
        Row(children: [
          Icon(res.sent ? Icons.cloud_done_rounded : Icons.cloud_queue_rounded,
              color: res.sent ? const Color(0xFF22C55E) : const Color(0xFFF59E0B)),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          Expanded(
              child: Text(res.sent ? 'Mesure enregistrée et envoyée' : 'Enregistrée · envoi dès le retour du réseau',
                  style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800))),
        ]),
        const SizedBox(height: 10),
        Text('LAeq ${res.laeq.toStringAsFixed(1)} · Lmax ${res.lmax.toStringAsFixed(1)} · Lmin ${res.lmin.toStringAsFixed(1)} dB(A)'),
        const SizedBox(height: 10),
        Row(children: [
          if (res.tag != null) ...[NoiseTagChip(res.tag!), const SizedBox(width: 8)],
          if (!res.sent)
            const StatusChip('En attente', Color(0xFFF59E0B))
          else if (q == null)
            Row(children: [
              const SizedBox(width: 14, height: 14, child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2)),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Text('Évaluation en cours…', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant)),
            ])
          else ...[
            StatusChip(qualityLabel(q), qualityColor(q)),
            if (r.qualityScore != null) ...[
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Text('Score ${(r.qualityScore! * 100).round()} %', style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
            ],
          ],
        ]),
        if (q != null && r.reasons.isNotEmpty) ...[
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          for (final code in r.reasons.where((c) => c != 'insufficient_neighbors'))
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 2),
              child: Text('• ${reasonLabel(code)}', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 13)),
            ),
        ],
      ]),
    );
  }
}

class _GaugePainter extends CustomPainter {
  _GaugePainter({required this.db, required this.active, required this.track});
  final double db;
  final bool active;
  final Color track;
  static const int ticks = 54;
  static const double minDb = 20, maxDb = 110;

  @override
  void paint(Canvas c, Size s) {
    final ctr = s.center(Offset.zero);
    final r = s.width / 2 - 6;
    final level = ((db - minDb) / (maxDb - minDb)).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
    final startAngle = 3 * pi / 4; // 135°
    const sweep = 3 * pi / 2; // 270°
    final p = Paint()
      ..strokeWidth = 7
      ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.round;
    for (var i = 0; i < ticks; i++) {
      final f = i / (ticks - 1);
      final a = startAngle + sweep * f;
      final inner = Offset(ctr.dx + cos(a) * (r - 18), ctr.dy + sin(a) * (r - 18));
      final outer = Offset(ctr.dx + cos(a) * r, ctr.dy + sin(a) * r);
      final on = f <= level && (active || db > 0);
      p.color = on ? noiseColor(minDb + f * (maxDb - minDb)) : track.withValues(alpha: 0.35);
      c.drawLine(inner, outer, p);
    }
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(_GaugePainter o) => o.db != db || o.active != active;
}

class _WavePainter extends CustomPainter {
  _WavePainter(this.values);
  final List<double> values;
  @override
  void paint(Canvas c, Size s) {
    if (values.isEmpty) return;
    const n = 48;
    final w = s.width / n;
    final p = Paint()
      ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.round
      ..strokeWidth = max(2, w * 0.5);
    for (var i = 0; i < values.length; i++) {
      final v = values[i];
      final h = (((v - 20) / 90).clamp(0.05, 1.0)) * s.height;
      final x = (n - values.length + i) * w + w / 2;
      p.color = noiseColor(v);
      c.drawLine(Offset(x, s.height / 2 - h / 2), Offset(x, s.height / 2 + h / 2), p);
    }
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(_WavePainter o) => true;
}

# FICHIER: ui/reviews_screen.dart
################################################################################

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

import '../core/models_social.dart';
import '../core/util.dart';
import '../state/reviews_controller.dart';
import 'widgets.dart';

class ReviewsScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const ReviewsScreen({super.key});
  @override
  State<ReviewsScreen> createState() => _ReviewsScreenState();
}

class _ReviewsScreenState extends State<ReviewsScreen> {
  bool _busy = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      context.read<ReviewsController>().loadHistory();
    });
  }

  Future<void> _vote(ReviewRequest r, String vote, {bool strong = false}) async {
    setState(() => _busy = true);
    try {
      await context.read<ReviewsController>().vote(r.id, vote, strong);
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) showToast(context, errorMessage(e));
    }
    if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final rev = context.watch<ReviewsController>();
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('Mes avis')),
      body: RefreshIndicator(
        onRefresh: () async {
          await rev.refresh();
          await rev.loadHistory();
        },
        child: ListView(padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 4, 16, 40), children: [
          if (rev.pending.isNotEmpty) ...[
            SectionTitle('En attente (${rev.pending.length})'),
            for (final r in rev.pending) _pendingCard(r, cs),
          ] else
            SoftCard(child: Text('Aucune demande d\'avis en ce moment.', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant))),
          const SectionTitle('Historique'),
          if (rev.history.isEmpty)
            SoftCard(child: Text('Aucun avis donné pour l\'instant.', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant))),
          for (final h in rev.history) _historyTile(h, cs),
        ]),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _pendingCard(ReviewRequest r, ColorScheme cs) {
    return SoftCard(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
      child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
        Row(children: [
          const Icon(Icons.hearing_rounded, color: Color(0xFF7C3AED)),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          Expanded(child: Text('Ce niveau vous semble-t-il juste ?', style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800))),
        ]),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        Text('${r.levelDb.toStringAsFixed(0)} dB(A) · à ${r.distanceM.round()} m · ${fmtAgo(r.measuredAt)}',
            style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 13)),
        Text('Expire dans ${fmtTime(r.expiresAt)}', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12)),
        const SizedBox(height: 10),
        Row(children: [
          Expanded(child: OutlinedButton(onPressed: _busy ? null : () => _vote(r, 'lower', strong: true), child: const Text('Plus faible'))),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          Expanded(child: FilledButton(onPressed: _busy ? null : () => _vote(r, 'confirm'), child: const Text('Oui'))),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          Expanded(child: OutlinedButton(onPressed: _busy ? null : () => _vote(r, 'higher', strong: true), child: const Text('Plus fort'))),
        ]),
        Align(
          alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
          child: TextButton(
            onPressed: _busy ? null : () => _vote(r, 'confirm', strong: true),
            child: const Text('Écart important'),
          ),
        ),
      ]),
    );
  }

  Widget _historyTile(ReviewHistoryItem h, ColorScheme cs) {
    final ok = h.matched == true;
    return SoftCard(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      child: Row(children: [
        Icon(
          h.matched == null ? Icons.hourglass_empty_rounded : (ok ? Icons.check_circle_rounded : Icons.cancel_rounded),
          color: h.matched == null ? cs.onSurfaceVariant : (ok ? const Color(0xFF22C55E) : const Color(0xFFEF4444)),
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 12),
        Expanded(child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
          Text('${h.levelDb.toStringAsFixed(0)} dB(A) · ${h.vote ?? '—'}', style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
          Text('${h.votedAt != null ? fmtAgo(h.votedAt!) : ''} · statut : ${h.status}', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12)),
        ])),
        StatusChip(
          h.outcome ?? 'en attente',
          h.matched == null ? cs.primary : (ok ? const Color(0xFF22C55E) : const Color(0xFFEF4444)),
        ),
      ]),
    );
  }
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: ui/road_sheet.dart
################################################################################

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

import '../core/api.dart';
import '../core/api_ext.dart';
import '../core/models.dart';
import '../core/models_roads.dart';
import '../core/util.dart';
import 'widgets.dart';

class RoadSheet extends StatefulWidget {
  const RoadSheet({super.key, required this.seg, required this.filters, this.state});
  final RoadSeg seg;
  final RoadState? state;
  final MapFilters filters;
  @override
  State<RoadSheet> createState() => _RoadSheetState();
}

class _RoadSheetState extends State<RoadSheet> {
  late final Future<RoadDetail> _f = context.read<ApiClient>().roadSegment(widget.seg.id, widget.filters);

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final s = widget.seg;
    return SafeArea(
      child: ListView(shrinkWrap: true, padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 0, 20, 24), children: [
        Row(children: [
          Icon(Icons.add_road_rounded, color: cs.primary, size: 30),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          Expanded(child: Text(s.title, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 19))),
        ]),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        Wrap(spacing: 8, runSpacing: 6, children: [
          StatusChip(roadClassLabel(s.highway), cs.primary),
          if (s.ref != null) StatusChip(s.ref!, cs.onSurfaceVariant),
          if (s.maxspeed != null) StatusChip('${s.maxspeed} km/h', cs.onSurfaceVariant),
          if (s.oneway) StatusChip('Sens unique', cs.onSurfaceVariant),
          StatusChip('${s.lengthM.round()} m', cs.onSurfaceVariant),
        ]),
        FutureBuilder<RoadDetail>(
          future: _f,
          builder: (context, snap) {
            if (snap.connectionState != ConnectionState.done) {
              return const Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(28), child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()));
            }
            if (snap.hasError) {
              return Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 20), child: Text(errorMessage(snap.error!), style: TextStyle(color: cs.error)));
            }
            final d = snap.data!;
            final st = d.state;
            final col = st.hasData ? noiseColor(st.laeq) : const Color(0xFF22C55E);
            return Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              if (st.hasData) ...[
                Row(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end, children: [
                  Text(st.laeq.toStringAsFixed(1), style: TextStyle(fontSize: 48, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: col, height: 1)),
                  const Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.only(left: 6, bottom: 6), child: Text('dB(A)', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700))),
                  const Spacer(),
                  StatusChip(noiseLabel(st.laeq), col),
                ]),
                const SizedBox(height: 10),
                Wrap(spacing: 8, runSpacing: 6, children: [
                  StatusChip('${st.count} mesure(s)', cs.primary, icon: Icons.straighten_rounded),
                  StatusChip('${st.devices} contributeur(s)', cs.primary, icon: Icons.people_alt_rounded),
                  StatusChip('Confiance ${(st.confidence * 100).round()} %', const Color(0xFF22C55E), icon: Icons.verified_rounded),
                  if (st.lmax != null) StatusChip('Lmax ${st.lmax!.toStringAsFixed(0)} dB', cs.onSurfaceVariant),
                ]),
                if (st.last != null)
                  Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8), child: Text('Dernière mesure : ${fmtAgo(st.last!)}', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 13))),
              ] else
                SoftCard(
                  child: Row(children: [
                    const Icon(Icons.eco_rounded, color: Color(0xFF22C55E), size: 30),
                    const SizedBox(width: 12),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(
                          'Pas encore mesurée (ou pas assez de contributeurs). Elle est affichée en vert, le niveau de base : cela ne veut pas dire qu\'elle est silencieuse. Une mission « route » peut vous la proposer.',
                          style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 13, height: 1.35)),
                    ),
                  ]),
                ),
              if (d.byPart.isNotEmpty) ...[
                const SectionTitle('Par plage horaire'),
                for (final p in d.byPart)
                  Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4),
                    child: Row(children: [
                      SizedBox(width: 90, child: Text(dayPartLabel(p.part))),
                      Expanded(
                        child: ClipRRect(
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(5),
                          child: LinearProgressIndicator(minHeight: 10, value: (p.laeq / 100).clamp(0.05, 1.0), color: noiseColor(p.laeq), backgroundColor: cs.outlineVariant.withValues(alpha: 0.3)),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 10),
                      Text('${p.laeq.round()} dB · ${p.count}', style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 12)),
                    ]),
                  ),
              ],
              if (d.connected.isNotEmpty) ...[
                const SectionTitle('Routes connectées'),
                Wrap(spacing: 6, runSpacing: 6, children: [
                  for (final c in d.connected.map((e) => e.name).toSet().take(8)) StatusChip(c, cs.onSurfaceVariant),
                ]),
              ],
            ]);
          },
        ),
      ]),
    );
  }
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: ui/schedule_screen.dart
################################################################################

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

import '../core/api.dart';
import '../core/util.dart';
import '../state/proposals_controller.dart';
import 'widgets.dart';

class ScheduleScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const ScheduleScreen({super.key});
  @override
  State<ScheduleScreen> createState() => _ScheduleScreenState();
}

class _ScheduleScreenState extends State<ScheduleScreen> {
  static const _days = ['Lun', 'Mar', 'Mer', 'Jeu', 'Ven', 'Sam', 'Dim'];
  bool _loading = true, _saving = false, _active = true;
  String _mode = 'both';
  Set<int> _sel = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6};
  TimeOfDay _start = const TimeOfDay(hour: 8, minute: 0);
  TimeOfDay _end = const TimeOfDay(hour: 20, minute: 0);
  final List<TimeOfDay> _pref = [];

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _load();
  }

  TimeOfDay _parse(String s) {
    final p = s.split(':');
    return TimeOfDay(hour: int.parse(p[0]), minute: int.parse(p[1]));
  }

  String _fmt(TimeOfDay t) =>
      '${t.hour.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:${t.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:00';
  String _show(TimeOfDay t) =>
      '${t.hour.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:${t.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';

  Future<void> _load() async {
    try {
      final s = await context.read<ApiClient>().getSchedule();
      if (s != null) {
        _active = s.active;
        _mode = s.mode;
        _sel = s.days.toSet();
        _start = _parse(s.start);
        _end = _parse(s.end);
        _pref
          ..clear()
          ..addAll(s.preferred.map(_parse));
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) showToast(context, errorMessage(e));
    }
    if (mounted) setState(() => _loading = false);
  }

  Future<void> _save() async {
    if (_sel.isEmpty) {
      showToast(context, 'Choisissez au moins un jour.');
      return;
    }
    setState(() => _saving = true);
    try {
      await context.read<ApiClient>().putSchedule({
        'days_of_week': _sel.toList()..sort(),
        'window_start': _fmt(_start),
        'window_end': _fmt(_end),
        'preferred_times': [for (final t in _pref) _fmt(t)],
        'mode': _mode,
        'active': _active,
      });
      if (mounted) {
        context.read<ProposalsController>().scheduleChanged();
        showToast(context, 'Planning enregistré.');
        Navigator.pop(context);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) showToast(context, errorMessage(e));
    }
    if (mounted) setState(() => _saving = false);
  }

  Future<TimeOfDay?> _pick(TimeOfDay initial) =>
      showTimePicker(context: context, initialTime: initial, builder: (c, w) => MediaQuery(
          data: MediaQuery.of(c).copyWith(alwaysUse24HourFormat: true), child: w!));

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('Mon planning')),
      body: _loading
          ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
          : ListView(padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 4, 16, 40), children: [
              SoftCard(
                child: SwitchListTile(
                  contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                  title: const Text('Planning actif', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
                  value: _active,
                  onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _active = v),
                ),
              ),
              const SectionTitle('Mode'),
              SegmentedButton<String>(
                segments: const [
                  ButtonSegment(value: 'scheduled', label: Text('Planifié')),
                  ButtonSegment(value: 'contextual', label: Text('Contextuel')),
                  ButtonSegment(value: 'both', label: Text('Les deux')),
                ],
                selected: {_mode},
                onSelectionChanged: (v) => setState(() => _mode = v.first),
                showSelectedIcon: false,
              ),
              const SectionTitle('Jours'),
              Wrap(spacing: 8, runSpacing: 8, children: [
                for (var i = 0; i < 7; i++)
                  FilterChip(
                    label: Text(_days[i]),
                    selected: _sel.contains(i),
                    showCheckmark: false,
                    onSelected: (v) => setState(() => v ? _sel.add(i) : _sel.remove(i)),
                  ),
              ]),
              const SectionTitle('Fenêtre horaire'),
              Row(children: [
                Expanded(
                    child: OutlinedButton.icon(
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.wb_twilight_rounded),
                        label: Text('Début ${_show(_start)}'),
                        onPressed: () async {
                          final t = await _pick(_start);
                          if (t != null) setState(() => _start = t);
                        })),
                const SizedBox(width: 10),
                Expanded(
                    child: OutlinedButton.icon(
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.nights_stay_outlined),
                        label: Text('Fin ${_show(_end)}'),
                        onPressed: () async {
                          final t = await _pick(_end);
                          if (t != null) setState(() => _end = t);
                        })),
              ]),
              SectionTitle(
                'Horaires préférés (${_pref.length}/12)',
                trailing: TextButton.icon(
                  onPressed: _pref.length >= 12
                      ? null
                      : () async {
                          final t = await _pick(const TimeOfDay(hour: 12, minute: 0));
                          if (t != null && !_pref.contains(t)) setState(() => _pref.add(t));
                        },
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.add_rounded),
                  label: const Text('Ajouter'),
                ),
              ),
              if (_pref.isEmpty)
                Text('Utilisés par le mode « planifié ».', style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant))
              else
                Wrap(spacing: 8, runSpacing: 8, children: [
                  for (final t in _pref)
                    InputChip(
                      label: Text(_show(t)),
                      onDeleted: () => setState(() => _pref.remove(t)),
                    ),
                ]),
              const SizedBox(height: 28),
              FilledButton(
                onPressed: _saving ? null : _save,
                child: _saving
                    ? const SizedBox(height: 22, width: 22, child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2.5))
                    : const Text('Enregistrer'),
              ),
            ]),
    );
  }
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: ui/search_sheet.dart
################################################################################

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:latlong2/latlong.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

import '../core/api.dart';
import '../core/models.dart';
import '../core/theme.dart';
import '../core/util.dart';

/// Retourne (lieu, démarrerItinéraire) ou null.
class SearchSheet extends StatefulWidget {
  const SearchSheet({super.key, required this.near});
  final LatLng near;
  @override
  State<SearchSheet> createState() => _SearchSheetState();
}

class _SearchSheetState extends State<SearchSheet> {
  final _ctl = TextEditingController();
  Timer? _debounce;
  List<PlaceResult> _results = [];
  bool _loading = false;
  String? _error;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _debounce?.cancel();
    _ctl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _onChanged(String v) {
    _debounce?.cancel();
    if (v.trim().length < 3) {
      setState(() => _results = []);
      return;
    }
    _debounce = Timer(const Duration(milliseconds: 450), () => _search(v.trim()));
  }

  Future<void> _search(String q) async {
    setState(() {
      _loading = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      final r = await context
          .read<ApiClient>()
          .searchPlaces(q, lat: widget.near.latitude, lon: widget.near.longitude);
      if (mounted) setState(() => _results = r);
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _error = errorMessage(e));
    }
    if (mounted) setState(() => _loading = false);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    return Padding(
      padding: EdgeInsets.only(bottom: MediaQuery.of(context).viewInsets.bottom),
      child: SafeArea(
        child: ConstrainedBox(
          constraints: BoxConstraints(maxHeight: MediaQuery.of(context).size.height * 0.75),
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 0, 16, 16),
            child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
              TextField(
                controller: _ctl,
                autofocus: true,
                onChanged: _onChanged,
                textInputAction: TextInputAction.search,
                decoration: fieldDecoration(context, 'Rechercher un lieu, une adresse', Icons.search_rounded),
              ),
              if (_loading) const Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: 8), child: LinearProgressIndicator(minHeight: 3)),
              if (_error != null)
                Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8),
                    child: Text(_error!, style: TextStyle(color: cs.error))),
              Flexible(
                child: ListView(shrinkWrap: true, children: [
                  for (final p in _results)
                    ListTile(
                      contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                      leading: Icon(Icons.place_outlined, color: cs.primary),
                      title: Text(p.name, maxLines: 1, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
                      subtitle: p.address == null ? null : Text(p.address!, maxLines: 2, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
                      trailing: IconButton(
                        tooltip: 'Itinéraire',
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.directions_rounded),
                        onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, (p, true)),
                      ),
                      onTap: () => Navigator.pop(context, (p, false)),
                    ),
                ]),
              ),
            ]),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: ui/settings_screen.dart
################################################################################

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

import '../core/api.dart';
import '../core/models.dart';
import '../core/theme.dart'; 
import '../core/util.dart';
import '../state/session_controller.dart';
import 'schedule_screen.dart';
import 'widgets.dart';

class SettingsScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const SettingsScreen({super.key});
  @override
  State<SettingsScreen> createState() => _SettingsScreenState();
}

class _SettingsScreenState extends State<SettingsScreen> {
  late Future<List<DeviceOut>> _devices;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _devices = context.read<ApiClient>().myDevices();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _run(Future<void> Function() f) async {
    try {
      await f();
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) showToast(context, errorMessage(e));
    }
  }

  Future<void> _logout() async {
    final s = context.read<SessionController>();
    await s.signOut();
    if (mounted) Navigator.of(context).popUntil((r) => r.isFirst);
  }

  Future<void> _delete() async {
    final ctl = TextEditingController();
    final ok = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (_) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('Supprimer mon compte'),
        content: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
          const Text("Action irréversible. Entrez votre mot de passe pour confirmer."),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          TextField(
            controller: ctl,
            obscureText: true,
            decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Mot de passe'),
          ),
        ]),
        actions: [
          TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, false), child: const Text('Annuler')),
          FilledButton(
            style: FilledButton.styleFrom(minimumSize: const Size(100, 44), backgroundColor: Colors.red),
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, true),
            child: const Text('Supprimer'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
    final password = ctl.text;
    ctl.dispose();
    if (ok != true || !mounted) return;
    final api = context.read<ApiClient>();
    final s = context.read<SessionController>();
    try {
      await api.deleteMe(password);
      await s.afterAccountDeleted();
      if (mounted) Navigator.of(context).popUntil((r) => r.isFirst);
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) showToast(context, errorMessage(e));
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final s = context.watch<SessionController>();
    final cs = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final u = s.user!;

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('Paramètres')),
      body: ListView(padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 4, 16, 40), children: [
        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- apparence
        const SectionTitle('Apparence'),
        SoftCard(
          child: SegmentedButton<ThemeMode>(
            segments: const [
              ButtonSegment(value: ThemeMode.system, icon: Icon(Icons.brightness_auto_rounded), label: Text('Auto')),
              ButtonSegment(value: ThemeMode.light, icon: Icon(Icons.light_mode_rounded), label: Text('Clair')),
              ButtonSegment(value: ThemeMode.dark, icon: Icon(Icons.dark_mode_rounded), label: Text('Sombre')),
            ],
            selected: {s.themeMode},
            onSelectionChanged: (v) => s.setThemeMode(v.first),
          ),
        ),

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- confidentialité
        const SectionTitle('Confidentialité et participation'),
        SoftCard(
          padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
          child: Column(children: [
            SwitchListTile(
              secondary: const Icon(Icons.place_rounded),
              title: const Text('Géolocalisation'),
              value: s.consentOf('geolocation'),
              onChanged: (v) => _run(() => s.setConsent('geolocation', v)),
            ),
            const Divider(height: 1),
            SwitchListTile(
              secondary: const Icon(Icons.mic_rounded),
              title: const Text('Microphone'),
              subtitle: const Text("Seuls des niveaux en dB(A) sont envoyés, jamais l'audio."),
              value: s.consentOf('microphone'),
              onChanged: (v) => _run(() => s.setConsent('microphone', v)),
            ),
            const Divider(height: 1),
            SwitchListTile(
              secondary: const Icon(Icons.pause_circle_outline_rounded),
              title: const Text('Mettre ma participation en pause'),
              value: u.participationPaused,
              onChanged: (v) => _run(() => s.setPaused(v)),
            ),
          ]),
        ),

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- collecte hybride
        const SectionTitle('Collecte hybride'),
        SoftCard(
          padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
          child: Column(children: [
            SwitchListTile(
              secondary: const Icon(Icons.auto_awesome_rounded),
              title: const Text('Propositions de mesure'),
              value: u.hybridCollectionEnabled,
              onChanged: (v) => _run(() => s.setHybrid(v)),
            ),
            const Divider(height: 1),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.event_available_rounded),
              title: const Text('Mon planning'),
              trailing: const Icon(Icons.chevron_right_rounded),
              onTap: () => Navigator.of(context)
                  .push(MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const ScheduleScreen())),
            ),
          ]),
        ),

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- appareils
        const SectionTitle('Mes appareils'),
        SoftCard(
          child: FutureBuilder<List<DeviceOut>>(
            future: _devices,
            builder: (context, snap) {
              if (snap.connectionState != ConnectionState.done) {
                return const Center(child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(12), child: CircularProgressIndicator()));
              }
              if (snap.hasError) {
                return Text(errorMessage(snap.error!), style: TextStyle(color: cs.error));
              }
              final list = snap.data!;
              if (list.isEmpty) {
                return Text("Aucun appareil enregistré. Il le sera à la première mesure.",
                    style: TextStyle(color: cs.onSurfaceVariant));
              }
              return Column(children: [
                for (final d in list)
                  ListTile(
                    contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                    leading: const Icon(Icons.smartphone_rounded),
                    title: Text(d.sampleRate != null ? 'Téléphone · ${d.sampleRate} Hz' : 'Téléphone'),
                    trailing: StatusChip(
                      d.calibrated ? 'Calibré' : 'Non calibré',
                      d.calibrated ? const Color(0xFF22C55E) : const Color(0xFFF59E0B),
                    ),
                  ),
              ]);
            },
          ),
        ),


        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- compte
        const SectionTitle('Compte'),
        SoftCard(
          padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
          child: Column(children: [
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.logout_rounded),
              title: const Text('Se déconnecter'),
              onTap: _logout,
            ),
            const Divider(height: 1),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.delete_forever_rounded, color: Colors.red),
              title: const Text('Supprimer mon compte', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.red)),
              onTap: _delete,
            ),
          ]),
        ),
      ]),
    );
  }
}
